=== forgelock/NOTES.md
     1	Controls: swipe the worker one cell to push crates; keyboard arrows/WASD also move.
     2	Undo: tap UNDO or press Z; RESET and R restart the current level instantly.
     3	Loop: align every colored core with its matching forge; conveyors, gates, and plates add hazards.

=== deadline-dungeon/NOTES.md
     1	Move with the left floating stick or WASD / arrow keys.
     2	ATTACK with the slash button, Space, Enter, or mouse click.
     3	DASH with the dash button or Shift; dash grants brief i-frames.
     4	Clear each room, collect the key detours, and reach the green gate.
     5	The daily seed is fixed for comparable runs; press P for a fresh practice seed.

=== kart-circuit-zero/NOTES.md
     1	Kart Circuit Zero
     2	Controls: drag left/right on the left half to steer; hold DRIFT on the right and release to boost.
     3	Keyboard: arrows/WASD steer, Space drifts, R restarts, 1–4 choose circuits, Enter restarts after a run.
     4	Loop: race 3 laps on one of four seeded circuits; beat the medal time and your saved ghost.
     5	Best times and best-run ghosts persist in localStorage on this device.

=== skyhammer/NOTES.md
     1	# Skyhammer
     2	Controls (touch): drag anywhere — the ship follows your finger 1:1 from where you grabbed; a second finger down = FOCUS (slower, tighter shot, hitbox shown); tap the BOMB button to clear the screen.
     3	Controls (keys): arrows/WASD move, Shift focus, Space/Z bomb, Enter restart. Fire is automatic.
     4	Loop: 3 seeded stages of wave grammar (aimed streams, fans, spirals, walls-with-gaps), each capped by a 3-phase boss with destructible pods. Near-misses graze and build the score multiplier (up to x8).
     5	Fail/Win: 3 lives, hit = life lost and bombs refill; clear stage 3 for the 1CC win screen. Score and best (localStorage) shown on the HUD.

=== backstreet-reckoning/NOTES.md
     1	# Backstreet Reckoning
     2	
     3	Controls (landscape): drag anywhere on the left half for a floating 8-way stick that walks across 3 depth lanes; PUNCH and JUMP buttons bottom-right. Keyboard: WASD/arrows to move, J punch, K/Space jump, Enter restart.
     4	Punch a downed foe to grab it, then swipe (or tap PUNCH) to throw; walk onto a pipe or crate and tap PUNCH to pick it up. Punch in mid-air for a knockdown kick, and smash bins/barrels for health.
     5	Loop: each seeded stage is 3 procedural street blocks plus a boss alley. The camera locks while a gang wave is on screen; clear it and "GO →" opens the next stretch. Attacks are lane-honest, so line up your depth lane before swinging.
     6	Fail/win: a KO costs one of 3 lives; beating the alley boss seeds the next, harder stage. Score and best score (localStorage key `br_best`) persist.
     7	Tech: plain canvas + vanilla JS, no build step, no network, no external assets; WebAudio synth only.

     1	# Vanguard Four
     2	
     3	Controls: drag anywhere on the left/open area = floating move stick; STRIKE button = attack (tap or hold to combo); SUPER button when the meter is full; tap a teammate portrait to take control of that hero.
     4	Keyboard: WASD/arrows move, J or Space strike, K super, 1-4 swap hero, M mute.
     5	Loop: clear wave rooms of 5 enemy types with 4 heroes (blade dash, gravity fist, arc lantern, storm chain); the 3 you aren't driving fight as bots and revive downed allies. A Warden boss guards every 5th room.
     6	Fail: all four heroes down ends the run; tap to redeploy instantly. Score = rooms cleared + kills, best persisted in localStorage.
     7	Tech: plain canvas + vanilla JS, no build step, no network, relative script paths only.

=== siegebreak/NOTES.md
     1	# Siegebreak
     2	LOOP: hold the rampart for 10 seeded nights — ladder rushes, grapple ropes, a ram at the gate, siege towers, shielded elites. Between nights spend valor on repairs/upgrades. Gate destroyed or all 3 wall segments breached = loss (nights survived = score, best saved locally).
     3	TOUCH: tap the wall to leap there and strike (overhead); swipe DOWN while tapping = kick (knocks ladders off, breaks elite shields); swipe LEFT/RIGHT = sweep. Tap a SPEAR/ARCHER/OIL chip then tap a wall to rally that squad there. Mash the gate when the ram arrives to pour oil. RALLY button fires the banner burst at 100%.
     4	KEYS: arrows/A-D move, SPACE overhead, UP/W sweep, DOWN/S kick, 1/2/3 pick a squad then Q/E/R send it to wall 1/2/3, ENTER banner burst / continue, R restart on the end screen.
     5	BUILD: no build step — open index.html (plain canvas + vanilla JS, portrait 390x700, WebAudio synth only, no network).

=== shellshock-pinball/NOTES.md
     1	# Shellshock Pinball
     2	Controls (touch): drag DOWN in the right plunger lane to set launch power, release to fire; tap/hold the LEFT or RIGHT half of the screen for that flipper (multi-touch); quick swipe = nudge (4 nudges in 2.6s = TILT, flippers die for that ball).
     3	Controls (keys): Shift/A/Z + Shift/D//  = flippers, hold Space/Enter to charge the plunger and release to launch, Q/E/W = nudge, N = new table, M = mute.
     4	Loop: each seed generates a table (pop bumpers, 2 drop-target banks, a spinner lane, a ramp overpass, a mode hole, an outlane kickback). Clear a full drop bank to light the mode hole; shoot the hole to start a 60s mission for jackpots, then repeat at a higher multiplier.
     5	Fail/win: 3 balls, then GAME OVER — PLAY THIS TABLE replays the same seed, NEW TABLE reseeds; best score is saved per seed in localStorage.

    45	  function mulberry32(seed) {
    46	    return function() {
    47	      let t = seed += 0x6D2B79F5;
    48	      t = Math.imul(t ^ t >>> 15, t | 1);
    49	      t ^= t + Math.imul(t ^ t >>> 7, t | 61);
    50	      return ((t ^ t >>> 14) >>> 0) / 4294967296;
    51	    };
    52	  }
    53	  function clamp(v, a, b) { return Math.max(a, Math.min(b, v)); }
    54	  function rand(a = 0, b = 1) { return a + state.rng() * (b - a); }
    55	  function roundedRect(c, x, y, w, h, r) {
    56	    const q = Math.min(r, w / 2, h / 2);
    57	    c.beginPath(); c.moveTo(x + q, y); c.arcTo(x + w, y, x + w, y + h, q);
    58	    c.arcTo(x + w, y + h, x, y + h, q); c.arcTo(x, y + h, x, y, q);
    59	    c.arcTo(x, y, x + w, y, q); c.closePath();
    60	  }
    61	  function circleRectHit(ball, rect) {
    62	    const x = clamp(ball.x, rect.x, rect.x + rect.w);
    63	    const y = clamp(ball.y, rect.y, rect.y + rect.h);
    64	    const dx = ball.x - x, dy = ball.y - y;
    65	    return dx * dx + dy * dy < ball.r * ball.r;
    66	  }
    67	  function rectsHit(a, b) {
    68	    return a.x < b.x + b.w && a.x + a.w > b.x && a.y < b.y + b.h && a.y + a.h > b.y;
    69	  }
    70	  function addScore(n) {
    71	    state.score += n;
    72	    if (state.score > state.best) { state.best = state.score; localStorage.setItem(BEST_KEY, state.best); }
    73	  }
    74	  function beep(freq = 280, duration = 0.045, type = 'sine') {
    75	    if (!audio) return;
    76	    try {
    77	      const now = audio.currentTime;
    78	      const o = audio.createOscillator(), g = audio.createGain();
    79	      o.type = type; o.frequency.setValueAtTime(freq, now);
    80	      g.gain.setValueAtTime(0.045, now); g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, now + duration);
    81	      o.connect(g).connect(audio.destination); o.start(now); o.stop(now + duration);
    82	    } catch (_) {}
    83	  }

    86	    if (audio && audio.state === 'suspended') audio.resume();

    89	  function buildLevel(level) {
    90	    state.seed = (0x9e3779b9 ^ Math.imul(level, 2654435761)) >>> 0;
    91	    state.rng = mulberry32(state.seed);
    92	    const cols = W < 360 ? 6 : 7;
    93	    const rows = 6 + Math.min(2, Math.floor((level - 1) / 3));
    94	    const gap = 5, side = 14, top = 106;
    95	    const bw = (W - side * 2 - gap * (cols - 1)) / cols, bh = 25;
    96	    const bricks = [];
    97	    for (let row = 0; row < rows; row++) {
    98	      for (let col = 0; col < cols; col++) {
    99	        const edge = col === 0 || col === cols - 1;
   100	        let present = true;
   101	        if (row > 1 && state.rng() < 0.13 + (level % 3) * 0.025) present = false;
   102	        if (row === rows - 1 && state.rng() < 0.18) present = false;
   103	        if (level % 5 === 0 && row === 0 && col > 0 && col < cols - 1) present = state.rng() > 0.18;
   104	        if (present) {
   105	          const hp = 1 + (level > 3 && state.rng() < 0.22 ? 1 : 0) + (level > 8 && state.rng() < 0.12 ? 1 : 0);
   106	          bricks.push({
   107	            row, col, x: side + col * (bw + gap), y: top + row * (bh + gap), w: bw, h: bh,

   109	            alive: true, support: false, hit: 0

   111	        }
   112	      }
   113	    }
   114	    state.bricks = bricks;

   126	  function newBall(attached = false, source = null) {

   130	      color: source ? source.color : '#f8ffff', trail: []

   133	  function launchBall(ball) {

   139	    state.armed = false; beep(420, 0.08, 'triangle');

   141	  function action() {
   142	    if (state.phase !== 'play') { restart(); return; }
   143	    wakeAudio();
   144	    const held = state.balls.find(b => b.stuck || !b.launched);
   145	    if (held) launchBall(held);

   147	  }

   149	  function destroyBrick(brick, source = 'ball') {
   150	    if (!brick || !brick.alive) return;
   151	    brick.alive = false; state.wallRemaining--;
   152	    const points = source === 'crush' ? 16 : 24;
   153	    state.combo = Math.min(99, state.combo + 1); state.comboTimer = 2.2;
   154	    addScore(points + state.combo * 3); state.shake = Math.min(12, state.shake + 2.5); state.flash = 0.05;
   155	    burst(brick.x + brick.w / 2, brick.y + brick.h / 2, brick.color, source === 'crush' ? 8 : 14);
   156	    beep(source === 'crush' ? 125 : 190 + state.combo * 8, 0.035, 'square');
   157	    if (state.rng() < 0.14) dropPowerup(brick.x + brick.w / 2, brick.y + brick.h / 2);
   158	    if (state.wallRemaining <= 0 && (!state.boss || state.boss.hp <= 0)) completeWall();
   159	    else collapseUnsupported(true);
   160	  }
   161	  function collapseUnsupported(makeDebris) {
   162	    const alive = state.bricks.filter(b => b.alive);
   163	    const supported = new Set();
   164	    const open = [];
   165	    for (const b of alive) if (b.row === 0 || b.col === 0 || b.col === Math.max(...state.bricks.map(x => x.col))) { supported.add(b); open.push(b); }
   166	    while (open.length) {
   167	      const b = open.pop();
   168	      for (const n of alive) {
   169	        if (supported.has(n)) continue;
   170	        if (Math.abs(n.row - b.row) + Math.abs(n.col - b.col) === 1) { supported.add(n); open.push(n); }
   171	      }
   172	    }
   173	    for (const b of alive) {
   174	      b.support = supported.has(b);
   175	      if (!b.support) {
   176	        b.alive = false; state.wallRemaining--;
   177	        if (makeDebris) {
   178	          state.debris.push({ x: b.x, y: b.y, w: b.w, h: b.h, vx: rand(-45, 45), vy: rand(15, 60),
   179	            angle: rand(-0.2, 0.2), spin: rand(-3, 3), color: b.color, mass: b.mass, life: 10 });
   180	          burst(b.x + b.w / 2, b.y + b.h / 2, b.color, 4);
   181	        }
   182	      }
   183	    }
   184	    if (state.wallRemaining <= 0 && (!state.boss || state.boss.hp <= 0) && state.phase === 'play') completeWall();
   185	  }

   187	  function dropPowerup(x, y) {
   188	    const types = ['multi', 'wreck', 'sticky', 'laser'];
   189	    state.powerups.push({ x, y, w: 30, h: 17, vy: 65, type: types[Math.floor(rand(0, types.length))], t: rand(0, TAU) });
   190	  }
   191	  function applyPowerup(type) {
   192	    state.shake = 7; beep(650, 0.11, 'sine');
   193	    if (type === 'multi') {
   194	      const base = state.balls.find(b => b.launched) || state.balls[0];
   195	      if (base) {
   196	        for (const sign of [-1, 1]) state.balls.push({ ...base, vx: base.vx * 0.68 + sign * 115, vy: base.vy * 0.94, trail: [], color: '#8bf6e7' });
   197	      }
   198	    } else if (type === 'wreck') {
   199	      state.balls.forEach(b => { b.heavy = true; b.r = Math.max(b.r, 10); b.color = '#ffca66'; });
   200	    } else if (type === 'sticky') state.paddle.sticky = 12;
   201	    else if (type === 'laser') state.paddle.laser = 12;
   202	    burst(state.paddle.x, state.paddle.y, powerColor(type), 22);
   203	  }
   204	  function powerColor(type) { return type === 'multi' ? '#64e6d4' : type === 'wreck' ? '#ffbd5c' : type === 'sticky' ? '#d593ff' : '#ff719d'; }
   205	
   206	  function fireLaser() {

   208	      { x: state.paddle.x + 21, y: state.paddle.y - 12, w: 4, h: 16, vy: -520 });

   210	  }
   211	  function completeWall() {
   212	    if (state.phase !== 'play') return;
   213	    state.phase = 'clear'; state.armed = true; state.shake = 12; burst(W / 2, H * 0.38, '#fff4a8', 55); beep(820, 0.18, 'triangle');
   214	    setTimeout(() => { if (state.phase === 'clear') { state.level++; state.phase = 'play'; buildLevel(state.level); } }, 900);
   215	  }
   216	  function loseBall(ball) {
   217	    const idx = state.balls.indexOf(ball); if (idx < 0) return;
   218	    state.balls.splice(idx, 1); burst(ball.x, H - 14, '#ff668c', 18); state.shake = 10; beep(90, 0.12, 'sawtooth');
   219	    if (state.balls.length === 0) {
   220	      state.lives--;
   221	      if (state.lives <= 0) { state.phase = 'over'; state.armed = false; return; }
   222	      state.balls = [newBall(true)]; state.armed = true; state.paddle.stun = 0;
   223	    }

   225	  function restart() {
   226	    state.phase = 'play'; state.level = 1; state.lives = 3; state.score = 0; state.paddle.x = W / 2; state.paddle.targetX = W / 2; buildLevel(1); wakeAudio();
   227	  }

   229	  function update(dt) {
   230	    if (state.phase === 'over') { updateParticles(dt); return; }
   231	    dt = Math.min(0.032, dt);
   232	    state.shake = Math.max(0, state.shake - dt * 22); state.flash = Math.max(0, state.flash - dt); state.comboTimer -= dt;
   233	    if (state.comboTimer <= 0) state.combo = 0;
   234	    const p = state.paddle;
   235	    if (state.paddle.stun > 0) state.paddle.stun -= dt;
   236	    if (p.sticky > 0) p.sticky -= dt;
   237	    if (p.laser > 0) p.laser -= dt;
   238	    const keyboard = (keyRight ? 1 : 0) - (keyLeft ? 1 : 0);
   239	    if (keyboard) p.targetX += keyboard * 430 * dt;
   240	    p.targetX = clamp(p.targetX, p.w / 2 + 8, W - p.w / 2 - 8);
   241	    const follow = p.stun > 0 ? 0.12 : 0.28;
   242	    p.x += (p.targetX - p.x) * Math.min(1, follow * 60 * dt);
   243	    if (state.boss) { state.boss.t += dt; state.boss.x = W / 2 + Math.sin(state.boss.t * 1.4) * (W * 0.33); }
   244	    for (const ball of [...state.balls]) updateBall(ball, dt);
   245	    updateDebris(dt); updatePowerups(dt); updateLasers(dt); updateParticles(dt);
   246	    if (state.phase === 'play' && state.boss && state.boss.hp <= 0) {
   247	      state.boss = null; addScore(500); burst(W / 2, 74, '#fff2a5', 35);
   248	      if (state.wallRemaining <= 0) completeWall();
   249	    }
   250	  }
   251	  function updateBall(ball, dt) {
   252	    if (ball.stuck || !ball.launched) { ball.x = state.paddle.x; ball.y = state.paddle.y - ball.r - 3; return; }
   253	    ball.trail.push({ x: ball.x, y: ball.y }); if (ball.trail.length > 6) ball.trail.shift();
   254	    ball.x += ball.vx * dt; ball.y += ball.vy * dt;
   255	    if (ball.x < ball.r) { ball.x = ball.r; ball.vx = Math.abs(ball.vx); beep(120, 0.018); }
   256	    if (ball.x > W - ball.r) { ball.x = W - ball.r; ball.vx = -Math.abs(ball.vx); beep(120, 0.018); }
   257	    if (ball.y < 62 + ball.r) { ball.y = 62 + ball.r; ball.vy = Math.abs(ball.vy); beep(120, 0.018); }
   258	    const p = state.paddle;
   259	    if (ball.vy > 0 && circleRectHit(ball, { x: p.x - p.w / 2, y: p.y, w: p.w, h: p.h })) {
   260	      ball.y = p.y - ball.r - 1;
   261	      const hit = clamp((ball.x - p.x) / (p.w / 2), -1, 1);
   262	      const speed = clamp(Math.hypot(ball.vx, ball.vy) * 1.015, 260, ball.heavy ? 475 : 420);
   263	      ball.vx = hit * speed * 0.92; ball.vy = -Math.sqrt(Math.max(120, speed * speed - ball.vx * ball.vx));
   264	      if (p.sticky > 0 && !ball.stuck) { ball.stuck = true; ball.launched = false; state.armed = true; }
   265	      burst(ball.x, p.y, ball.heavy ? '#ffca66' : '#eaffff', 4); beep(245, 0.025);
   266	    }
   267	    for (const d of state.debris) {
   268	      if (!circleRectHit(ball, d)) continue;
   269	      if (ball.heavy) { d.life = 0; addScore(12); burst(ball.x, ball.y, d.color, 5); }
   270	      else { bounceFromRect(ball, d); d.life -= 1.5; }
   271	      state.shake = Math.min(8, state.shake + 1.2); break;
   272	    }
   273	    for (const b of state.bricks) {
   274	      if (!b.alive || !circleRectHit(ball, b)) continue;
   275	      const beforeY = ball.y;
   276	      b.hit = 0.12; b.hp -= ball.heavy ? 2 : 1;
   277	      if (b.hp <= 0) destroyBrick(b, 'ball'); else { addScore(4); burst(ball.x, ball.y, b.color, 3); beep(240, 0.022); }
   278	      if (!ball.heavy || b.hp > 0) bounceFromRect(ball, b); else {
   279	        ball.vy *= 1.015; ball.vx *= 1.015;
   280	        if (Math.abs(ball.y - beforeY) < 0.2) ball.vy *= -1;
   281	      }
   282	      break;
   283	    }
   284	    if (state.boss && circleRectHit(ball, { x: state.boss.x - state.boss.r, y: state.boss.y - state.boss.r, w: state.boss.r * 2, h: state.boss.r * 2 })) {
   285	      state.boss.hp -= ball.heavy ? 2 : 1; bounceFromRect(ball, { x: state.boss.x - state.boss.r, y: state.boss.y - state.boss.r, w: state.boss.r * 2, h: state.boss.r * 2 });
   286	      burst(state.boss.x, state.boss.y, '#fff19a', 6); addScore(12); state.shake = 4;
   287	    }
   288	    if (ball.y - ball.r > H + 20) loseBall(ball);
   289	  }
   290	  function bounceFromRect(ball, rect) {
   291	    const left = Math.abs(ball.x - rect.x), right = Math.abs(ball.x - (rect.x + rect.w));
   292	    const top = Math.abs(ball.y - rect.y), bottom = Math.abs(ball.y - (rect.y + rect.h));
   293	    if (Math.min(left, right) < Math.min(top, bottom)) ball.vx *= -1; else ball.vy *= -1;
   294	    const speed = clamp(Math.hypot(ball.vx, ball.vy) * 1.003, 230, ball.heavy ? 470 : 410);
   295	    const len = Math.max(1, Math.hypot(ball.vx, ball.vy)); ball.vx = ball.vx / len * speed; ball.vy = ball.vy / len * speed;
   296	  }
   297	
   298	  function updateDebris(dt) {
   299	    for (const d of state.debris) {
   300	      d.vy += 370 * d.mass * dt; d.x += d.vx * dt; d.y += d.vy * dt; d.angle += d.spin * dt; d.life -= dt;
   301	      if (d.x < -40 || d.x > W + 40 || d.y > H + 60) d.life = 0;
   302	      const box = { x: d.x, y: d.y, w: d.w, h: d.h };
   303	      if (d.vy > 0 && rectsHit(box, { x: state.paddle.x - state.paddle.w / 2, y: state.paddle.y, w: state.paddle.w, h: state.paddle.h })) {
   304	        d.life = 0; state.paddle.stun = Math.max(state.paddle.stun, 0.8); state.shake = 9; burst(d.x + d.w / 2, state.paddle.y, '#ff718d', 10); beep(72, 0.08, 'sawtooth');
   305	      }
   306	      if (d.vy > 0) for (const b of state.bricks) {
   307	        if (b.alive && rectsHit(box, b)) { destroyBrick(b, 'crush'); d.life = 0; break; }
   308	      }
   309	    }
   310	    state.debris = state.debris.filter(d => d.life > 0);
   311	  }
   312	  function updatePowerups(dt) {
   313	    for (const p of state.powerups) {
   314	      p.y += p.vy * dt; p.t += dt * 4;
   315	      if (rectsHit({ x: p.x - p.w / 2, y: p.y - p.h / 2, w: p.w, h: p.h }, { x: state.paddle.x - state.paddle.w / 2, y: state.paddle.y - 5, w: state.paddle.w, h: state.paddle.h + 10 })) { p.y = H + 99; applyPowerup(p.type); }
   316	    }
   317	    state.powerups = state.powerups.filter(p => p.y < H + 30);
   318	  }
   319	  function updateLasers(dt) {
   320	    for (const l of state.lasers) {
   321	      l.y += l.vy * dt;
   322	      for (const b of state.bricks) if (b.alive && rectsHit(l, b)) { l.y = -100; destroyBrick(b, 'laser'); break; }
   323	      if (state.boss && rectsHit(l, { x: state.boss.x - state.boss.r, y: state.boss.y - state.boss.r, w: state.boss.r * 2, h: state.boss.r * 2 })) { state.boss.hp--; l.y = -100; burst(state.boss.x, state.boss.y, '#ff8bb5', 4); }
   324	    }
   325	    state.lasers = state.lasers.filter(l => l.y > -30);
   326	  }
   327	  function burst(x, y, color, count) {
   328	    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) state.particles.push({ x, y, vx: rand(-125, 125), vy: rand(-145, 85), life: rand(0.3, 0.8), max: 0.8, size: rand(1.5, 4), color });
   329	  }
   330	  function updateParticles(dt) {
   331	    for (const q of state.particles) { q.x += q.vx * dt; q.y += q.vy * dt; q.vy += 210 * dt; q.life -= dt; }
   332	    state.particles = state.particles.filter(q => q.life > 0);
   333	  }
   334	
   335	  function draw() {
   336	    ctx.setTransform(pixelScale, 0, 0, pixelScale, 0, 0);
   337	    const g = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, 0, 0, H); g.addColorStop(0, '#0a1021'); g.addColorStop(0.55, '#0b1020'); g.addColorStop(1, '#121021');
   338	    ctx.fillStyle = g; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
   339	    ctx.save();
   340	    const sx = state.shake ? rand(-state.shake, state.shake) : 0, sy = state.shake ? rand(-state.shake, state.shake) : 0; ctx.translate(sx, sy);
   341	    drawStars(); drawHud(); drawArena();
   342	    for (const b of state.bricks) if (b.alive) drawBrick(b);
   343	    for (const d of state.debris) drawDebris(d);
   344	    if (state.boss) drawBoss();
   345	    for (const p of state.powerups) drawPowerup(p);
   346	    for (const l of state.lasers) { ctx.fillStyle = '#ff8bb5'; ctx.shadowColor = '#ff6b9b'; ctx.shadowBlur = 12; roundedRect(ctx, l.x, l.y, l.w, l.h, 2); ctx.fill(); ctx.shadowBlur = 0; }
   347	    drawPaddle(); for (const ball of state.balls) drawBall(ball); drawParticles();
   348	    ctx.restore();
   349	    if (state.flash) { ctx.fillStyle = `rgba(255,255,255,${state.flash * 2})`; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H); }
   350	    if (state.phase === 'over') drawEndCard();
   351	  }
   352	  function drawStars() {
   353	    ctx.fillStyle = '#d8e6ff'; for (const s of state.stars) { ctx.globalAlpha = s.a; ctx.fillRect(s.x, s.y, s.r, s.r); } ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
   354	  }
   355	  function drawHud() {
   356	    ctx.fillStyle = '#f3f7ff'; ctx.font = `700 14px ${FONT}`; ctx.textBaseline = 'top';
   357	    ctx.fillText('BREACH & BRICK', 14, 15);
   358	    ctx.fillStyle = '#8591b1'; ctx.font = `11px ${FONT}`; ctx.fillText(`WALL ${String(state.level).padStart(2, '0')}`, 14, 36);
   359	    ctx.textAlign = 'right'; ctx.fillStyle = '#e8f0ff'; ctx.font = `700 14px ${FONT}`; ctx.fillText(String(state.score).padStart(5, '0'), W - 14, 15);
   360	    ctx.fillStyle = '#8591b1'; ctx.font = `11px ${FONT}`; ctx.fillText(`BEST ${String(state.best).padStart(5, '0')}`, W - 14, 36); ctx.textAlign = 'left';
   361	    for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) { ctx.fillStyle = i < state.lives ? '#ff719d' : '#2a2f42'; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(18 + i * 18, 62, 5, 0, TAU); ctx.fill(); }
   362	    if (state.combo > 1 && state.comboTimer > 0) { ctx.fillStyle = '#ffc85b'; ctx.font = `700 11px ${FONT}`; ctx.fillText(`COMBO x${state.combo}`, 78, 57); }
   363	    if (state.paddle.sticky > 0 || state.paddle.laser > 0) {
   364	      ctx.textAlign = 'right'; ctx.font = `10px ${FONT}`; ctx.fillStyle = state.paddle.laser > 0 ? '#ff8bb5' : '#d593ff';
   365	      ctx.fillText(`${state.paddle.laser > 0 ? 'LASER' : 'STICKY'} ${Math.ceil(Math.max(state.paddle.laser, state.paddle.sticky))}`, W - 14, 57); ctx.textAlign = 'left';
   366	    }
   367	  }
   368	  function drawArena() {
   369	    ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(105,133,190,0.2)'; ctx.lineWidth = 1; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(8, 75); ctx.lineTo(W - 8, 75); ctx.stroke();
   370	    ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(90,120,190,0.05)'; ctx.fillRect(8, 78, W - 16, H - 160);
   371	    if (state.armed && state.phase === 'play') { ctx.fillStyle = '#8998ba'; ctx.font = `11px ${FONT}`; ctx.textAlign = 'center'; ctx.fillText('DRAG TO STEER  •  TAP TO LAUNCH', W / 2, H - 25); ctx.textAlign = 'left'; }
   372	  }
   373	  function drawBrick(b) {
   374	    const alpha = b.support ? 1 : 0.8; ctx.globalAlpha = alpha;
   375	    const grad = ctx.createLinearGradient(b.x, b.y, b.x, b.y + b.h); grad.addColorStop(0, b.color); grad.addColorStop(1, shade(b.color, -0.42));
   376	    ctx.fillStyle = grad; roundedRect(ctx, b.x, b.y, b.w, b.h, 5); ctx.fill();
   377	    ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.23)'; roundedRect(ctx, b.x + 3, b.y + 3, b.w - 6, 3, 2); ctx.fill();
   378	    if (b.maxHp > 1) { ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(4,8,18,0.45)'; ctx.fillRect(b.x + 7, b.y + b.h - 5, (b.w - 14) * (b.hp / b.maxHp), 2); }
   379	    ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
   380	  }
   381	  function drawDebris(d) { ctx.save(); ctx.translate(d.x + d.w / 2, d.y + d.h / 2); ctx.rotate(d.angle); ctx.fillStyle = shade(d.color, -0.25); roundedRect(ctx, -d.w / 2, -d.h / 2, d.w, d.h, 4); ctx.fill(); ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,.25)'; ctx.stroke(); ctx.restore(); }
   382	  function drawBoss() {
   383	    const b = state.boss; ctx.save(); ctx.translate(b.x, b.y); ctx.rotate(b.t * 0.7); ctx.fillStyle = '#3a2849'; ctx.strokeStyle = '#ff719d'; ctx.lineWidth = 2; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(0, 0, b.r + 7, 0, TAU); ctx.fill(); ctx.stroke();
   384	    ctx.fillStyle = '#ffcf69'; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(0, 0, b.r * 0.62 + Math.sin(b.t * 6) * 2, 0, TAU); ctx.fill(); ctx.fillStyle = '#fff5c4'; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(-4, -4, 4, 0, TAU); ctx.fill(); ctx.restore();
   385	    ctx.fillStyle = '#27182e'; ctx.fillRect(W * 0.25, 80, W * 0.5, 4); ctx.fillStyle = '#ff719d'; ctx.fillRect(W * 0.25, 80, W * 0.5 * b.hp / b.maxHp, 4);
   386	  }
   387	  function drawPowerup(p) { ctx.save(); ctx.translate(p.x, p.y); ctx.rotate(Math.sin(p.t) * 0.08); ctx.fillStyle = powerColor(p.type); ctx.shadowColor = powerColor(p.type); ctx.shadowBlur = 12; roundedRect(ctx, -p.w / 2, -p.h / 2, p.w, p.h, 6); ctx.fill(); ctx.shadowBlur = 0; ctx.fillStyle = '#07101d'; ctx.font = `700 10px ${FONT}`; ctx.textAlign = 'center'; ctx.textBaseline = 'middle'; ctx.fillText(p.type === 'multi' ? 'M' : p.type === 'wreck' ? 'W' : p.type === 'sticky' ? 'S' : 'L', 0, 1); ctx.restore(); }
   388	  function drawPaddle() {
   389	    const p = state.paddle; ctx.save(); ctx.translate(p.x, p.y); const col = p.stun > 0 ? '#ff526f' : p.laser > 0 ? '#ff8bb5' : p.sticky > 0 ? '#d593ff' : '#71e3d0';
   390	    ctx.shadowColor = col; ctx.shadowBlur = 16; ctx.fillStyle = col; roundedRect(ctx, -p.w / 2, 0, p.w, p.h, 7); ctx.fill(); ctx.shadowBlur = 0; ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,.65)'; roundedRect(ctx, -p.w / 2 + 9, 3, p.w - 18, 3, 2); ctx.fill(); ctx.restore();
   391	  }
   392	  function drawBall(ball) {
   393	    for (let i = 0; i < ball.trail.length; i++) { const t = ball.trail[i], a = i / ball.trail.length * 0.23; ctx.fillStyle = `rgba(159,244,236,${a})`; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(t.x, t.y, ball.r * (i / ball.trail.length) * 0.8, 0, TAU); ctx.fill(); }
   394	    ctx.fillStyle = ball.color; ctx.shadowColor = ball.color; ctx.shadowBlur = ball.heavy ? 22 : 11; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(ball.x, ball.y, ball.r, 0, TAU); ctx.fill(); ctx.shadowBlur = 0; ctx.fillStyle = '#fff'; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(ball.x - ball.r * .3, ball.y - ball.r * .35, ball.r * .25, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
   395	  }
   396	  function drawParticles() { for (const q of state.particles) { ctx.globalAlpha = Math.max(0, q.life / q.max); ctx.fillStyle = q.color; ctx.fillRect(q.x, q.y, q.size, q.size); } ctx.globalAlpha = 1; }
   397	  function drawEndCard() {
   398	    ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(6,9,18,.76)'; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H); ctx.textAlign = 'center';
   399	    ctx.fillStyle = '#ff719d'; ctx.font = `700 28px ${FONT}`; ctx.fillText('RUN ENDED', W / 2, H * .39);
   400	    ctx.fillStyle = '#f3f7ff'; ctx.font = `15px ${FONT}`; ctx.fillText(`SCORE ${state.score}  •  BEST ${state.best}`, W / 2, H * .39 + 36);
   401	    ctx.fillStyle = '#9eabc8'; ctx.font = `12px ${FONT}`; ctx.fillText('TAP OR PRESS ENTER TO REBUILD', W / 2, H * .39 + 72); ctx.textAlign = 'left';
   402	  }

   404	  function shade(hex, amount) {
   405	    const n = parseInt(hex.slice(1), 16), r = clamp((n >> 16) + amount * 255, 0, 255), g = clamp(((n >> 8) & 255) + amount * 255, 0, 255), b = clamp((n & 255) + amount * 255, 0, 255);
   406	    return `rgb(${r | 0},${g | 0},${b | 0})`;
   407	  }
   408	  function pointFromEvent(e) { const rect = canvas.getBoundingClientRect(); return { x: e.clientX - rect.left, y: e.clientY - rect.top }; }
   409	  canvas.addEventListener('pointerdown', e => { e.preventDefault(); wakeAudio(); const q = pointFromEvent(e); pointer = { down: true, x: q.x, y: q.y, startX: q.x, startY: q.y, started: performance.now() }; state.paddle.targetX = q.x; try { canvas.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId); } catch (_) {} }, { passive: false });
   410	  canvas.addEventListener('pointermove', e => { e.preventDefault(); const q = pointFromEvent(e); if (pointer.down || e.pointerType === 'mouse') { pointer.x = q.x; pointer.y = q.y; state.paddle.targetX = q.x; } }, { passive: false });
   411	  canvas.addEventListener('pointerup', e => { e.preventDefault(); const q = pointFromEvent(e); const tap = Math.hypot(q.x - pointer.startX, q.y - pointer.startY) < 16 && performance.now() - pointer.started < 420; pointer.down = false; if (tap) action(); }, { passive: false });
   412	  canvas.addEventListener('pointercancel', () => { pointer.down = false; }, { passive: true });
   413	  canvas.addEventListener('touchmove', e => e.preventDefault(), { passive: false });
   414	  window.addEventListener('keydown', e => { if (e.key === 'ArrowLeft' || e.key.toLowerCase() === 'a') { keyLeft = true; e.preventDefault(); } if (e.key === 'ArrowRight' || e.key.toLowerCase() === 'd') { keyRight = true; e.preventDefault(); } if (e.key === ' ' || e.key === 'Enter' || e.key.toLowerCase() === 'w') { action(); e.preventDefault(); } });
   415	  window.addEventListener('keyup', e => { if (e.key === 'ArrowLeft' || e.key.toLowerCase() === 'a') keyLeft = false; if (e.key === 'ArrowRight' || e.key.toLowerCase() === 'd') keyRight = false; });

    37	  function mulberry32(seed) {
    38	    return () => {
    39	      seed |= 0;
    40	      seed = seed + 0x6D2B79F5 | 0;
    41	      let t = Math.imul(seed ^ seed >>> 15, 1 | seed);
    42	      t = t + Math.imul(t ^ t >>> 7, 61 | t) ^ t;
    43	      return ((t ^ t >>> 14) >>> 0) / 4294967296;
    44	    };
    45	  }
    46	  function pick(rng, list) { return list[Math.floor(rng() * list.length)]; }
    47	  function inside(x, y, w, h) { return x >= 0 && y >= 0 && x < w && y < h; }
    48	  function insideRoom(x, y, w, h) { return x > 0 && y > 0 && x < w - 1 && y < h - 1; }
    49	  function hasCrate(crates, x, y, skip = -1) {
    50	    return crates.some((c, i) => i !== skip && c.x === x && c.y === y);
    51	  }
    52	  function getCrate(crates, x, y) {
    53	    return crates.findIndex(c => c.x === x && c.y === y);
    54	  }
    55	  function reverseCandidates(state, w, h) {
    56	    const out = [];
    57	    const { x, y } = state.worker;
    58	    for (const d of DIRS) {
    59	      const px = x + d.x;
    60	      const py = y + d.y;
    61	      if (insideRoom(px, py, w, h) && !hasCrate(state.crates, px, py)) {
    62	        out.push({ kind: "walk", dx: d.x, dy: d.y });
    63	      }
    64	    }
    65	    for (const d of DIRS) {
    66	      const crate = getCrate(state.crates, x + d.x, y + d.y);
    67	      const px = x - d.x;
    68	      const py = y - d.y;
    69	      if (crate >= 0 && insideRoom(px, py, w, h) && !hasCrate(state.crates, px, py, crate)) {
    70	        out.push({ kind: "push", dx: d.x, dy: d.y, crate });
    71	      }
    72	    }
    73	    return out;
    74	  }
    75	  function applyReverse(state, op) {
    76	    const old = clonePoint(state.worker);
    77	    if (op.kind === "walk") {
    78	      state.worker.x += op.dx;
    79	      state.worker.y += op.dy;
    80	      return { dx: -op.dx, dy: -op.dy };
    81	    }
    82	    state.crates[op.crate].x = old.x;
    83	    state.crates[op.crate].y = old.y;
    84	    state.worker.x = old.x - op.dx;
    85	    state.worker.y = old.y - op.dy;
    86	    return { dx: op.dx, dy: op.dy };
    87	  }
    88	  function solveState(state, targets) {
    89	    return targets.every(t => state.crates.some(c => c.x === t.x && c.y === t.y && c.color === t.color));
    90	  }
    91	  function makeTargetPoints(w, h, count, rng) {
    92	    const midX = Math.floor(w / 2);
    93	    const midY = Math.floor(h / 2);
    94	    const pool = [
    95	      { x: 1, y: 1 }, { x: w - 2, y: h - 2 }, { x: w - 2, y: 1 }, { x: 1, y: h - 2 },
    96	      { x: midX, y: 1 }, { x: w - 2, y: midY }, { x: midX, y: h - 2 }, { x: 1, y: midY }
    97	    ];
    98	    const points = [];
    99	    for (const p of pool.sort(() => rng() - .5)) {
   100	      if (!points.some(q => q.x === p.x && q.y === p.y)) points.push(p);
   101	      if (points.length === count) return points;
   102	    }
   103	    return points;
   104	  }
   105	  function makeLevel(index, seedBump = 0) {
   106	    const rng = mulberry32(0x41C64E6D + index * 0x9E3779B9 + seedBump * 0x6D2B79F5);
   107	    const stage = Math.floor(index / 6);
   108	    const w = index < 6 ? 7 : index < 18 ? 8 : 9;
   109	    const h = index < 6 ? 7 : index < 12 ? 7 : 8;
   110	    const count = index < 6 ? 1 : index < 12 ? 2 : index < 24 ? 3 : 4;
   111	    const targets = makeTargetPoints(w, h, count, rng).map((p, i) => ({ ...p, color: i % COLORS.length }));
   112	    const occupied = new Set(targets.map(p => key(p.x, p.y)));
   113	    let worker = { x: Math.floor(w / 2), y: Math.floor(h / 2) };
   114	    if (occupied.has(key(worker.x, worker.y))) {
   115	      const options = [];
   116	      for (let y = 1; y < h - 1; y++) for (let x = 1; x < w - 1; x++) if (!occupied.has(key(x, y))) options.push({ x, y });
   117	      worker = pick(rng, options);
   118	    }
   119	    const state = { worker, crates: targets.map(p => ({ x: p.x, y: p.y, color: p.color })) };
   120	    const trace = [cloneState(state)];
   121	    const reverseOps = [];
   122	    let pushes = 0;
   123	    const desiredPushes = Math.min(5 + stage * 2, Math.max(3, count * 3));
   124	    const goalSteps = 18 + index * 5;
   125	    const runReverse = () => {
   126	      const candidates = reverseCandidates(state, w, h);
   127	      if (!candidates.length) return false;
   128	      const pushMoves = candidates.filter(c => c.kind === "push");
   129	      let op;
   130	      const walkMoves = candidates.filter(c => c.kind === "walk");
   131	      if (pushMoves.length && (pushes < desiredPushes || !walkMoves.length || rng() < 0.34)) {
   132	        op = pick(rng, pushMoves);
   133	        pushes++;
   134	      } else {
   135	        op = pick(rng, walkMoves);
   136	      }
   137	      reverseOps.push(applyReverse(state, op));
   138	      trace.push(cloneState(state));
   139	      return true;
   140	    };

   144	
   145	    const traceCells = new Set();
   146	    trace.forEach(s => {
   147	      traceCells.add(key(s.worker.x, s.worker.y));
   148	      s.crates.forEach(c => traceCells.add(key(c.x, c.y)));
   149	    });
   150	    const free = [];
   151	    for (let y = 1; y < h - 1; y++) for (let x = 1; x < w - 1; x++) {
   152	      if (!traceCells.has(key(x, y))) free.push({ x, y });
   153	    }
   154	    const used = new Set();
   155	    const takeFree = () => {
   156	      const choices = free.filter(p => !used.has(key(p.x, p.y)));
   157	      if (!choices.length) return null;
   158	      const p = pick(rng, choices);
   159	      used.add(key(p.x, p.y));
   160	      return p;
   161	    };
   162	    const conveyors = [];
   163	    const gates = [];
   164	    let plate = null;
   165	    let door = null;
   166	    if (index >= 12) {
   167	      for (let i = 0; i < (index >= 18 ? 3 : 2); i++) {
   168	        const p = takeFree();
   169	        if (p) {
   170	          const d = pick(rng, DIRS);
   171	          conveyors.push({ ...p, dx: d.x, dy: d.y });
   172	        }
   173	      }
   174	    }
   175	    if (index >= 18) {
   176	      const p = takeFree();
   177	      if (p) {
   178	        const d = pick(rng, DIRS);
   179	        gates.push({ ...p, dx: d.x, dy: d.y });
   180	      }
   181	    }
   182	    if (index >= 24) {
   183	      plate = takeFree();
   184	      door = takeFree();
   185	    }
   186	    const walls = new Set();
   187	    const wallCount = Math.min(free.length - used.size, index < 6 ? 1 : 2 + stage);
   188	    for (let i = 0; i < wallCount; i++) {
   189	      const p = takeFree();
   190	      if (p) walls.add(key(p.x, p.y));
   191	    }
   192	    if (reverseOps.length < Math.max(16, Math.floor(goalSteps * .75)) && seedBump < 20) return makeLevel(index, seedBump + 1);
   193	    const mechanics = index < 6 ? "PUSH ROUTE" : index < 12 ? "DUAL CORE" : index < 18 ? "COLOR SORT" : index < 24 ? "FLOW GATES" : "LOCK SEQUENCE";
   194	    return {
   195	      w, h, targets, worker: state.worker, crates: state.crates, walls, conveyors, gates, plate, door,
   196	      par: reverseOps.length, mechanics, proof: reverseOps.length > 0
   197	    };
   198	  }
   199	  function loadProgress() {
   200	    try {
   201	      const saved = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(SAVE_KEY) || "null");
   202	      if (saved && Array.isArray(saved.stars)) return {
   203	        unlocked: Math.max(0, saved.unlocked | 0),
   204	        stars: saved.stars.slice(0, 30).map(n => Math.max(0, Math.min(3, n | 0))),
   205	        best: Array.isArray(saved.best) ? saved.best.slice(0, 30).map(n => n > 0 ? n | 0 : 0) : []
   206	      };
   207	    } catch (_) {}
   208	    return { unlocked: 0, stars: [], best: [] };
   209	  }
   210	  function saveProgress() {
   211	    try { localStorage.setItem(SAVE_KEY, JSON.stringify(progress)); } catch (_) {}
   212	  }
   213	  function startLevel(index) {
   214	    levelIndex = Math.max(0, Math.min(levels.length - 1, index));
   215	    const l = levels[levelIndex];
   216	    play = { worker: clonePoint(l.worker), crates: cloneCrates(l.crates), history: [], moves: 0, won: false, stars: 0 };
   217	    mode = "game";
   218	    pulse = 0;
   219	    particles = [];
   220	  }
   221	  function openLevels() { mode = "select"; pointer = null; }
   222	  function resetLevel() {
   223	    startLevel(levelIndex);
   224	    burstAt(195, 340, "#8ba5bb", 9);
   225	  }
   226	  function undo() {
   227	    if (!play || !play.history.length) return;
   228	    const previous = play.history.pop();
   229	    play.worker = previous.worker;
   230	    play.crates = previous.crates;
   231	    play.moves = previous.moves;
   232	    play.won = false;
   233	    play.stars = 0;
   234	    shake = 0;
   235	    pulse = .2;
   236	  }
   237	  function isDoorOpen() {
   238	    const l = levels[levelIndex];
   239	    if (!l.door || !l.plate) return true;
   240	    return hasCrate(play.crates, l.plate.x, l.plate.y);
   241	  }
   242	  function gateAt(x, y) { return levels[levelIndex].gates.find(g => g.x === x && g.y === y); }
   243	  function conveyorAt(x, y) { return levels[levelIndex].conveyors.find(c => c.x === x && c.y === y); }
   244	  function canEnter(from, to) {
   245	    const l = levels[levelIndex];
   246	    if (!insideRoom(to.x, to.y, l.w, l.h) || l.walls.has(key(to.x, to.y))) return false;
   247	    if (l.door && l.door.x === to.x && l.door.y === to.y && !isDoorOpen()) return false;
   248	    const gate = gateAt(to.x, to.y);
   249	    if (gate && (to.x - from.x !== gate.dx || to.y - from.y !== gate.dy)) return false;
   250	    return true;
   251	  }
   252	  function resolveConveyor() {
   253	    const l = levels[levelIndex];
   254	    for (let n = 0; n < 6; n++) {
   255	      const belt = conveyorAt(play.worker.x, play.worker.y);
   256	      if (!belt) break;
   257	      const next = { x: play.worker.x + belt.dx, y: play.worker.y + belt.dy };
   258	      if (!canEnter(play.worker, next)) break;
   259	      const crate = getCrate(play.crates, next.x, next.y);
   260	      if (crate >= 0) {
   261	        const beyond = { x: next.x + belt.dx, y: next.y + belt.dy };
   262	        if (getCrate(play.crates, beyond.x, beyond.y) >= 0 || !canEnter(next, beyond)) break;
   263	        play.crates[crate].x = beyond.x;
   264	        play.crates[crate].y = beyond.y;
   265	      }
   266	      play.worker = next;
   267	      burstAt(cellCenter(next.x, next.y).x, cellCenter(next.x, next.y).y, "#9cecff", 2);
   268	    }
   269	  }
   270	  function tryMove(dx, dy) {
   271	    if (mode !== "game" || !play || play.won) return;
   272	    const from = clonePoint(play.worker);
   273	    const next = { x: from.x + dx, y: from.y + dy };
   274	    if (!canEnter(from, next)) { shake = .08; return; }
   275	    const crate = getCrate(play.crates, next.x, next.y);
   276	    if (crate >= 0) {
   277	      const beyond = { x: next.x + dx, y: next.y + dy };
   278	      if (getCrate(play.crates, beyond.x, beyond.y) >= 0 || !canEnter(next, beyond)) { shake = .08; return; }
   279	      play.history.push({ worker: clonePoint(play.worker), crates: cloneCrates(play.crates), moves: play.moves });
   280	      play.crates[crate].x = beyond.x;
   281	      play.crates[crate].y = beyond.y;
   282	      play.worker = next;
   283	    } else {
   284	      play.history.push({ worker: clonePoint(play.worker), crates: cloneCrates(play.crates), moves: play.moves });
   285	      play.worker = next;
   286	    }
   287	    play.moves++;
   288	    resolveConveyor();
   289	    const c = cellCenter(play.worker.x, play.worker.y);
   290	    burstAt(c.x, c.y, crate >= 0 ? COLORS[play.crates[crate].color].glow : "#9cecff", crate >= 0 ? 7 : 3);
   291	    pulse = .35;
   292	    if (solveState(play, levels[levelIndex].targets)) winLevel();
   293	  }
   294	  function winLevel() {
   295	    play.won = true;
   296	    play.stars = play.moves <= levels[levelIndex].par ? 3 : play.moves <= Math.ceil(levels[levelIndex].par * 1.3) ? 2 : 1;
   297	    progress.stars[levelIndex] = Math.max(progress.stars[levelIndex] || 0, play.stars);
   298	    progress.best[levelIndex] = progress.best[levelIndex] ? Math.min(progress.best[levelIndex], play.moves) : play.moves;
   299	    progress.unlocked = Math.max(progress.unlocked || 0, Math.min(levels.length - 1, levelIndex + 1));
   300	    saveProgress();
   301	    const c = cellCenter(play.worker.x, play.worker.y);
   302	    burstAt(c.x, c.y, "#ffe19a", 26);
   303	    shake = .35;
   304	  }
   305	  function burstAt(x, y, color, count) {
   306	    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
   307	      const a = Math.random() * TAU;
   308	      const speed = 18 + Math.random() * 62;
   309	      particles.push({ x, y, vx: Math.cos(a) * speed, vy: Math.sin(a) * speed - 12, life: .35 + Math.random() * .45, max: .75, color, size: 1.5 + Math.random() * 3 });
   310	    }
   311	  }
   312	  function update(dt) {
   313	    elapsed += dt;
   314	    pulse = Math.max(0, pulse - dt);
   315	    shake = Math.max(0, shake - dt * 1.8);
   316	    for (const p of particles) { p.life -= dt; p.x += p.vx * dt; p.y += p.vy * dt; p.vy += 88 * dt; }
   317	    particles = particles.filter(p => p.life > 0);
   318	  }
   319	  function resize() {
   320	    const width = window.innerWidth;
   321	    const height = window.innerHeight;
   322	    const rawDpr = Math.min(window.devicePixelRatio || 1, 2);
   323	    const dpr = Math.min(rawDpr, Math.max(.5, 960 / Math.max(width, height)));
   324	    canvas.width = Math.max(1, Math.round(width * dpr));
   325	    canvas.height = Math.max(1, Math.round(height * dpr));
   326	    view.dpr = dpr;
   327	    view.scale = Math.min(width / VW, height / VH);
   328	    view.ox = (width - VW * view.scale) / 2;
   329	    view.oy = (height - VH * view.scale) / 2;
   330	  }
   331	  function logicalPoint(e) {
   332	    const r = canvas.getBoundingClientRect();
   333	    return { x: (e.clientX - r.left - view.ox) / view.scale, y: (e.clientY - r.top - view.oy) / view.scale };
   334	  }
   335	  function roundRect(x, y, w, h, r) {
   336	    r = Math.min(r, w / 2, h / 2);
   337	    ctx.beginPath();
   338	    ctx.moveTo(x + r, y); ctx.lineTo(x + w - r, y); ctx.quadraticCurveTo(x + w, y, x + w, y + r);
   339	    ctx.lineTo(x + w, y + h - r); ctx.quadraticCurveTo(x + w, y + h, x + w - r, y + h);
   340	    ctx.lineTo(x + r, y + h); ctx.quadraticCurveTo(x, y + h, x, y + h - r);
   341	    ctx.lineTo(x, y + r); ctx.quadraticCurveTo(x, y, x + r, y); ctx.closePath();
   342	  }
   343	  function text(value, x, y, size, color, align = "left", weight = 700) {
   344	    ctx.font = `${weight} ${size}px system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, "Segoe UI", sans-serif`;
   345	    ctx.textAlign = align; ctx.textBaseline = "middle"; ctx.fillStyle = color; ctx.fillText(value, x, y);
   346	  }
   347	  function line(x1, y1, x2, y2, color, width = 1) {
   348	    ctx.strokeStyle = color; ctx.lineWidth = width; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(x1, y1); ctx.lineTo(x2, y2); ctx.stroke();
   349	  }
   350	  function drawBackground() {
   351	    const g = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, 0, 0, VH);
   352	    g.addColorStop(0, "#0c1723"); g.addColorStop(.55, "#09111b"); g.addColorStop(1, "#070c13");
   353	    ctx.fillStyle = g; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, VW, VH);
   354	    ctx.globalAlpha = .16;
   355	    for (let x = 10; x < VW; x += 20) line(x, 0, x, VH, "#1f3947");
   356	    for (let y = 8; y < VH; y += 20) line(0, y, VW, y, "#1f3947");
   357	    ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
   358	    ctx.fillStyle = "#142431"; ctx.fillRect(0, 69, VW, 1); ctx.fillRect(0, VH - 113, VW, 1);
   359	  }
   360	  function drawHeader() {
   361	    text("FORGELOCK", 22, 25, 18, "#f2f7fd", "left", 900);
   362	    text("PUSH-BLOCK FABRICATION", 23, 47, 8, "#5d93a8", "left", 800);
   363	    if (mode === "game") {
   364	      const l = levels[levelIndex];
   365	      text(`LEVEL ${String(levelIndex + 1).padStart(2, "0")}`, 198, 22, 11, "#dbe7ef", "left", 800);
   366	      text(l.mechanics, 198, 43, 8, "#67b3c9", "left", 800);
   367	      button(300, 13, 72, 33, "RESET", "#172a37", "#7dd9ee", 10);
   368	    } else {
   369	      button(300, 13, 72, 33, "PLAY", "#172a37", "#7dd9ee", 10);
   370	    }
   371	  }
   372	  function button(x, y, w, h, label, fill, accent, size = 11, disabled = false) {
   373	    ctx.save();
   374	    ctx.shadowColor = disabled ? "transparent" : "rgba(0,0,0,.25)"; ctx.shadowBlur = 8; ctx.shadowOffsetY = 3;
   375	    roundRect(x, y, w, h, 9); ctx.fillStyle = disabled ? "#131b23" : fill; ctx.fill(); ctx.shadowColor = "transparent";
   376	    ctx.strokeStyle = disabled ? "#202c37" : accent; ctx.lineWidth = 1; ctx.stroke();
   377	    text(label, x + w / 2, y + h / 2 + 1, size, disabled ? "#53616c" : accent, "center", 900);
   378	    ctx.restore();
   379	  }
   380	  function boardMetrics(l) {
   381	    const cell = Math.min(46, (VW - 38) / l.w, 392 / l.h);
   382	    return { cell, x: (VW - l.w * cell) / 2, y: 104 + (392 - l.h * cell) / 2 };
   383	  }
   384	  function cellCenter(x, y) {
   385	    const m = boardMetrics(levels[levelIndex]);
   386	    return { x: m.x + (x + .5) * m.cell, y: m.y + (y + .5) * m.cell };
   387	  }
   388	  function tileRect(m, x, y, inset = 2) {
   389	    return { x: m.x + x * m.cell + inset, y: m.y + y * m.cell + inset, w: m.cell - inset * 2, h: m.cell - inset * 2 };
   390	  }
   391	  function drawBoard() {
   392	    const l = levels[levelIndex];
   393	    const m = boardMetrics(l);
   394	    const shakeX = shake ? (Math.random() - .5) * shake * 9 : 0;
   395	    const shakeY = shake ? (Math.random() - .5) * shake * 7 : 0;
   396	    ctx.save(); ctx.translate(shakeX, shakeY);
   397	    roundRect(m.x - 12, m.y - 12, l.w * m.cell + 24, l.h * m.cell + 24, 17);
   398	    ctx.fillStyle = "#0d1a25"; ctx.fill(); ctx.strokeStyle = "#203b48"; ctx.lineWidth = 1; ctx.stroke();
   399	    for (let y = 0; y < l.h; y++) for (let x = 0; x < l.w; x++) {
   400	      const r = tileRect(m, x, y, 2);
   401	      if (x === 0 || y === 0 || x === l.w - 1 || y === l.h - 1 || l.walls.has(key(x, y))) {
   402	        roundRect(r.x, r.y, r.w, r.h, 5); ctx.fillStyle = "#111b25"; ctx.fill();
   403	        ctx.save(); ctx.clip();
   404	        for (let q = -r.h; q < r.w + r.h; q += 9) line(r.x + q, r.y + r.h, r.x + q + r.h, r.y, "#213541", 2);
   405	        ctx.restore();
   406	        continue;
   407	      }
   408	      roundRect(r.x, r.y, r.w, r.h, 5); ctx.fillStyle = ((x + y) % 2) ? "#1a2a34" : "#1b2d38"; ctx.fill();
   409	      ctx.strokeStyle = "#284451"; ctx.lineWidth = 1; ctx.stroke();
   410	    }
   411	    for (const b of l.conveyors) drawConveyor(m, b);
   412	    for (const g of l.gates) drawGate(m, g);
   413	    if (l.plate) drawPlate(m, l.plate, isDoorOpen());
   414	    if (l.door) drawDoor(m, l.door, isDoorOpen());
   415	    for (const t of l.targets) drawTarget(m, t);
   416	    for (const c of play.crates) drawCrate(m, c);
   417	    drawWorker(m, play.worker);
   418	    ctx.restore();
   419	  }
   420	  function drawConveyor(m, b) {
   421	    const r = tileRect(m, b.x, b.y, 4);
   422	    ctx.save(); roundRect(r.x, r.y, r.w, r.h, 4); ctx.clip();
   423	    ctx.fillStyle = "#1d4a59"; ctx.fill();
   424	    const shift = (elapsed * 28) % 18;
   425	    ctx.strokeStyle = "#57d3e2"; ctx.lineWidth = 2; ctx.globalAlpha = .75;
   426	    for (let p = -30 + shift; p < r.w + r.h; p += 18) {
   427	      ctx.beginPath();
   428	      if (b.dx !== 0) { const x = b.dx > 0 ? r.x + p : r.x + r.w - p; ctx.moveTo(x, r.y + 7); ctx.lineTo(x + (b.dx > 0 ? 5 : -5), r.y + r.h / 2); ctx.lineTo(x, r.y + r.h - 7); }
   429	      else { const y = b.dy > 0 ? r.y + p : r.y + r.h - p; ctx.moveTo(r.x + 7, y); ctx.lineTo(r.x + r.w / 2, y + (b.dy > 0 ? 5 : -5)); ctx.lineTo(r.x + r.w - 7, y); }
   430	      ctx.stroke();
   431	    }
   432	    ctx.restore(); ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
   433	  }
   434	  function drawGate(m, g) {
   435	    const r = tileRect(m, g.x, g.y, 5);
   436	    roundRect(r.x, r.y, r.w, r.h, 4); ctx.fillStyle = "#542e50"; ctx.fill(); ctx.strokeStyle = "#e36ca6"; ctx.lineWidth = 1; ctx.stroke();
   437	    const cx = r.x + r.w / 2, cy = r.y + r.h / 2;
   438	    ctx.strokeStyle = "#ff9cc5"; ctx.lineWidth = 2; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(cx - g.dx * 7 - g.dy * 5, cy - g.dy * 7 + g.dx * 5); ctx.lineTo(cx + g.dx * 7 + g.dy * 5, cy + g.dy * 7 - g.dx * 5); ctx.stroke();
   439	    text("›", cx + g.dx * 5, cy + g.dy * 5, 15, "#ffbfd7", "center", 900);
   440	  }
   441	  function drawPlate(m, p, active) {
   442	    const r = tileRect(m, p.x, p.y, 7);
   443	    roundRect(r.x, r.y, r.w, r.h, 4); ctx.fillStyle = active ? "#5b5430" : "#303840"; ctx.fill(); ctx.strokeStyle = active ? "#ffdc73" : "#71818b"; ctx.lineWidth = 2; ctx.stroke();
   444	    line(r.x + 7, r.y + r.h / 2, r.x + r.w - 7, r.y + r.h / 2, active ? "#ffe69a" : "#687782", 2);
   445	  }
   446	  function drawDoor(m, p, open) {
   447	    const r = tileRect(m, p.x, p.y, 4);
   448	    roundRect(r.x, r.y, r.w, r.h, 4); ctx.fillStyle = open ? "#31513e" : "#49243e"; ctx.fill(); ctx.strokeStyle = open ? "#8de68c" : "#ff78ae"; ctx.lineWidth = 2; ctx.stroke();
   449	    if (!open) { for (let x = r.x + 5; x < r.x + r.w; x += 9) line(x, r.y + 4, x, r.y + r.h - 4, "#d95087", 2); }
   450	    text(open ? "✓" : "×", r.x + r.w / 2, r.y + r.h / 2, 15, open ? "#c6ffc3" : "#ffabc9", "center", 900);
   451	  }
   452	  function drawTarget(m, t) {
   453	    const c = COLORS[t.color]; const center = { x: m.x + (t.x + .5) * m.cell, y: m.y + (t.y + .5) * m.cell };
   454	    const rad = m.cell * .31;
   455	    ctx.save(); ctx.globalAlpha = .28 + pulse * .35; ctx.shadowColor = c.glow; ctx.shadowBlur = 12;
   456	    ctx.strokeStyle = c.main; ctx.lineWidth = 2; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(center.x, center.y, rad, 0, TAU); ctx.stroke(); ctx.shadowBlur = 0;
   457	    ctx.globalAlpha = .8; ctx.fillStyle = c.deep; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(center.x, center.y, rad * .5, 0, TAU); ctx.fill(); ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
   458	    ctx.strokeStyle = c.glow; ctx.lineWidth = 1; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(center.x, center.y, rad * .68, -Math.PI * .75, Math.PI * .25); ctx.stroke();
   459	    ctx.restore();
   460	  }
   461	  function drawCrate(m, c) {
   462	    const p = { x: m.x + c.x * m.cell + 5, y: m.y + c.y * m.cell + 5 };
   463	    const size = m.cell - 10; const palette = COLORS[c.color];
   464	    ctx.save(); ctx.shadowColor = palette.main; ctx.shadowBlur = 9; roundRect(p.x, p.y, size, size, 7); ctx.fillStyle = palette.deep; ctx.fill(); ctx.shadowBlur = 0;
   465	    ctx.strokeStyle = palette.glow; ctx.lineWidth = 2; ctx.stroke();
   466	    line(p.x + 6, p.y + size - 6, p.x + size - 6, p.y + 6, palette.main, 1.4);
   467	    line(p.x + 6, p.y + 6, p.x + size - 6, p.y + size - 6, palette.main, 1.4);
   468	    ctx.fillStyle = palette.main; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(p.x + size / 2, p.y + size / 2, 3.2, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
   469	    ctx.restore();
   470	  }
   471	  function drawWorker(m, p) {
   472	    const c = { x: m.x + (p.x + .5) * m.cell, y: m.y + (p.y + .5) * m.cell };
   473	    const r = m.cell * .28;
   474	    ctx.save(); ctx.shadowColor = "#8be5ff"; ctx.shadowBlur = 10; ctx.fillStyle = "#d9f8ff"; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(c.x, c.y + 2, r, 0, TAU); ctx.fill(); ctx.shadowBlur = 0;
   475	    ctx.fillStyle = "#267e9b"; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(c.x, c.y - r * .35, r * 1.05, Math.PI, TAU); ctx.fill();
   476	    ctx.fillStyle = "#10364a"; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(c.x - r * .32, c.y + 1, 2, 0, TAU); ctx.arc(c.x + r * .32, c.y + 1, 2, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
   477	    ctx.restore();
   478	  }
   479	  function drawParticles() {
   480	    for (const p of particles) { ctx.globalAlpha = Math.max(0, p.life / p.max); ctx.fillStyle = p.color; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(p.x, p.y, p.size, 0, TAU); ctx.fill(); }
   481	    ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
   482	  }
   483	  function drawGameFooter() {
   484	    const l = levels[levelIndex];
   485	    text("SWIPE TO PUSH  •  Z UNDO", VW / 2, 535, 10, "#75a9b7", "center", 800);
   486	    text(`${play.moves} MOVES`, 24, 557, 11, "#dbeaf0", "left", 850);
   487	    text(`PAR ${l.par}`, 24, 577, 9, "#658390", "left", 800);

   534	  }
   535	  function hit(x, y, r) { return x >= r.x && x <= r.x + r.w && y >= r.y && y <= r.y + r.h; }
   536	  function handleClick(p) {
   537	    if (mode === "select") {
   538	      if (hit(p.x, p.y, { x: 296, y: 10, w: 80, h: 40 }) || hit(p.x, p.y, { x: 24, y: 636, w: 342, h: 48 })) { startLevel(levelIndex); return; }
   539	      for (let i = 0; i < levels.length; i++) if (hit(p.x, p.y, selectCardRect(i)) && i <= (progress.unlocked || 0)) { startLevel(i); return; }
   540	      return;
   541	    }
   542	    if (play.won) {
   543	      if (hit(p.x, p.y, { x: 50, y: 388, w: 136, h: 52 })) { resetLevel(); return; }
   544	      if (hit(p.x, p.y, { x: 204, y: 388, w: 136, h: 52 })) { levelIndex < levels.length - 1 ? startLevel(levelIndex + 1) : openLevels(); return; }
   545	      return;
   546	    }
   547	    if (hit(p.x, p.y, { x: 294, y: 10, w: 82, h: 40 })) { resetLevel(); return; }
   548	    if (hit(p.x, p.y, { x: 20, y: 596, w: 168, h: 62 })) { undo(); return; }
   549	    if (hit(p.x, p.y, { x: 202, y: 596, w: 170, h: 62 })) { openLevels(); return; }
   550	  }
   551	  function pointerDown(e) {
   552	    e.preventDefault(); const p = logicalPoint(e); pointer = { id: e.pointerId, x: p.x, y: p.y, sx: p.x, sy: p.y }; canvas.setPointerCapture?.(e.pointerId);
   553	  }
   554	  function pointerUp(e) {
   555	    e.preventDefault(); if (!pointer || pointer.id !== e.pointerId) return; const p = logicalPoint(e); const dx = p.x - pointer.sx; const dy = p.y - pointer.sy; const was = pointer; pointer = null;
   556	    if (mode === "game" && !play.won && (Math.abs(dx) > 24 || Math.abs(dy) > 24) && was.sy > 78 && was.sy < 590) {
   557	      if (Math.abs(dx) > Math.abs(dy)) tryMove(dx > 0 ? 1 : -1, 0); else tryMove(0, dy > 0 ? 1 : -1);
   558	    } else if (Math.abs(dx) < 16 && Math.abs(dy) < 16) handleClick(p);
   559	  }
   560	  function keyDown(e) {
   561	    const k = e.key.toLowerCase();
   562	    if (["arrowup", "arrowdown", "arrowleft", "arrowright", " "].includes(k)) e.preventDefault();
   563	    if (mode === "select") {
   564	      if (k === "escape" || k === "enter" || k === " ") startLevel(levelIndex);
   565	      return;
   566	    }
   567	    if (play.won) {
   568	      if (k === "enter" || k === " ") { levelIndex < levels.length - 1 ? startLevel(levelIndex + 1) : openLevels(); e.preventDefault(); }
   569	      if (k === "escape") openLevels();
   570	      return;
   571	    }
   572	    if (k === "arrowup" || k === "w") tryMove(0, -1);
   573	    else if (k === "arrowdown" || k === "s") tryMove(0, 1);
   574	    else if (k === "arrowleft" || k === "a") tryMove(-1, 0);
   575	    else if (k === "arrowright" || k === "d") tryMove(1, 0);
   576	    else if (k === "z") undo();
   577	    else if (k === "r") resetLevel();
   578	    else if (k === "escape") openLevels();
   579	  }
   580	  function loop(now) {
   581	    const dt = Math.min(.034, Math.max(0, (now - lastFrame) / 1000)); lastFrame = now; update(dt); render(); requestAnimationFrame(loop);
   582	  }
   583	
   584	  levels = Array.from({ length: 30 }, (_, i) => makeLevel(i));
   585	  progress.unlocked = Math.min(29, Math.max(0, progress.unlocked || 0));
   586	  startLevel(0);
   587	  resize();
   588	  window.addEventListener("resize", resize, { passive: true });
   589	  canvas.addEventListener("pointerdown", pointerDown, { passive: false });
   590	  canvas.addEventListener("pointerup", pointerUp, { passive: false });
   591	  canvas.addEventListener("pointercancel", () => { pointer = null; }, { passive: true });
   592	  canvas.addEventListener("touchmove", e => e.preventDefault(), { passive: false });
   593	  window.addEventListener("keydown", keyDown, { passive: false });
   594	  requestAnimationFrame(loop);
   595	})();

   160	  function movementVector() {
   161	    let x = 0, y = 0;
   162	    if (input.keys.a || input.keys.arrowleft) x -= 1;
   163	    if (input.keys.d || input.keys.arrowright) x += 1;
   164	    if (input.keys.w || input.keys.arrowup) y -= 1;
   165	    if (input.keys.s || input.keys.arrowdown) y += 1;
   166	    if (input.joy.active) { x += input.joy.x; y += input.joy.y; }
   167	    const m = Math.hypot(x, y);
   168	    return m > 1 ? { x: x / m, y: y / m, strength: 1 } : { x, y, strength: Math.min(1, m) };
   169	  }
   170	  function updatePlayer(dt) {
   171	    const p = game.player;
   172	    p.invuln = Math.max(0, p.invuln - dt);
   173	    p.dashCooldown = Math.max(0, p.dashCooldown - dt);
   174	    p.attackCooldown = Math.max(0, p.attackCooldown - dt);
   175	    p.attack = Math.max(0, p.attack - dt);
   176	    if (input.dash || game.dashQueued) {
   177	      game.dashQueued = false;
   178	      input.dash = false;
   179	      if (p.dashCooldown <= 0) {
   180	        const v = movementVector();
   181	        const dx = v.strength ? v.x : p.faceX;
   182	        const dy = v.strength ? v.y : p.faceY;
   183	        p.dash = .19;
   184	        p.dashCooldown = 1.05;
   185	        p.invuln = .23;
   186	        p.faceX = dx; p.faceY = dy;
   187	        burst(p.x, p.y, 178, 12);
   188	        game.shake = .22;
   189	      }
   190	    }
   191	    const v = movementVector();
   192	    if (v.strength > .12) { p.faceX = v.x; p.faceY = v.y; }
   193	    if (input.attack || game.attackQueued) {
   194	      game.attackQueued = false;
   195	      input.attack = false;
   196	      if (p.attackCooldown <= 0) {
   197	        p.attack = .17;
   198	        p.attackCooldown = .28;

   200	      }
   201	    }
   202	    const speed = p.dash > 0 ? 530 : 154;
   203	    const oldX = p.x, oldY = p.y;
   204	    p.x += v.x * speed * dt;
   205	    p.y += v.y * speed * dt;
   206	    if (p.dash > 0) p.dash -= dt;
   207	    p.x = clamp(p.x, arena.l + p.r, arena.r - p.r - 2);
   208	    p.y = clamp(p.y, arena.t + p.r, arena.b - p.r);
   209	    if (collidesObstacle(p.x, p.y, p.r)) { p.x = oldX; p.y = oldY; }

   211	      p.trail.push({ x: p.x, y: p.y, life: .28 });
   212	      if (p.trail.length > 8) p.trail.shift();
   213	    }
   214	    p.trail.forEach(t => t.life -= dt);
   215	    p.trail = p.trail.filter(t => t.life > 0);
   216	    if (game.key && !game.key.collected && distance(p, game.key) < 25) {
   217	      game.key.collected = true;
   218	      burst(game.key.x, game.key.y, 49, 20);
   219	      game.shake = .16;
   220	    }
   221	  }
   222	  function collidesObstacle(x, y, r) {
   223	    return game.rooms[game.roomIndex].obstacles.some(o => {
   224	      const nx = clamp(x, o.x, o.x + o.w), ny = clamp(y, o.y, o.y + o.h);
   225	      return Math.hypot(x - nx, y - ny) < r;
   226	    });
   227	  }

   229	  function doAttack() {
   230	    const p = game.player;
   231	    burst(p.x + p.faceX * 24, p.y + p.faceY * 24, 42, 4);
   232	    for (const e of game.enemies) {
   233	      const dx = e.x - p.x, dy = e.y - p.y, dist = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
   234	      const dot = (dx * p.faceX + dy * p.faceY) / dist;
   235	      if (dist < (e.type === 'warden' ? 62 : 55) && dot > -.12 && e.hitCooldown <= 0) {
   236	        e.hp -= 1;
   237	        e.hitCooldown = .18;
   238	        e.hurt = .18;
   239	        e.x += dx / dist * 16;
   240	        e.y += dy / dist * 16;
   241	        burst(e.x, e.y, e.type === 'warden' ? 322 : 24, 7);
   242	        game.shake = .1;
   243	        if (e.hp <= 0) {
   244	          burst(e.x, e.y, e.type === 'warden' ? 322 : 8, e.type === 'warden' ? 26 : 13);
   245	        }
   246	      }
   247	    }
   248	    game.enemies = game.enemies.filter(e => e.hp > 0);
   249	  }
