   251	  function updateEnemies(dt) {
   252	    const p = game.player;
   253	    for (const e of game.enemies) {
   254	      e.hurt = Math.max(0, e.hurt - dt);
   255	      e.hitCooldown = Math.max(0, e.hitCooldown - dt);
   256	      e.phase += dt * (e.type === 'skitter' ? 5 : 1.5);
   257	      let dx = p.x - e.x, dy = p.y - e.y, d = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
   258	      if (e.type === 'watcher') {
   259	        if (d > 134) { e.x += dx / d * 24 * dt; e.y += dy / d * 24 * dt; }
   260	        if (d < 220 && Math.sin(e.phase * .7) > .82 && e.shotClock !== 1) {
   261	          e.shotClock = 1;
   262	          game.bolts.push({ x: e.x, y: e.y, vx: dx / d * 112, vy: dy / d * 112, life: 2.6 });
   263	        }
   264	        if (Math.sin(e.phase * .7) < .72) e.shotClock = 0;
   265	      } else if (e.type === 'skitter') {
   266	        const sx = -dy / d * Math.sin(e.phase) * 34;
   267	        const sy = dx / d * Math.sin(e.phase) * 34;
   268	        e.x += (dx / d * 66 + sx) * dt;
   269	        e.y += (dy / d * 66 + sy) * dt;
   270	      } else if (e.type === 'warden') {
   271	        if (d > 116) { e.x += dx / d * 30 * dt; e.y += dy / d * 30 * dt; }
   272	        if (d < 270 && Math.sin(e.phase * .72) > .91 && e.shotClock !== 1) {
   273	          e.shotClock = 1;
   274	          for (let i = -1; i <= 1; i++) {
   275	            const a = Math.atan2(dy, dx) + i * .23;
   276	            game.bolts.push({ x: e.x, y: e.y, vx: Math.cos(a) * 136, vy: Math.sin(a) * 136, life: 2.4, heavy: true });
   277	          }
   278	        }
   279	        if (Math.sin(e.phase * .72) < .75) e.shotClock = 0;
   280	      } else {
   281	        e.x += dx / d * 47 * e.speed * dt;
   282	        e.y += dy / d * 47 * e.speed * dt;
   283	      }
   284	      e.x = clamp(e.x, arena.l + 18, arena.r - 22);
   285	      e.y = clamp(e.y, arena.t + 18, arena.b - 18);
   286	      if (d < p.r + (e.type === 'warden' ? 24 : 14)) hurtPlayer(e.x, e.y, e.type === 'warden' ? 1.3 : 1);
   287	    }
   288	  }
   289	  function updateBolts(dt) {
   290	    for (const b of game.bolts) {
   291	      b.x += b.vx * dt; b.y += b.vy * dt; b.life -= dt;
   292	      if (b.life > 0 && distance(b, game.player) < game.player.r + 8) {
   293	        b.life = 0;
   294	        hurtPlayer(b.x, b.y, b.heavy ? 1.1 : .8);
   295	        burst(b.x, b.y, 335, 5);
   296	      }
   297	    }
   298	    game.bolts = game.bolts.filter(b => b.life > 0 && b.x > -20 && b.x < W + 20 && b.y > -20 && b.y < H + 20);
   299	  }
   300	  function updateHazards(dt) {
   301	    const p = game.player;
   302	    for (const h of game.hazards) {
   303	      h.hitCooldown = Math.max(0, h.hitCooldown - dt);
   304	      h.active = h.timing ? Math.sin(game.roomTime * 2.8 + h.phase) > -.18 : true;
   305	      if (h.active && h.hitCooldown <= 0 && circleRect(p.x, p.y, p.r, h)) {
   306	        h.hitCooldown = .9;
   307	        hurtPlayer(h.x + h.w / 2, h.y + h.h / 2, 1.2);
   308	      }
   309	    }
   310	  }
   311	  function hurtPlayer(fromX, fromY, amount) {
   312	    const p = game.player;
   313	    if (p.invuln > 0 || game.mode !== 'run') return;
   314	    p.invuln = .65;
   315	    game.roomHits += 1;
   316	    game.roomFlash = .2;
   317	    game.runTime += 2;
   318	    game.roomPenalty += 2;
   319	    game.shake = .5;
   320	    const dx = p.x - fromX, dy = p.y - fromY, d = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
   321	    p.x = clamp(p.x + dx / d * 28, arena.l + p.r, arena.r - p.r - 2);
   322	    p.y = clamp(p.y + dy / d * 28, arena.t + p.r, arena.b - p.r);
   323	    burst(p.x, p.y, 347, 13);
   324	    if (game.roomHits >= 3) restartRoom();
   325	  }
   326	  function restartRoom() {
   327	    game.roomPenalty = 0;
   328	    game.runTime += 10;
   329	    game.roomFlash = .7;
   330	    game.transitionText = `ROOM ${pad(game.roomIndex + 1)} RESET  +10s`;
   331	    loadRoom(game.roomIndex, false);
   332	    game.transition = 1.05;
   333	  }
   334	  function advanceRoom() {
   335	    if (game.roomIndex >= game.rooms.length - 1) {
   336	      game.mode = 'done';
   337	      game.completed = true;
   338	      game.won = true;
   339	      const key = `${game.type}:${game.seed}`;
   340	      const previousBest = bests[key];
   341	      game.newBest = previousBest == null || game.runTime < previousBest;
   342	      game.best = previousBest == null ? game.runTime : Math.min(previousBest, game.runTime);
   343	      bests[key] = game.best;
   344	      saveBest();
   345	      burst(314, 368, 168, 42);
   346	      return;
   347	    }
   348	    game.roomIndex += 1;
   349	    loadRoom(game.roomIndex, false);
   350	  }
   351	
   352	  function updateParticles(dt) {
   353	    for (const p of game.particles) { p.x += p.vx * dt; p.y += p.vy * dt; p.vx *= .966; p.vy *= .966; p.life -= dt; }
   354	    game.particles = game.particles.filter(p => p.life > 0);
   355	  }
   356	  function burst(x, y, hue, count) {
   357	    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
   358	      const a = Math.random() * TAU, s = 18 + Math.random() * 90;
   359	      game.particles.push({ x, y, vx: Math.cos(a) * s, vy: Math.sin(a) * s, life: .3 + Math.random() * .45, max: .75, size: 1 + Math.random() * 3, hue });
   360	    }
   361	  }
   362	
   363	  function draw() {
   364	    ctx.setTransform(canvas.width / W, 0, 0, canvas.height / H, 0, 0);
   365	    const sh = game.shake * 7;
   366	    ctx.fillStyle = '#080c16'; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
   367	    ctx.save();
   368	    if (sh) ctx.translate((Math.random() - .5) * sh, (Math.random() - .5) * sh);
   369	    drawBackdrop();
   370	    drawHud();
   371	    drawRoom();
   372	    drawEntities();
   373	    drawParticles();
   374	    drawControls();
   375	    ctx.restore();
   376	    if (game.mode === 'done') drawDone();
   377	    if (game.transition > 0 && game.mode === 'run') drawTransition();
   378	  }
   379	  function drawBackdrop() {
   380	    const g = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, 0, 0, H);
   381	    g.addColorStop(0, '#0b1222'); g.addColorStop(.52, '#111a2d'); g.addColorStop(1, '#080c16');
   382	    ctx.fillStyle = g; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
   383	    ctx.strokeStyle = '#18243a'; ctx.lineWidth = 1;
   384	    for (let x = 12; x < W; x += 24) { ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(x, 90); ctx.lineTo(x, H); ctx.stroke(); }
   385	    for (let y = 100; y < H; y += 24) { ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(0, y); ctx.lineTo(W, y); ctx.stroke(); }
   386	    ctx.fillStyle = '#0e1728'; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, 78);
   387	    ctx.strokeStyle = '#283756'; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(0, 78); ctx.lineTo(W, 78); ctx.stroke();
   388	  }
   389	  function drawHud() {
   390	    const room = game.rooms[game.roomIndex];
   391	    ctx.fillStyle = '#92a5c8'; ctx.font = '700 10px ui-sans-serif, system-ui'; ctx.letterSpacing = '1px';
   392	    ctx.fillText(`${game.type === 'daily' ? 'DAILY' : 'PRACTICE'}  /  SEED ${game.seedLabel}`, 15, 18);
   393	    ctx.fillStyle = '#f3f7ff'; ctx.font = '800 23px ui-monospace, SFMono-Regular, Menlo, monospace'; ctx.letterSpacing = '0px';
   394	    ctx.fillText(formatTime(game.runTime), 14, 48);
   395	    ctx.fillStyle = '#92a5c8'; ctx.font = '700 10px ui-sans-serif, system-ui';
   396	    ctx.fillText(`BEST ${game.best == null ? '--:--.--' : formatTime(game.best)}`, 15, 65);
   397	    pill(210, 10, 58, 29, `R${pad(game.roomIndex + 1)}`, '#17243c', '#b9c9e4');
   398	    pill(274, 10, 72, 29, 'PRACTICE', '#17243c', '#93f2d6');
   399	    for (let i = 0; i < 10; i++) {
   400	      ctx.fillStyle = i < game.roomIndex ? '#4ae2bd' : (i === game.roomIndex ? '#ffd36b' : '#263754');
   401	      ctx.fillRect(15 + i * 26, 82, 20, 3);
   402	    }
   403	    ctx.fillStyle = '#b9c9e4'; ctx.font = '800 11px ui-sans-serif, system-ui';
   404	    ctx.fillText(`${roomLabel(room)}  ·  SPLIT ${formatTime(game.roomTime)}`, 15, 104);
   405	    if (game.roomPenalty > 0) { ctx.fillStyle = '#ff7582'; ctx.fillText(`+${game.roomPenalty.toFixed(0)}s`, 286, 104); }
   406	  }
   407	  function pill(x, y, w, h, text, fill, color) {
   408	    ctx.fillStyle = fill; roundRect(x, y, w, h, 9); ctx.fill();
   409	    ctx.strokeStyle = '#304363'; ctx.stroke();
   410	    ctx.fillStyle = color; ctx.font = '800 9px ui-sans-serif, system-ui'; ctx.textAlign = 'center'; ctx.fillText(text, x + w / 2, y + 19); ctx.textAlign = 'left';
   411	  }
   412	  function drawRoom() {
   413	    const room = game.rooms[game.roomIndex];
   414	    ctx.fillStyle = '#0c1525'; roundRect(arena.l, arena.t + 12, arena.r - arena.l, arena.b - arena.t - 12, 14); ctx.fill();
   415	    ctx.strokeStyle = `hsla(${room.hue}, 70%, 64%, .35)`; ctx.lineWidth = 2; ctx.stroke();
   416	    ctx.fillStyle = `hsla(${room.hue}, 70%, 64%, .1)`; ctx.fillRect(arena.l + 4, arena.t + 16, 6, arena.b - arena.t - 20);
   417	    for (const o of room.obstacles) {
   418	      ctx.fillStyle = '#192941'; roundRect(o.x, o.y, o.w, o.h, 7); ctx.fill();
   419	      ctx.strokeStyle = '#2c4362'; ctx.stroke();
   420	      ctx.fillStyle = '#315073'; ctx.fillRect(o.x + 5, o.y + 7, 3, Math.max(4, o.h - 14));
   421	    }
   422	    for (const h of game.hazards) drawHazard(h);
   423	    drawGate();
   424	    if (game.key && !game.key.collected) drawKey(game.key);
   425	  }
   426	  function drawHazard(h) {
   427	    ctx.save(); ctx.globalAlpha = h.active ? 1 : .22;
   428	    ctx.fillStyle = h.active ? '#d74d75' : '#532d50'; roundRect(h.x, h.y, h.w, h.h, 5); ctx.fill();
   429	    ctx.strokeStyle = h.active ? '#ff8a7f' : '#8d516d'; ctx.lineWidth = 1;
   430	    const n = h.w > h.h ? 5 : 4;
   431	    for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
   432	      const px = h.w > h.h ? h.x + 7 + i * (h.w - 14) / Math.max(1, n - 1) : h.x + h.w / 2;
   433	      const py = h.w > h.h ? h.y + h.h / 2 : h.y + 7 + i * (h.h - 14) / Math.max(1, n - 1);
   434	      ctx.beginPath();
   435	      if (h.w > h.h) { ctx.moveTo(px - 5, py + 5); ctx.lineTo(px, py - 5); ctx.lineTo(px + 5, py + 5); }
   436	      else { ctx.moveTo(px - 5, py - 5); ctx.lineTo(px + 5, py); ctx.lineTo(px - 5, py + 5); }
   437	      ctx.stroke();
   438	    }
   439	    if (h.timing) { ctx.fillStyle = '#ffd36b'; ctx.globalAlpha *= .7; ctx.fillRect(h.x, h.y - 4, h.w, 2); }
   440	    ctx.restore();
   441	  }
   442	  function drawGate() {
   443	    const g = game.gate;
   444	    ctx.save(); ctx.globalAlpha = g.open ? .98 : .38;
   445	    ctx.fillStyle = g.open ? '#41e2b0' : '#5e708f'; roundRect(g.x, g.y, g.w, g.h, 7); ctx.fill();
   446	    ctx.fillStyle = g.open ? '#d8fff4' : '#a5b3c8'; ctx.fillRect(g.x + 6, g.y + 10, 3, g.h - 20); ctx.fillRect(g.x + 13, g.y + 10, 3, g.h - 20);
   447	    ctx.strokeStyle = g.open ? '#b7ffe8' : '#8493aa'; ctx.stroke();
   448	    if (g.open) { ctx.fillStyle = '#9dffe0'; ctx.font = '800 9px ui-sans-serif, system-ui'; ctx.textAlign = 'center'; ctx.fillText('GO', g.x + 11, g.y - 7); ctx.textAlign = 'left'; }
   449	    ctx.restore();
   450	  }
   451	  function drawKey(k) {
   452	    const pulse = 1 + Math.sin(performance.now() / 180) * .12;
   453	    ctx.save(); ctx.translate(k.x, k.y); ctx.rotate(-.25); ctx.scale(pulse, pulse);
   454	    ctx.shadowColor = '#ffd36b'; ctx.shadowBlur = 16; ctx.strokeStyle = '#ffe69a'; ctx.lineWidth = 4;
   455	    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(-5, 0, 7, 0, TAU); ctx.stroke(); ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(2, 0); ctx.lineTo(16, 0); ctx.lineTo(16, 5); ctx.moveTo(10, 0); ctx.lineTo(10, 5); ctx.stroke();
   456	    ctx.restore();
   457	    ctx.fillStyle = '#ffd36b'; ctx.font = '800 9px ui-sans-serif, system-ui'; ctx.fillText('KEY', k.x - 12, k.y - 17);
   458	  }
   459	  function drawEntities() {
   460	    for (const b of game.bolts) {
   461	      ctx.fillStyle = b.heavy ? '#ff8b8b' : '#f79cf4'; ctx.shadowColor = ctx.fillStyle; ctx.shadowBlur = 12;
   462	      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(b.x, b.y, b.heavy ? 6 : 4, 0, TAU); ctx.fill(); ctx.shadowBlur = 0;
   463	    }
   464	    for (const e of game.enemies) drawEnemy(e);
   465	    const p = game.player;
   466	    for (const t of p.trail) { ctx.fillStyle = `hsla(167, 84%, 70%, ${t.life * .55})`; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(t.x, t.y, 6 * t.life, 0, TAU); ctx.fill(); }
   467	    if (p.attack > 0) {
   468	      ctx.save(); ctx.translate(p.x, p.y); ctx.rotate(Math.atan2(p.faceY, p.faceX));
   469	      ctx.strokeStyle = '#d9fff5'; ctx.shadowColor = '#45e1bb'; ctx.shadowBlur = 18; ctx.lineWidth = 7; ctx.lineCap = 'round';
   470	      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(4, 0, 29, -.78, .78); ctx.stroke(); ctx.restore();
   471	    }
   472	    ctx.save(); ctx.globalAlpha = p.invuln > 0 && Math.floor(p.invuln * 18) % 2 === 0 ? .4 : 1;
   473	    ctx.fillStyle = '#e9fff9'; ctx.shadowColor = '#43e0b8'; ctx.shadowBlur = p.dash > 0 ? 23 : 10;
   474	    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(p.x, p.y, p.r, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
   475	    ctx.fillStyle = '#1a3945'; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(p.x + p.faceX * 4, p.y + p.faceY * 4, 3, 0, TAU); ctx.fill(); ctx.restore();
   476	    if (game.roomHits > 0) {
   477	      ctx.fillStyle = '#8da1bf'; ctx.font = '800 9px ui-sans-serif, system-ui'; ctx.fillText(`IMPACT ${Math.floor(game.roomHits)}/3`, 27, 119);
   478	      for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) { ctx.fillStyle = i < game.roomHits ? '#ff6e7e' : '#31415b'; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(102 + i * 11, 116, 3, 0, TAU); ctx.fill(); }
   479	    }
   480	  }
   481	  function drawEnemy(e) {
   482	    ctx.save(); ctx.translate(e.x, e.y); ctx.globalAlpha = e.hurt > 0 ? .45 : 1;
   483	    const boss = e.type === 'warden';
   484	    const color = boss ? '#c183ff' : e.type === 'watcher' ? '#ffbd6f' : e.type === 'skitter' ? '#ff718a' : '#f35d76';
   485	    ctx.shadowColor = color; ctx.shadowBlur = boss ? 20 : 10; ctx.fillStyle = color;
   486	    if (boss) {
   487	      ctx.rotate(Math.sin(e.phase) * .05); ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(0, 0, 27, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
   488	      ctx.fillStyle = '#211a38'; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(0, 0, 16, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
   489	      ctx.strokeStyle = '#f0d6ff'; ctx.lineWidth = 3; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(0, 0, 21, e.phase, e.phase + 4.8); ctx.stroke();
   490	      ctx.fillStyle = '#ffd36b'; ctx.font = '800 9px ui-sans-serif, system-ui'; ctx.textAlign = 'center'; ctx.fillText('WARDEN', 0, -38); ctx.textAlign = 'left';
   491	      ctx.fillStyle = '#2e2046'; roundRect(-34, 32, 68, 5, 2); ctx.fill(); ctx.fillStyle = '#c183ff'; ctx.fillRect(-34, 32, 68 * Math.max(0, e.hp / e.maxHp), 5);
   492	    } else if (e.type === 'watcher') {
   493	      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(0, 0, 14, 0, TAU); ctx.fill(); ctx.fillStyle = '#382b23'; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(0, 0, 6, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
   494	      ctx.strokeStyle = '#ffe0a8'; ctx.lineWidth = 2; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(-17, 0); ctx.lineTo(17, 0); ctx.stroke();
   495	    } else if (e.type === 'skitter') {
   496	      ctx.rotate(e.phase * .4); ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(0, -15); ctx.lineTo(12, 12); ctx.lineTo(-12, 12); ctx.closePath(); ctx.fill();
   497	      ctx.fillStyle = '#401c32'; ctx.fillRect(-3, 3, 6, 5);
   498	    } else {
   499	      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(0, 0, 14, 0, TAU); ctx.fill(); ctx.fillStyle = '#421b35'; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(4, -3, 4, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
   500	      ctx.strokeStyle = '#ffb0a4'; ctx.lineWidth = 2; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(-13, 9); ctx.lineTo(-20, 15); ctx.moveTo(13, 9); ctx.lineTo(20, 15); ctx.stroke();
   501	    }
   502	    ctx.restore();
   503	  }
   504	  function drawParticles() {
   505	    for (const p of game.particles) { ctx.fillStyle = `hsla(${p.hue}, 90%, 70%, ${Math.min(1, p.life * 2.2)})`; ctx.fillRect(p.x, p.y, p.size, p.size); }
   506	  }
   507	  function drawControls() {
   508	    const j = input.joy;
   509	    ctx.save(); ctx.globalAlpha = .8;
   510	    ctx.fillStyle = '#13233a'; ctx.strokeStyle = '#46607c'; ctx.lineWidth = 2; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(j.cx, j.cy, 38, 0, TAU); ctx.fill(); ctx.stroke();
   511	    ctx.fillStyle = '#6d8ba5'; ctx.globalAlpha = .66; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(j.cx + j.x * 17, j.cy + j.y * 17, 17, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
   512	    controlButton(224, 523, 64, 64, '#253657', '#f5c8d0', 'ATTACK');
   513	    controlButton(291, 449, 52, 52, '#273652', '#93f2d6', 'DASH');
   514	    ctx.globalAlpha = 1; ctx.fillStyle = '#8096b4'; ctx.font = '700 8px ui-sans-serif, system-ui'; ctx.textAlign = 'center'; ctx.fillText('MOVE', j.cx, j.cy + 3); ctx.textAlign = 'left';
   515	    ctx.restore();
   516	  }
   517	  function controlButton(x, y, w, h, fill, color, label) {
   518	    ctx.fillStyle = fill; roundRect(x, y, w, h, 17); ctx.fill(); ctx.strokeStyle = '#527091'; ctx.lineWidth = 2; ctx.stroke();
   519	    ctx.fillStyle = color; ctx.font = `900 ${label === 'ATTACK' ? 9 : 8}px ui-sans-serif, system-ui`; ctx.textAlign = 'center'; ctx.fillText(label, x + w / 2, y + h / 2 + 3); ctx.textAlign = 'left';
   520	    if (label === 'DASH' && game.player && game.player.dashCooldown > 0) { ctx.fillStyle = '#0b1222aa'; roundRect(x, y, w, h * Math.min(1, game.player.dashCooldown / 1.05), 17); ctx.fill(); }
   521	  }
   522	  function drawTransition() {
   523	    ctx.fillStyle = `rgba(6,10,18,${Math.min(.8, game.transition * .72)})`; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
   524	    ctx.fillStyle = '#eaf3ff'; ctx.font = '900 30px ui-sans-serif, system-ui'; ctx.textAlign = 'center'; ctx.fillText(game.transitionText, W / 2, 303);
   525	    ctx.fillStyle = '#8ea4c8'; ctx.font = '700 11px ui-sans-serif, system-ui'; ctx.fillText('CLEAR THE ROOM · REACH THE GREEN GATE', W / 2, 327); ctx.textAlign = 'left';
   526	  }
   527	  function drawDone() {
   528	    ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(5, 9, 17, .84)'; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
   529	    ctx.fillStyle = '#44e0b6'; ctx.font = '900 13px ui-sans-serif, system-ui'; ctx.textAlign = 'center'; ctx.fillText('GAUNTLET CLEARED', W / 2, 170);
   530	    ctx.fillStyle = '#f1f7ff'; ctx.font = '900 37px ui-monospace, monospace'; ctx.fillText(formatTime(game.runTime), W / 2, 220);
   531	    ctx.fillStyle = '#8fa4c4'; ctx.font = '700 11px ui-sans-serif, system-ui'; ctx.fillText(game.newBest ? 'NEW SEED BEST' : `BEST  ${formatTime(game.best)}`, W / 2, 244);
   532	    resultButton(49, 290, 262, 54, 'RUN DAILY', '#1d3b50', '#a7ffea');
   533	    resultButton(49, 356, 262, 54, 'NEW PRACTICE SEED', '#30274d', '#dfc5ff');
   534	    ctx.fillStyle = '#627795'; ctx.font = '700 10px ui-sans-serif, system-ui'; ctx.fillText('TAP A BUTTON  ·  PRESS D OR P', W / 2, 450);
   535	    ctx.textAlign = 'left';
   536	  }
   537	  function resultButton(x, y, w, h, label, fill, color) {
   538	    ctx.fillStyle = fill; roundRect(x, y, w, h, 14); ctx.fill(); ctx.strokeStyle = '#4a6380'; ctx.stroke();
   539	    ctx.fillStyle = color; ctx.font = '900 12px ui-sans-serif, system-ui'; ctx.textAlign = 'center'; ctx.fillText(label, x + w / 2, y + 33); ctx.textAlign = 'left';
   540	  }
   541	
   542	  function roundRect(x, y, w, h, r) {
   543	    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(x + r, y); ctx.arcTo(x + w, y, x + w, y + h, r); ctx.arcTo(x + w, y + h, x, y + h, r); ctx.arcTo(x, y + h, x, y, r); ctx.arcTo(x, y, x + w, y, r); ctx.closePath();
   544	  }
   545	  function clamp(n, a, b) { return Math.max(a, Math.min(b, n)); }
   546	  function distance(a, b) { return Math.hypot(a.x - b.x, a.y - b.y); }
   547	  function circleRect(x, y, r, q) { const nx = clamp(x, q.x, q.x + q.w), ny = clamp(y, q.y, q.y + q.h); return Math.hypot(x - nx, y - ny) < r; }
   548	  function canvasPoint(ev) {
   549	    const rect = canvas.getBoundingClientRect();
   550	    return { x: (ev.clientX - rect.left) * W / rect.width, y: (ev.clientY - rect.top) * H / rect.height };
   551	  }
   552	  function pressAt(x, y, id) {
   553	    input.pointer.x = x; input.pointer.y = y;
   554	    if (game.mode === 'done') {
   555	      if (x >= 40 && x <= 320 && y >= 278 && y <= 350) { resetRun('daily', dailySeed()); return; }
   556	      if (x >= 40 && x <= 320 && y >= 350 && y <= 428) { resetRun('practice'); return; }
   557	    }
   558	    if (y < 55 && x > 265) { resetRun('practice'); return; }
   559	    if (x < 142 && y > 505) { input.joy.active = true; input.joy.id = id; updateJoy(x, y); return; }
   560	    if (x > 210 && y > 500) { game.attackQueued = true; return; }
   561	    if (x > 270 && y > 425 && y < 515) { game.dashQueued = true; return; }
   562	  }
   563	  function updateJoy(x, y) {
   564	    const dx = x - input.joy.cx, dy = y - input.joy.cy, d = Math.hypot(dx, dy), max = 31;
   565	    const s = d > max ? max / d : 1;
   566	    input.joy.x = dx * s / max; input.joy.y = dy * s / max;
   567	  }
   568	  function releasePointer(id) {
   569	    if (input.joy.id === id) { input.joy.active = false; input.joy.id = null; input.joy.x = 0; input.joy.y = 0; }
   570	  }
   571	
   572	  canvas.addEventListener('pointerdown', ev => { ev.preventDefault(); canvas.setPointerCapture?.(ev.pointerId); const q = canvasPoint(ev); pressAt(q.x, q.y, ev.pointerId); }, { passive: false });
   573	  canvas.addEventListener('pointermove', ev => { if (input.joy.id === ev.pointerId) { ev.preventDefault(); const q = canvasPoint(ev); updateJoy(q.x, q.y); } }, { passive: false });
   574	  canvas.addEventListener('pointerup', ev => { ev.preventDefault(); releasePointer(ev.pointerId); }, { passive: false });
   575	  canvas.addEventListener('pointercancel', ev => releasePointer(ev.pointerId));
   576	  canvas.addEventListener('contextmenu', ev => ev.preventDefault());
   577	  window.addEventListener('keydown', ev => {
   578	    const k = ev.key.toLowerCase();
   579	    if (['arrowup', 'arrowdown', 'arrowleft', 'arrowright', ' ', 'shift', 'enter'].includes(k)) ev.preventDefault();
   580	    if (k === 'p') { resetRun('practice'); return; }
   581	    if (k === 'd' && game.mode === 'done') { resetRun('daily', dailySeed()); return; }
   582	    input.keys[k] = true;
   583	    if ((k === ' ' || k === 'enter') && game.mode === 'run') game.attackQueued = true;
   584	    if (k === 'shift' && game.mode === 'run') game.dashQueued = true;
   585	    if (k === 'r' && game.mode === 'run') restartRoom();
   586	  });
   587	  window.addEventListener('keyup', ev => { input.keys[ev.key.toLowerCase()] = false; });
   588	  window.addEventListener('blur', () => { input.keys = Object.create(null); input.joy.active = false; input.joy.x = 0; input.joy.y = 0; });
   589	  function resize() {
   590	    const rect = canvas.getBoundingClientRect();
   591	    dpr = Math.min(window.devicePixelRatio || 1, 2);
   592	    const longAxis = Math.max(rect.width, rect.height) * dpr;
   593	    const cap = longAxis > 960 ? 960 / longAxis : 1;
   594	    canvas.width = Math.max(1, Math.floor(rect.width * dpr * cap));
   595	    canvas.height = Math.max(1, Math.floor(rect.height * dpr * cap));
   596	    scaleX = rect.width / W; scaleY = rect.height / H;
   597	  }
   598	  window.addEventListener('resize', resize);
   599	  resetRun('daily', dailySeed());
   600	  resize();
   601	  function frame(now) { const dt = game.last ? (now - game.last) / 1000 : 0; game.last = now; update(dt); draw(); requestAnimationFrame(frame); }
   602	  requestAnimationFrame(frame);

     5	  var TAU = Math.PI * 2;
     6	  function clamp(v, a, b) { return v < a ? a : (v > b ? b : v); }
     7	  function lerp(a, b, t) { return a + (b - a) * t; }
     8	
     9	  function mulberry32(a) {
    10	    return function () {
    11	      a |= 0; a = a + 0x6D2B79F5 | 0;
    12	      var t = Math.imul(a ^ a >>> 15, 1 | a);
    13	      t = t + Math.imul(t ^ t >>> 7, 61 | t) ^ t;
    14	      return ((t ^ t >>> 14) >>> 0) / 4294967296;
    15	    };
    16	  }
    17	  function rngObj(seed) {
    18	    var f = mulberry32(seed >>> 0);
    19	    return {
    20	      r: f,
    21	      range: function (a, b) { return a + f() * (b - a); },
    22	      int: function (a, b) { return Math.floor(a + f() * (b - a + 1)); },
    23	      pick: function (arr) { return arr[Math.floor(f() * arr.length)]; },
    24	      chance: function (p) { return f() < p; }
    25	    };
    26	  }

   159	    for (var i = 0; i < n; i++) {

   169	  function updateParts(dt) {
   170	    for (var i = parts.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {

   175	      if (p.life <= 0) parts.splice(i, 1);

   186	    }

   100	    G.pb.push({ x: x, y: y, vx: vx, vy: vy, d: d });
   101	  }
   102	  function mult() { return 1 + Math.min(7, G.meter * 0.08); }
   103	  function addScore(v) { G.score += Math.round(v); }
   104	
   105	  function sfx(name) {
   106	    if (soundCd > 0 && (name === 'shot' || name === 'hit')) return;
   107	    if (name === 'shot') { SH.tone(880, 0.045, 'square', 0.035, 620); soundCd = 0.05; }
   108	    else if (name === 'hit') { SH.tone(320, 0.04, 'square', 0.05, 200); soundCd = 0.03; }
   109	    else if (name === 'boom') { SH.noise(0.32, 0.4, 1800); SH.tone(160, 0.28, 'sawtooth', 0.14, 50); }
   110	    else if (name === 'death') { SH.noise(0.6, 0.5, 900); SH.tone(220, 0.6, 'sawtooth', 0.2, 40); }
   111	    else if (name === 'bomb') { SH.noise(0.8, 0.5, 2600); SH.tone(90, 0.7, 'sawtooth', 0.22, 35); }
   112	    else if (name === 'graze') { SH.tone(1650, 0.03, 'sine', 0.03); }
   113	    else if (name === 'warn') { SH.tone(440, 0.18, 'square', 0.09, 660); }
   114	    else if (name === 'clear') { SH.tone(523, 0.14, 'triangle', 0.16); SH.tone(784, 0.3, 'triangle', 0.14); }
   115	  }
   116	
   117	  function useBomb() {
   118	    if (G.state !== 'play' || G.bombs <= 0 || !G.p.alive) return;
   119	    G.bombs--;
   120	    G.bombFx = 0.75;
   121	    G.p.inv = Math.max(G.p.inv, 1.8);
   122	    shake = Math.max(shake, 12); shakeT = 0.5; flash = 0.7;
   123	    sfx('bomb');
   124	    for (var i = 0; i < G.eb.length; i++) {
   125	      var b = G.eb[i];
   126	      addScore(20 * mult());
   127	      if (i % 3 === 0) SH.spawnParts(b.x, b.y, 1, b.c, 70, 3, 0.35);
   128	    }
   129	    G.eb.length = 0;
   130	    for (var j = G.en.length - 1; j >= 0; j--) damageEnemy(G.en[j], 42, j);
   131	    if (G.boss && G.boss.state === 'fight') {
   132	      G.boss.hp -= 90;
   133	      for (var k = 0; k < G.boss.pods.length; k++) if (G.boss.pods[k].hp > 0) G.boss.pods[k].hp -= 60;
   134	      SH.spawnParts(G.boss.x, G.boss.y, 14, '#ffffff', 130, 3, 0.5);
   135	    }
   136	  }
   137	
   138	  /* ---------------- patterns ---------------- */
   139	  function aimAt(x, y) { return Math.atan2(G.p.y - y, G.p.x - x); }
   140	
   141	  function runPattern(o, pat, dt) {
   142	    if (!pat) return;
   143	    if (o.sa === undefined) { o.sa = Math.random() * TAU; o.ft = pat.period * 0.5; o.bl = 0; o.bt = 0; o.wr = SH.rngObj(pat.seed || 7); }
   144	    var k = pat.kind;
   145	    if (k === 'spiral' || k === 'arms') {
   146	      o.sa += pat.rate * dt;
   147	      o.ft -= dt;
   148	      if (o.ft <= 0) {
   149	        o.ft += pat.period;
   150	        var arms = pat.arms;
   151	        for (var i = 0; i < arms; i++) {
   152	          var a = o.sa + i * TAU / arms;
   153	          if (pat.sweep) a += Math.sin(o.sa * 0.5) * 0.6;
   154	          eb(o.x, o.y, a, pat.speed, pat.r, pat.c);
   155	        }
   156	      }
   157	      return;
   158	    }
   159	    o.ft -= dt;
   160	    if (o.ft <= 0) {
   161	      o.ft += pat.period;
   162	      if (k === 'aimed') { o.bl = pat.burst || 1; o.bt = 0; }
   163	      else if (k === 'fan') {
   164	        var base = aimAt(o.x, o.y), n = pat.n;
   165	        for (var f = 0; f < n; f++) {
   166	          var ang = base + (f / (n - 1) - 0.5) * pat.spread;
   167	          eb(o.x, o.y, ang, pat.speed, pat.r, pat.c);
   168	        }
   169	      } else if (k === 'ring') {
   170	        var off = pat.aim ? aimAt(o.x, o.y) : 0;
   171	        o.sa = (o.sa || 0) + (pat.spin || 0);
   172	        for (var g = 0; g < pat.n; g++) {
   173	          eb(o.x, o.y, off + o.sa + g * TAU / pat.n, pat.speed, pat.r, pat.c);
   174	        }
   175	      } else if (k === 'wall') {
   176	        var gc = 40 + o.wr.r() * (W() - 80);
   177	        var step = W() / pat.count;
   178	        for (var w = 0; w < pat.count; w++) {
   179	          var wx = step * 0.5 + w * step;
   180	          if (Math.abs(wx - gc) < pat.gap * 0.5) continue;
   181	          eb(wx, o.y, Math.PI / 2, pat.speed, pat.r, pat.c);
   182	        }
   183	      } else if (k === 'rain') {
   184	        var rx = 16 + o.wr.r() * (W() - 32);
   185	        eb(rx, -8, Math.PI / 2 + (o.wr.r() - 0.5) * 0.5, pat.speed, pat.r, pat.c);
   186	      }
   187	    }
   188	    if (o.bl > 0) {
   189	      o.bt -= dt;
   190	      if (o.bt <= 0) {
   191	        o.bt = 0.1;
   192	        o.bl--;
   193	        var b0 = aimAt(o.x, o.y), nn = pat.n;
   194	        for (var q = 0; q < nn; q++) {
   195	          var aa = b0 + (nn === 1 ? 0 : (q / (nn - 1) - 0.5) * pat.spread * nn * 0.5);
   196	          eb(o.x, o.y, aa, pat.speed, pat.r, pat.c);
   197	        }
   198	      }
   199	    }
   200	  }
   201	
   202	  /* ---------------- spawning ---------------- */
   203	  function spawnWave(wv) {
   204	    for (var i = 0; i < wv.list.length; i++) {
   205	      var d = wv.list[i];
   206	      G.en.push({
   207	        x: d.x, y: d.y, tx: d.tx, ty: d.ty, speed: d.speed, hold: d.hold,
   208	        ovx: d.ovx, ovy: d.ovy, hp: d.hp, maxhp: d.hp, r: d.r, kind: d.kind,
   209	        color: d.color, pat: d.pat, score: d.score, sway: d.sway,
   210	        st: 'wait', wait: d.delay, life: 0, flash: 0, rot: Math.random() * TAU
   211	      });
   212	    }
   213	  }
   214	
   215	  function spawnBoss() {
   216	    var bs = G.stage.boss;
   217	    G.boss = {
   218	      name: bs.name, def: bs, phaseIdx: 0,
   219	      hp: bs.phases[0].hp, maxhp: bs.phases[0].hp,
   220	      x: W() * 0.5, y: -70, t: 0, state: 'enter',
   221	      moveIdx: 0, moveT: 0, flash: 0,
   222	      pods: [], sub: {}, ph: 0
   223	    };
   224	    makePods();
   225	  }
   226	  function makePods() {
   227	    var ph = G.boss.def.phases[G.boss.phaseIdx];
   228	    G.boss.pods = [];
   229	    for (var i = 0; i < ph.pods; i++) {
   230	      G.boss.pods.push({ ox: (i === 0 ? -48 : 48), oy: 8, hp: ph.podHP, maxhp: ph.podHP, r: 13, flash: 0, o: {} });
   231	    }
   232	  }
   233	
   234	  /* ---------------- damage ---------------- */
   235	  function damageEnemy(e, d, idx) {
   236	    e.hp -= d; e.flash = 0.09;
   237	    if (e.hp <= 0) {
   238	      addScore(e.score * mult());
   239	      SH.spawnParts(e.x, e.y, 14, e.color, 150, 3.5, 0.55);
   240	      SH.spawnParts(e.x, e.y, 6, '#ffffff', 90, 2.5, 0.3);
   241	      shake = Math.max(shake, 4); shakeT = Math.max(shakeT, 0.16);
   242	      sfx('boom');
   243	      G.en.splice(idx === undefined ? G.en.indexOf(e) : idx, 1);
   244	    }
   245	  }
   246	
   247	  function playerHit() {
   248	    var p = G.p;
   249	    if (p.inv > 0 || !p.alive || G.state !== 'play') return;
   250	    G.lives--;
   251	    G.meter = 0;
   252	    p.inv = 2.6; p.hurt = 0.5;
   253	    shake = 16; shakeT = 0.6; flash = 0.5; hitFlash = 0.6;
   254	    sfx('death');
   255	    SH.spawnParts(p.x, p.y, 30, '#7ef9ff', 190, 4, 0.7);
   256	    SH.spawnParts(p.x, p.y, 16, '#ffffff', 120, 3, 0.5);
   257	    G.eb.length = 0;
   258	    G.bombs = 3;
   259	    p.x = W() * 0.5; p.y = H() - 120;
   260	    G.driveId = null;
   261	    if (G.lives <= 0) {
   262	      G.state = 'over'; G.overT = 0;
   263	      if (G.score > best) { best = G.score; saveBest(best); }
   264	    }
   265	  }
   266	
   267	  /* ---------------- update ---------------- */
   268	  function update(dt) {
   269	    G.t += dt;
   270	    if (soundCd > 0) soundCd -= dt;
   271	    if (grazeCd > 0) grazeCd -= dt;
   272	    if (shakeT > 0) { shakeT -= dt; if (shakeT <= 0) shake = 0; } else shake *= Math.pow(0.02, dt);
   273	    flash = Math.max(0, flash - dt * 2.2);
   274	    hitFlash = Math.max(0, hitFlash - dt * 1.6);
   275	    if (G.bombFx > 0) G.bombFx -= dt;
   276	    if (G.bannerT > 0) G.bannerT -= dt;
   277	    if (G.intro > 0) G.intro -= dt;
   278	    SH.updateParts(dt);
   279	
   280	    for (var s = 0; s < stars.length; s++) {
   281	      var st = stars[s];
   282	      st.y += st.v * dt * (1 + G.stageIdx * 0.15);
   283	      if (st.y > H() + 4) { st.y = -4; st.x = Math.random() * W(); }
   284	    }
   285	
   286	    if (G.state === 'over' || G.state === 'win') { G.overT += dt; return; }
   287	
   288	    if (G.state === 'clear') {
   289	      G.clearT -= dt;
   290	      updateBullets(dt, true);
   291	      if (G.clearT <= 0) {
   292	        if (G.stageIdx >= 2) {
   293	          G.state = 'win'; G.overT = 0;
   294	          if (G.score > best) { best = G.score; saveBest(best); }
   295	        } else { G.state = 'play'; beginStage(G.stageIdx + 1); }
   296	      }
   297	      return;
   298	    }
   299	
   300	    G.stageT += dt;
   301	    G.meter = Math.max(0, G.meter - 5 * dt);
   302	    updatePlayer(dt);
   303	
   304	    // waves
   305	    var stg = G.stage;
   306	    while (G.waveIdx < stg.waves.length && G.stageT >= stg.waves[G.waveIdx].t) {
   307	      spawnWave(stg.waves[G.waveIdx]); G.waveIdx++;
   308	    }
   309	    if (!G.boss && G.waveIdx >= stg.waves.length && G.stageT >= stg.bossAt) {
   310	      if (G.bossWarn === 0) { G.bossWarn = 2.2; sfx('warn'); }
   311	    }
   312	    if (G.bossWarn > 0 && !G.boss) {
   313	      G.bossWarn -= dt;
   314	      if (G.bossWarn <= 0) { spawnBoss(); G.bossWarn = 0; }
   315	    }
   316	
   317	    updateEnemies(dt);
   318	    updateBoss(dt);
   319	    updateBullets(dt, false);
   320	    collide();
   321	  }
   322	
   323	  function updatePlayer(dt) {
   324	    var p = G.p;
   325	    if (p.inv > 0) p.inv -= dt;
   326	    if (p.hurt > 0) p.hurt -= dt;
   327	
   328	    // focus: 2nd finger down, or shift
   329	    var extra = 0;
   330	    for (var i = 0; i < input.pointers.length; i++) if (!input.pointers[i].bomb && input.pointers[i].id !== G.driveId) extra++;
   331	    p.focus = extra > 0 || !!(input.keys['ShiftLeft'] || input.keys['ShiftRight']);
   332	
   333	    // relative drag: ship follows the finger 1:1 from wherever it was grabbed
   334	    if (G.driveId !== null) {
   335	      var d = null;
   336	      for (var j = 0; j < input.pointers.length; j++) if (input.pointers[j].id === G.driveId) d = input.pointers[j];
   337	      if (d) {
   338	        var f = p.focus ? 0.42 : 1;
   339	        p.x += (d.x - G.anchorX) * f;
   340	        p.y += (d.y - G.anchorY) * f;
   341	        G.anchorX = d.x; G.anchorY = d.y;
   342	        G.dragX = p.x; G.dragY = p.y;
   343	      }
   344	    }
   345	    // keyboard
   346	    var kx = (key('ArrowRight', 'KeyD') ? 1 : 0) - (key('ArrowLeft', 'KeyA') ? 1 : 0);
   347	    var ky = (key('ArrowDown', 'KeyS') ? 1 : 0) - (key('ArrowUp', 'KeyW') ? 1 : 0);
   348	    if (kx || ky) {
   349	      var m = Math.sqrt(kx * kx + ky * ky) || 1;
   350	      var sp = p.focus ? 108 : 258;
   351	      p.x += (kx / m) * sp * dt; p.y += (ky / m) * sp * dt;
   352	      G.dragX = p.x; G.dragY = p.y;
   353	    }
   354	    p.x = clamp(p.x, 10, W() - 10);
   355	    p.y = clamp(p.y, 20, H() - 16);
   356	
   357	    // auto fire
   358	    p.fire -= dt;
   359	    if (p.fire <= 0) {
   360	      if (p.focus) {
   361	        p.fire = 0.07;
   362	        pb(p.x, p.y - 10, 0, -760, 1.5);
   363	        pb(p.x - 5, p.y - 6, 0, -740, 1.1);
   364	        pb(p.x + 5, p.y - 6, 0, -740, 1.1);
   365	      } else {
   366	        p.fire = 0.085;
   367	        pb(p.x - 6, p.y - 8, 0, -700, 1.2);
   368	        pb(p.x + 6, p.y - 8, 0, -700, 1.2);
   369	        pb(p.x - 9, p.y - 4, -150, -640, 0.7);
   370	        pb(p.x + 9, p.y - 4, 150, -640, 0.7);
   371	      }
   372	      sfx('shot');
   373	    }
   374	  }
   375	
   376	  function updateEnemies(dt) {
   377	    for (var i = G.en.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
   378	      var e = G.en[i];
   379	      e.life += dt;
   380	      if (e.flash > 0) e.flash -= dt;
   381	      e.rot += dt * 1.6;
   382	      if (e.st === 'wait') {
   383	        e.wait -= dt;
   384	        if (e.wait <= 0) e.st = 'in';
   385	        continue;
   386	      }
   387	      if (e.st === 'in') {
   388	        var dx = e.tx - e.x, dy = e.ty - e.y, d = Math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);
   389	        if (d < 3) { e.st = 'hold'; e.holdT = e.hold; e.x = e.tx; e.y = e.ty; }
   390	        else { e.x += dx / d * e.speed * dt; e.y += dy / d * e.speed * dt; }
   391	      } else if (e.st === 'hold') {
   392	        e.holdT -= dt;
   393	        if (e.sway) e.x = e.tx + Math.sin(e.life * 1.5) * 26;
   394	        if (e.holdT <= 0) e.st = 'out';
   395	      } else {
   396	        e.x += e.ovx * dt; e.y += e.ovy * dt;
   397	      }
   398	      if (e.y > 12 && e.st !== 'out') runPattern(e, e.pat, dt);
   399	      if (e.y > H() + 50 || e.x < -70 || e.x > W() + 70) G.en.splice(i, 1);
   400	    }
   401	  }
   402	
   403	  function updateBoss(dt) {
   404	    var b = G.boss;
   405	    if (!b) return;
   406	    b.t += dt;
   407	    if (b.flash > 0) b.flash -= dt;
   408	    if (b.state === 'enter') {
   409	      b.y += 90 * dt;
   410	      if (b.y >= 104) { b.y = 104; b.state = 'fight'; b.moveT = 0; b.moveIdx = 0; b.sub = {}; }
   411	      return;
   412	    }
   413	    if (b.state === 'dying') {
   414	      b.dieT -= dt;
   415	      if (Math.random() < 0.5) {
   416	        SH.spawnParts(b.x + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 80, b.y + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 46, 5, '#ffdca8', 160, 4, 0.6);
   417	      }
   418	      shake = Math.max(shake, 7); shakeT = Math.max(shakeT, 0.2);
   419	      if (b.dieT <= 0) {
   420	        SH.spawnParts(b.x, b.y, 60, '#ffffff', 260, 5, 0.9);
   421	        sfx('bomb'); flash = 1;
   422	        addScore(20000 + G.lives * 5000 + G.bombs * 2000);
   423	        G.boss = null; G.eb.length = 0;
   424	        G.state = 'clear'; G.clearT = 2.8;
   425	        G.banner = G.stageIdx >= 2 ? 'ALL CLEAR' : 'STAGE ' + (G.stageIdx + 1) + ' CLEAR';
   426	        G.bannerT = 2.8; sfx('clear');
   427	      }
   428	      return;
   429	    }
   430	    if (b.state === 'break') {
   431	      b.brk -= dt;
   432	      b.y = 104 - Math.sin(Math.max(0, b.brk) * 3) * 6;
   433	      if (b.brk <= 0) {
   434	        b.phaseIdx++;
   435	        var ph = b.def.phases[b.phaseIdx];
   436	        b.hp = ph.hp; b.maxhp = ph.hp;
   437	        makePods();
   438	        b.state = 'fight'; b.moveIdx = 0; b.moveT = 0; b.sub = {};
   439	      }
   440	      return;
   441	    }
   442	
   443	    // fight movement
   444	    var sp = 0.42 + b.phaseIdx * 0.16;
   445	    b.x = W() * 0.5 + Math.sin(b.t * sp) * (86 - b.phaseIdx * 6);
   446	    b.y = 104 + Math.sin(b.t * sp * 1.7) * 12;
   447	
   448	    var ph2 = b.def.phases[b.phaseIdx];
   449	    var mv = ph2.moves[b.moveIdx % ph2.moves.length];
   450	    b.moveT += dt;
   451	    if (b.moveT > mv.dur) {
   452	      b.moveT = 0; b.moveIdx++; b.sub = {};
   453	      G.eb.length = Math.min(G.eb.length, 900);
   454	    }
   455	    // pods alive scale fire
   456	    var alive = 0;
   457	    for (var i = 0; i < b.pods.length; i++) if (b.pods[i].hp > 0) alive++;
   458	    var rate = 1 - 0.22 * (b.pods.length - alive);
   459	
   460	    var src = b.sub;
   461	    src.x = b.x; src.y = b.y + 8;
   462	    if (src.sa === undefined) { src.sa = 0; src.ft = 0.25; src.bl = 0; src.bt = 0; src.wr = SH.rngObj(mv.seed || 11); }
   463	    runPattern(src, mv, dt * rate);
   464	
   465	    // pods fire a simple aimed pop
   466	    for (var k = 0; k < b.pods.length; k++) {
   467	      var pod = b.pods[k];
   468	      if (pod.hp <= 0) continue;
   469	      if (pod.flash > 0) pod.flash -= dt;
   470	      var o = pod.o;
   471	      o.x = b.x + pod.ox; o.y = b.y + pod.oy;
   472	      if (o.sa === undefined) { o.sa = 0; o.ft = 1.2 + k * 0.4; o.bl = 0; o.bt = 0; o.wr = SH.rngObj(31 + k); }
   473	      runPattern(o, {
   474	        kind: 'aimed', period: 1.5 - G.stageIdx * 0.2, n: 2 + G.stageIdx, spread: 0.16,
   475	        burst: 1, speed: 120 + G.stageIdx * 20, c: '#ff9ad1', r: 3.6
   476	      }, dt);
   477	    }
   478	  }
   479	
   480	  function updateBullets(dt, freeze) {
   481	    var i, b;
   482	    for (i = G.pb.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
   483	      b = G.pb[i];
   484	      b.x += b.vx * dt; b.y += b.vy * dt;
   485	      if (b.y < -20 || b.x < -20 || b.x > W() + 20) G.pb.splice(i, 1);
   486	    }
   487	    for (i = G.eb.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
   488	      b = G.eb[i];
   489	      if (b.cu) {
   490	        var c = Math.cos(b.cu * dt), s = Math.sin(b.cu * dt);
   491	        var nx = b.vx * c - b.vy * s, ny = b.vx * s + b.vy * c;
   492	        b.vx = nx; b.vy = ny;
   493	      }
   494	      if (b.ac) { b.vx *= 1 + b.ac * dt; b.vy *= 1 + b.ac * dt; }
   495	      b.x += b.vx * dt; b.y += b.vy * dt;
   496	      if (b.x < -30 || b.x > W() + 30 || b.y < -46 || b.y > H() + 40) G.eb.splice(i, 1);
   497	    }
   498	  }
   499	
   500	  function collide() {
   501	    var i, j, b, e, dx, dy, p = G.p;
   502	    // player bullets vs enemies / boss
   503	    for (i = G.pb.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
   504	      b = G.pb[i];
   505	      var gone = false;
   506	      for (j = G.en.length - 1; j >= 0; j--) {
   507	        e = G.en[j];
   508	        if (e.st === 'wait') continue;
   509	        dx = b.x - e.x; dy = b.y - e.y;
   510	        if (dx * dx + dy * dy < e.r * e.r) {
   511	          damageEnemy(e, b.d, j);
   512	          SH.spawnParts(b.x, b.y, 1, '#fff2a8', 60, 2, 0.18);
   513	          sfx('hit');
   514	          gone = true; break;
   515	        }
   516	      }
   517	      if (gone) { G.pb.splice(i, 1); continue; }
   518	      var bo = G.boss;
   519	      if (bo && (bo.state === 'fight' || bo.state === 'break')) {
   520	        var hitPod = false;
   521	        for (var k = 0; k < bo.pods.length; k++) {
   522	          var pod = bo.pods[k];
   523	          if (pod.hp <= 0) continue;
   524	          dx = b.x - (bo.x + pod.ox); dy = b.y - (bo.y + pod.oy);
   525	          if (dx * dx + dy * dy < pod.r * pod.r) {
   526	            pod.hp -= b.d; pod.flash = 0.08;
   527	            addScore(b.d * 6 * mult());
   528	            SH.spawnParts(b.x, b.y, 1, '#fff2a8', 60, 2, 0.18);
   529	            if (pod.hp <= 0) {
   530	              SH.spawnParts(bo.x + pod.ox, bo.y + pod.oy, 22, '#ffd0a0', 180, 4, 0.65);
   531	              addScore(3000 * mult()); sfx('boom');
   532	              shake = Math.max(shake, 8); shakeT = 0.25;
   533	            }
   534	            hitPod = true; break;
   535	          }
   536	        }
   537	        if (hitPod) { G.pb.splice(i, 1); continue; }
   538	        if (bo.state === 'fight') {
   539	          dx = b.x - bo.x; dy = (b.y - bo.y) * 1.5;
   540	          if (dx * dx + dy * dy < 34 * 34) {
   541	            var podsLeft = 0;
   542	            for (var q = 0; q < bo.pods.length; q++) if (bo.pods[q].hp > 0) podsLeft++;
   543	            var dmg = b.d * (podsLeft === 0 ? 1.35 : 1);
   544	            bo.hp -= dmg; bo.flash = 0.06;
   545	            addScore(dmg * 8 * mult());
   546	            SH.spawnParts(b.x, b.y, 1, '#fff2a8', 60, 2, 0.2);
   547	            sfx('hit');
   548	            G.pb.splice(i, 1);
   549	            if (bo.hp <= 0) {
   550	              if (bo.phaseIdx >= bo.def.phases.length - 1) {
   551	                bo.state = 'dying'; bo.dieT = 1.7; G.eb.length = 0; sfx('boom');
   552	              } else {
   553	                bo.state = 'break'; bo.brk = 1.5; G.eb.length = 0;
   554	                flash = 0.6; shake = 14; shakeT = 0.5; sfx('boom');
   555	                SH.spawnParts(bo.x, bo.y, 36, '#ffffff', 200, 4, 0.7);
   556	                addScore(6000 * mult());
   557	              }
   558	            }
   559	            continue;
   560	          }
   561	        }
   562	      }
   563	    }
   564	
   565	    // enemy bullets vs player (graze + hit)
   566	    if (!p.alive) return;
   567	    for (i = G.eb.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
   568	      b = G.eb[i];
   569	      dx = b.x - p.x; dy = b.y - p.y;
   570	      var d2 = dx * dx + dy * dy;
   571	      var hr = b.r + p.r;
   572	      if (d2 < hr * hr) {
   573	        if (p.inv <= 0) { playerHit(); return; }
   574	      } else {
   575	        var gr = b.r + (p.focus ? 15 : 12);
   576	        if (!b.gz && d2 < gr * gr && p.inv <= 0) {
   577	          b.gz = 1; G.grazes++;
   578	          G.meter = Math.min(90, G.meter + 2.5);
   579	          addScore(30 * mult());
   580	          if (grazeCd <= 0) { sfx('graze'); grazeCd = 0.05; }
   581	          SH.spawnParts(b.x, b.y, 1, '#ffffff', 40, 2, 0.2);
   582	        }
   583	      }
   584	    }
   585	    // body collision with enemies
   586	    for (j = G.en.length - 1; j >= 0; j--) {
   587	      e = G.en[j];
   588	      if (e.st === 'wait') continue;
   589	      dx = e.x - p.x; dy = e.y - p.y;
   590	      if (dx * dx + dy * dy < (e.r + p.r) * (e.r + p.r)) { if (p.inv <= 0) { playerHit(); return; } }
   591	    }
   592	    var bb = G.boss;
   593	    if (bb && bb.state === 'fight') {
   594	      dx = bb.x - p.x; dy = (bb.y - p.y) * 1.4;
   595	      if (dx * dx + dy * dy < 30 * 30 && p.inv <= 0) playerHit();
   596	    }
   597	  }
   598	
   599	  /* ---------------- draw ---------------- */
   600	  function drawBG() {
   601	    var th = G.stage.theme;
   602	    var g = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, 0, 0, H());
   603	    g.addColorStop(0, th.sky[0]); g.addColorStop(1, th.sky[1]);

   619	      ctx.fillRect(st.x, st.y, st.s, st.s * 2.2);
   620	    }
   780	    txt('BEST ' + String(Math.max(best, G.score)).padStart(8, '0'), 8, 29, 9, '#8fa6c8');

   784

   849	
   850	  function drawOverlay() {
   851	    if (G.state !== 'over' && G.state !== 'win') return;
   852	    ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(3,5,12,0.78)';
   853	    ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W(), H());
   854	    var win = G.state === 'win';
   855	    txt(win ? 'ALL CLEAR' : 'SHIP LOST', W() * 0.5, H() * 0.38, 30, win ? '#7ef9ff' : '#ff5f9e', 'center');
   856	    if (win) txt('1CC — THREE STAGES DOWN', W() * 0.5, H() * 0.38 + 22, 11, '#ffd166', 'center');
   857	    txt('SCORE  ' + G.score, W() * 0.5, H() * 0.5, 16, '#ffffff', 'center');
   858	    txt('BEST   ' + best, W() * 0.5, H() * 0.5 + 20, 12, '#8fa6c8', 'center');
   859	    txt('GRAZE  ' + G.grazes, W() * 0.5, H() * 0.5 + 38, 12, '#8fa6c8', 'center');
   860	    if (G.overT > 0.6) {
   861	      ctx.globalAlpha = 0.6 + Math.sin(G.t * 5) * 0.4;
   862	      txt('TAP  ·  ENTER  TO RESTART', W() * 0.5, H() * 0.66, 13, '#ffffff', 'center');
   863	      ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
   864	    }
   865	  }
   866	
   867	  function draw() {
   868	    ctx.setTransform(1, 0, 0, 1, 0, 0);
   869	    ctx.fillStyle = '#000000';
   870	    ctx.fillRect(0, 0, SH.canvas.width, SH.canvas.height);
   871	    SH.applyTransform();
   872	    if (shake > 0.2) {
   873	      ctx.translate((Math.random() - 0.5) * shake, (Math.random() - 0.5) * shake);
   874	    }
   875	    drawBG();
   876	    if (G.bombFx > 0) {
   877	      var r = (0.75 - G.bombFx) * 900;
   878	      ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(255,209,102,' + Math.max(0, G.bombFx) + ')';
   879	      ctx.lineWidth = 14;
   880	      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(G.p.x, G.p.y, Math.max(1, r), 0, TAU); ctx.stroke();
   881	    }
   882	    for (var i = 0; i < G.en.length; i++) if (G.en[i].st !== 'wait') drawEnemy(G.en[i]);
   883	    drawBoss();
   884	    SH.drawParts(ctx);
   885	    drawBullets();
   886	    if (G.state !== 'over' && G.state !== 'win') {
   887	      var p = G.p;
   888	      var alpha = p.inv > 0 ? (Math.floor(G.t * 22) % 2 ? 0.35 : 1) : 1;
   889	      drawShip(p.x, p.y, p.focus, alpha);
   890	    }
   891	    if (flash > 0) {
   892	      ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,' + Math.min(0.75, flash) + ')';
   893	      ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W(), H());
   894	    }
   895	    if (hitFlash > 0) {
   896	      ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,60,110,' + Math.min(0.4, hitFlash * 0.5) + ')';
   897	      ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W(), H());
   898	    }
   899	    drawHUD();
   900	    drawOverlay();
   901	  }
   902	
   903	  /* ---------------- loop ---------------- */
   904	  var last = 0;
   905	  function frame(ts) {
   906	    requestAnimationFrame(frame);
   907	    if (!last) last = ts;
   908	    var dt = (ts - last) / 1000;
   909	    last = ts;
   910	    if (dt > 0.05) dt = 0.05;
   911	    if (dt <= 0) dt = 1 / 60;
   912	    update(dt);
   913	    draw();
   914	  }
   915	
   916	  SH.__g = function () { return G; };
   917	  newGame();
   918	  requestAnimationFrame(frame);
   919	})(SH);

   247	  function playerHit() {
   248	    var p = G.p;
   249	    if (p.inv > 0 || !p.alive || G.state !== 'play') return;
   250	    G.lives--;
   251	    G.meter = 0;
   252	    p.inv = 2.6; p.hurt = 0.5;
   253	    shake = 16; shakeT = 0.6; flash = 0.5; hitFlash = 0.6;
   254	    sfx('death');
   255	    SH.spawnParts(p.x, p.y, 30, '#7ef9ff', 190, 4, 0.7);
   256	    SH.spawnParts(p.x, p.y, 16, '#ffffff', 120, 3, 0.5);
   257	    G.eb.length = 0;
   258	    G.bombs = 3;
   259	    p.x = W() * 0.5; p.y = H() - 120;
   260	    G.driveId = null;
   261	    if (G.lives <= 0) {
   262	      G.state = 'over'; G.overT = 0;
   263	      if (G.score > best) { best = G.score; saveBest(best); }
   264	    }
   265	  }
   266	
   267	  /* ---------------- update ---------------- */
   268	  function update(dt) {
   269	    G.t += dt;
   270	    if (soundCd > 0) soundCd -= dt;
   271	    if (grazeCd > 0) grazeCd -= dt;
   272	    if (shakeT > 0) { shakeT -= dt; if (shakeT <= 0) shake = 0; } else shake *= Math.pow(0.02, dt);
   273	    flash = Math.max(0, flash - dt * 2.2);
   274	    hitFlash = Math.max(0, hitFlash - dt * 1.6);
   275	    if (G.bombFx > 0) G.bombFx -= dt;
   276	    if (G.bannerT > 0) G.bannerT -= dt;
   277	    if (G.intro > 0) G.intro -= dt;
   278	    SH.updateParts(dt);
   279	
   280	    for (var s = 0; s < stars.length; s++) {
   281	      var st = stars[s];
   282	      st.y += st.v * dt * (1 + G.stageIdx * 0.15);
   283	      if (st.y > H() + 4) { st.y = -4; st.x = Math.random() * W(); }
   284	    }
   285	
   286	    if (G.state === 'over' || G.state === 'win') { G.overT += dt; return; }
   287	
   288	    if (G.state === 'clear') {
   289	      G.clearT -= dt;
   290	      updateBullets(dt, true);
   291	      if (G.clearT <= 0) {
   292	        if (G.stageIdx >= 2) {
   293	          G.state = 'win'; G.overT = 0;
   294	          if (G.score > best) { best = G.score; saveBest(best); }
   295	        } else { G.state = 'play'; beginStage(G.stageIdx + 1); }
   296	      }
   297	      return;
   298	    }
   299	
   300	    G.stageT += dt;
   301	    G.meter = Math.max(0, G.meter - 5 * dt);
   302	    updatePlayer(dt);
   303	
   304	    // waves
   305	    var stg = G.stage;
   306	    while (G.waveIdx < stg.waves.length && G.stageT >= stg.waves[G.waveIdx].t) {
   307	      spawnWave(stg.waves[G.waveIdx]); G.waveIdx++;
   308	    }
   309	    if (!G.boss && G.waveIdx >= stg.waves.length && G.stageT >= stg.bossAt) {
   310	      if (G.bossWarn === 0) { G.bossWarn = 2.2; sfx('warn'); }
   311	    }
   312	    if (G.bossWarn > 0 && !G.boss) {
   313	      G.bossWarn -= dt;
   314	      if (G.bossWarn <= 0) { spawnBoss(); G.bossWarn = 0; }
   315	    }
   316	
   317	    updateEnemies(dt);
   318	    updateBoss(dt);
   319	    updateBullets(dt, false);
   320	    collide();
   321	  }
   322	
   323	  function updatePlayer(dt) {
   324	    var p = G.p;
   325	    if (p.inv > 0) p.inv -= dt;
   620	    }
   621	    ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
   622	  }

   650	  function drawEnemy(e) {

   664	      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(0, 0, e.r * 0.4, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();

   725	      ctx.restore();

     1	'use strict';
     2	/* Backstreet Reckoning - core: math, rng, input, audio, fx */
     3	(function (root) {
     4	
     5	  /* ---------- math ---------- */
     6	  const clamp = (v, a, b) => (v < a ? a : v > b ? b : v);
     7	  const lerp = (a, b, t) => a + (b - a) * t;
     8	  const sign = (v) => (v < 0 ? -1 : v > 0 ? 1 : 0);
     9	  const approach = (v, t, d) => (v < t ? Math.min(v + d, t) : Math.max(v - d, t));
    10	
    11	  /* ---------- seeded rng ---------- */
    12	  function makeRng(seed) {
    13	    let s = (seed >>> 0) || 1;
    14	    const f = function () {
    15	      s |= 0; s = (s + 0x6D2B79F5) | 0;
    16	      let t = Math.imul(s ^ (s >>> 15), 1 | s);
    17	      t = (t + Math.imul(t ^ (t >>> 7), 61 | t)) ^ t;
    18	      return ((t ^ (t >>> 14)) >>> 0) / 4294967296;
    19	    };
    20	    f.range = (a, b) => a + f() * (b - a);
    21	    f.int = (a, b) => Math.floor(a + f() * (b - a + 1));
    22	    f.pick = (arr) => arr[Math.floor(f() * arr.length)];
    23	    return f;
    24	  }
    25

    46	    tone(freq, dur, type, vol, slide) {
    47	      if (!this.ctx || this.muted) return;
    48	      const t = this.ctx.currentTime;
    49	      const o = this.ctx.createOscillator();
    50	      const g = this.ctx.createGain();
    51	      o.type = type || 'square';
    52	      o.frequency.setValueAtTime(freq, t);
    53	      if (slide) o.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(Math.max(30, slide), t + dur);
    54	      g.gain.setValueAtTime(0.0001, t);
    55	      g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(vol || 0.2, t + 0.008);
    56	      g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.0001, t + dur);
    57	      o.connect(g); g.connect(this.master);
    58	      o.start(t); o.stop(t + dur + 0.02);
    59	    },
    60	    noise(dur, vol, hp, lp) {
    61	      if (!this.ctx || this.muted) return;
    62	      const t = this.ctx.currentTime;
    63	      const n = Math.floor(this.ctx.sampleRate * dur);

    71	      src.connect(f); f.connect(g); g.connect(this.master);
    72	      src.start(t);
    73	    },

   143	            self.stick.ox += dx * (1 - R / m);
   144	            self.stick.oy += dy * (1 - R / m);

   215	  const FX = {
   216	    parts: [],
   217	    shakeT: 0, shakeMag: 0,
   218	    texts: [],
   219	    MAX: 220,
   220	    burst(x, y, z, n, color, spd, opt) {
   221	      opt = opt || {};
   222	      for (let i = 0; i < n && this.parts.length < this.MAX; i++) {
   223	        const a = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2;
   224	        const s = spd * (0.4 + Math.random() * 0.8);
   225	        this.parts.push({
   226	          x: x, y: y, z: z,
   227	          vx: Math.cos(a) * s, vy: Math.sin(a) * s - (opt.up || 0),
   228	          life: opt.life || (0.35 + Math.random() * 0.4),
   229	          max: opt.life || 0.7,
   230	          r: opt.r || (2 + Math.random() * 3),
   231	          c: color, g: opt.g === undefined ? 900 : opt.g
   232	        });
   233	      }
   234	    },
   235	    text(x, y, z, str, color) {
   236	      if (this.texts.length > 18) this.texts.shift();
   237	      this.texts.push({ x: x, y: y, z: z, s: str, c: color, life: 0.75, max: 0.75 });
   238	    },
   239	    shake(mag, dur) {
   240	      if (mag > this.shakeMag || this.shakeT <= 0) { this.shakeMag = mag; }
   241	      this.shakeT = Math.max(this.shakeT, dur);
   242	    },
   243	    update(dt) {
   244	      for (let i = this.parts.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
   245	        const p = this.parts[i];
   246	        p.life -= dt;
   247	        if (p.life <= 0) { this.parts.splice(i, 1); continue; }
   248	        p.x += p.vx * dt; p.y += p.vy * dt; p.vy += p.g * dt;
   249	        if (p.y > 0) { p.y = 0; p.vy *= -0.35; p.vx *= 0.6; }
   250	      }
   251	      for (let i = this.texts.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
   252	        const t = this.texts[i];
   253	        t.life -= dt; t.y += 42 * dt;
   254	        if (t.life <= 0) this.texts.splice(i, 1);
   255	      }
   256	      if (this.shakeT > 0) { this.shakeT -= dt; if (this.shakeT <= 0) this.shakeMag = 0; }
   257	    },
   258	    offset() {
   259	      if (this.shakeT <= 0) return { x: 0, y: 0 };
   260	      const m = this.shakeMag * Math.min(1, this.shakeT * 6);
   261	      return { x: (Math.random() * 2 - 1) * m, y: (Math.random() * 2 - 1) * m };
   262	    },
   263	    clear() { this.parts.length = 0; this.texts.length = 0; this.shakeT = 0; this.shakeMag = 0; }
   264	  };
