    61	    heavy: { name: 'Hauler', hp: 74, spd: 0.34, dmg: 18, col: '#c9a13f', col2: '#7a5f18', w: 0.44, atkR: 0.72, cd: [1.1, 1.9], score: 260 },
    62	    acro: { name: 'Vault Twin', hp: 24, spd: 0.86, dmg: 8, col: '#a97ae8', col2: '#5c3d94', w: 0.26, atkR: 0.58, cd: [0.4, 0.75], score: 140 },
    63	    boss: { name: 'Boss', hp: 240, spd: 0.46, dmg: 20, col: '#d8425f', col2: '#79162b', w: 0.52, atkR: 0.80, cd: [0.8, 1.4], score: 900 }
    64	  };
    65	  const BOSS_NAMES = ['MARLO STEEL', 'CROW VANCE', 'DUTCH RAMONE', 'SABLE KURTZ', 'BIG MERIDIAN', 'OTTO GRIST'];
    66	  const BLOCK_NAMES = ['LOWER WHARF', 'TIN ROW', 'SODIUM MILE', 'GLASS YARD', 'CINDER WALK', 'NINTH CUT'];
    67	
    68	  /* ================= state ================= */
    69	  let G = null;
    70	
    71	  function newGame() {
    72	    G = {
    73	      stage: 1, seed: 1337,
    74	      score: 0, lives: 3,
    75	      best: +(localStorage.getItem('br_best') || 0),
    76	      mode: 'play',          // play | over | stageclear
    77	      t: 0, hintT: 7,
    78	      banner: null, bannerT: 0,
    79	      seg: 0, camX: 0, camLock: null, goT: 0,
    80	      foes: [], items: [], props: [], shots: [], waves: [], pending: [],
    81	      player: null, boss: null, bossName: '',
    82	      flash: 0, slowT: 0
    83	    };
    84	    buildStage(1);
    85	    G.player = makePlayer();
    86	    FX.clear();
    87	  }
    88	
    89	  function makePlayer() {
    90	    return {
    91	      kind: 'player', x: G.camX + W * 0.28, z: 0.55, y: 0, vx: 0, vz: 0, vy: 0,
    92	      face: 1, hp: 120, maxhp: 120, w: 0.32,
    93	      state: 'idle', st: 0, inv: 1.0, hurtT: 0,
    94	      atk: null, atkT: 0, hasHit: false, combo: 0, comboT: 0,
    95	      weapon: null, carry: null, downT: 0, anim: 0, jumpKick: false
    96	    };
    97	  }
    98	
    99	  /* ================= stage generation ================= */
   100	  function buildStage(n) {
   101	    L = segLen();
   102	    const rng = makeRng(G.seed + n * 7919);
   103	    G.stage = n;
   104	    G.seg = 0; G.camX = 0; G.camLock = null; G.goT = 0;
   105	    G.foes = []; G.items = []; G.props = []; G.shots = []; G.pending = []; G.boss = null;
   106	    G.bossName = BOSS_NAMES[(n - 1) % BOSS_NAMES.length];
   107	    G.blockName = BLOCK_NAMES[(n - 1) % BLOCK_NAMES.length];
   108	    G.waves = [];
   109	    const diff = 1 + (n - 1) * 0.28;
   110	
   111	    for (let s = 0; s < SEGS; s++) {
   112	      const start = s * L;
   113	      // props & items
   114	      const nProps = rng.int(3, 6);
   115	      for (let i = 0; i < nProps; i++) {
   116	        G.props.push({
   117	          x: start + rng.range(W * 0.4, L - W * 0.3),
   118	          z: rng.pick(LANES) + rng.range(-0.06, 0.06),
   119	          kind: rng.pick(['bin', 'barrel', 'bin']),
   120	          hp: 2, hitT: 0
   121	        });
   122	      }
   123	      if (s < SEGS - 1 && rng() < 0.85) {
   124	        G.items.push(mkItem('pipe', start + rng.range(W * 0.5, L - W * 0.4), rng.pick(LANES)));
   125	      }
   126	      if (rng() < 0.7) {
   127	        G.items.push(mkItem('crate', start + rng.range(W * 0.5, L - W * 0.4), rng.pick(LANES)));
   128	      }
   129	      // waves
   130	      if (s < SEGS - 1) {
   131	        const wCount = s === 0 ? 2 : (rng() < 0.5 ? 2 : 3);
   132	        for (let w = 0; w < wCount; w++) {
   133	          const list = [];
   134	          const size = Math.min(6, 2 + Math.round(rng.range(0, 1.4) + s * 0.6 + diff * 0.7));
   135	          for (let k = 0; k < size; k++) {
   136	            let t = 'rusher';
   137	            const r = rng();
   138	            if (r < 0.30) t = 'rusher';
   139	            else if (r < 0.52) t = 'tosser';
   140	            else if (r < 0.72) t = 'acro';
   141	            else if (r < 0.88) t = 'rusher';
   142	            else t = 'heavy';
   143	            if (s === 0 && w === 0 && (t === 'heavy')) t = 'rusher';
   144	            list.push(t);
   145	            if (t === 'acro') list.push('acro'); // twins
   146	          }
   147	          G.waves.push({ seg: s, f: wCount === 2 ? (w === 0 ? 0.18 : 0.96) : (w * 0.42 + 0.14), list: list, done: false });
   148	        }
   149	      } else {
   150	        G.waves.push({ seg: s, f: 0.20, list: ['rusher', 'rusher', 'tosser'], done: false });
   151	        G.waves.push({ seg: s, f: 0.92, list: ['BOSS'], done: false });
   152	      }
   153	    }
   154	    buildSky();
   155	    banner('STAGE ' + n + ' — ' + G.blockName, 2.0);
   156	  }
   157	
   158	  function mkItem(kind, x, z) {
   159	    return { kind: kind, x: x, z: z, y: 0, vy: 0, vx: 0, life: 0, dur: kind === 'pipe' ? 12 : 1 };
   160	  }
   161	
   162	  function banner(text, t) { G.banner = text; G.bannerT = t; }
   163	
   164	  /* ================= spawning ================= */
   165	  function spawnFoe(type, side) {
   166	    const c = FOE[type === 'BOSS' ? 'boss' : type];
   167	    const isBoss = type === 'BOSS';
   168	    const diff = 1 + (G.stage - 1) * 0.22;
   169	    const x = side > 0 ? G.camX + W + 40 + Math.random() * 120 : G.camX - 40 - Math.random() * 120;
   170	    const f = {
   171	      kind: 'foe', type: isBoss ? 'boss' : type, x: x,
   172	      z: clamp(LANES[Math.floor(Math.random() * 3)] + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.08, 0.05, 0.95),
   173	      y: 0, vx: 0, vz: 0, vy: 0, face: side > 0 ? -1 : 1,
   174	      maxhp: Math.round(c.hp * (isBoss ? 1 + (G.stage - 1) * 0.35 : diff)),
   175	      spd: c.spd, dmg: Math.round(c.dmg * (isBoss ? 1 + (G.stage - 1) * 0.16 : Math.min(2, diff))),
   176	      w: c.w, col: c.col, col2: c.col2, score: c.score,
   177	      state: 'idle', st: 0, inv: 0, hurtT: 0, downT: 0,
   178	      atk: null, atkT: 0, hasHit: false, think: Math.random() * 0.5,
   179	      cd: 0.6 + Math.random(), anim: Math.random() * 6, tx: 0, tz: 0,
   180	      armorFlash: 0, phase2: false, hitStack: 0, hitStackT: 0
   180	      armorFlash: 0, phase2: false, hitStack: 0, hitStackT: 0
   181	    };
   182	    f.hp = f.maxhp;
   183	    if (isBoss) { f.name = G.bossName; G.boss = f; }
   184	    G.foes.push(f);
   185	    return f;
   186	  }
   187	
   188	  function checkWaves() {
   189	    const segStart = G.seg * L;
   190	    const prog = (G.camX - segStart) / Math.max(1, L - W);
   191	    let pending = false;
   192	    for (const w of G.waves) {
   193	      if (w.done || w.seg !== G.seg) { if (!w.done && w.seg === G.seg) pending = true; continue; }
   194	      if (prog >= w.f - 0.001) {
   195	        w.done = true;
   196	        for (let i = 0; i < w.list.length; i++) G.pending.push(w.list[i]);
   197	        G.camLock = G.camX;
   198	      } else pending = true;
   199	    }
   200	    // trickle-spawn: never more than MAX_ACTIVE brawlers on screen at once
   201	    let side = G.spawnSide || 1;
   202	    while (G.pending.length && G.foes.length < MAX_ACTIVE) {
   203	      const t = G.pending.shift();
   204	      spawnFoe(t, side);
   205	      side = -side;
   206	      if (t === 'BOSS') { banner(G.bossName, 2.0); Audio.tone(90, 0.6, 'sawtooth', 0.25, 55); }
   207	    }
   208	    G.spawnSide = side;
   209	
   210	    const alive = G.foes.length > 0 || G.pending.length > 0;
   211	    if (alive) { if (G.camLock === null) G.camLock = G.camX; }
   212	    else if (!pending) { G.camLock = null; G.goT = 1; }
   213	    else if (!alive) { G.camLock = null; }
   214	  }
   215	
   216	  /* ================= combat helpers ================= */
   217	  function inLane(a, b, tol) { return Math.abs(a.z - b.z) <= (tol || LANE_TOL); }
   218	
   219	  function hitReach(a, b, reach) {
   220	    const dx = b.x - a.x;
   221	    if (a.face > 0 ? (dx < -b.w * BH * 0.4) : (dx > b.w * BH * 0.4)) return false;
   222	    return Math.abs(dx) <= reach + b.w * BH * 0.45;
   223	  }
   224	
   225	  function damageFoe(f, dmg, kd, dir, kind) {
   226	    if (f.inv > 0 || f.state === 'dying' || f.state === 'carried') return false;
   227	    let stagger = true;
   228	    if (f.type === 'heavy' && !kd && dmg < 15) {
   229	      // armors through jabs
   230	      f.hitStack++; f.hitStackT = 1.1;
   231	      dmg = Math.round(dmg * 0.5);
   232	      f.armorFlash = 0.18;
   233	      stagger = false;
   234	      if (f.hitStack >= 5) { f.hitStack = 0; stagger = true; kd = true; }
   235	    }
   236	    if (f.type === 'boss' && !kd && dmg < 15) {
   237	      f.hitStack++; f.hitStackT = 1.2;
   238	      if (f.hitStack < 4) { stagger = false; f.armorFlash = 0.15; }
   239	      else f.hitStack = 0;
   240	    }
   241	    f.hp -= dmg;
   242	    FX.burst(f.x, -BH * 0.55, f.z, stagger ? 8 : 4, stagger ? '#ffd76b' : '#cfd6e0', 190, { up: 60, life: 0.35 });
   243	    if (f.hp <= 0) {
   244	      f.hp = 0; f.state = 'down'; f.downT = 1e9; f.vy = -480; f.vx = dir * 320; f.dying = true;
   245	      G.score += f.score;
   246	      FX.text(f.x, -BH * 0.9, f.z, '+' + f.score, '#ffe27a');
   247	      FX.burst(f.x, -BH * 0.5, f.z, 16, f.col, 260, { up: 120 });
   248	      Audio.ko(); FX.shake(6 * (W / 960), 0.25);
   249	    } else if (kd) {
   250	      f.state = 'down'; f.downT = 0; f.vy = -430; f.vx = dir * 300; f.inv = 0.05;
   251	      Audio.heavy(); FX.shake(5 * (W / 960), 0.2);
   252	    } else if (stagger) {
   253	      f.state = 'hurt'; f.hurtT = 0.24; f.vx = dir * 130;
   254	      Audio.hit();
   255	    } else {
   256	      Audio.noise(0.08, 0.2, 1800, 2);
   257	      f.vx = dir * 40;
   258	    }
   259	    if (kind === 'combo') FX.shake(3 * (W / 960), 0.12);
   260	    return true;
   261	  }
   262	
   263	  function damagePlayer(p, dmg, kd, dir) {
   264	    if (p.inv > 0 || p.state === 'down' || G.mode !== 'play') return;
   265	    p.hp -= dmg;
   266	    if (p.carry) dropCarry(p, true);
   267	    FX.burst(p.x, -BH * 0.55, p.z, 8, '#ff6a6a', 200, { up: 60 });
   268	    G.flash = 0.18;
   269	    if (p.hp <= 0) {
   270	      p.hp = 0; p.state = 'down'; p.downT = 0; p.vy = -480; p.vx = dir * 300; p.ko = true;
   271	      Audio.ko(); FX.shake(9 * (W / 960), 0.35);
   272	    } else if (kd) {
   273	      p.state = 'down'; p.downT = 0; p.vy = -420; p.vx = dir * 280;
   274	      Audio.hurt(); FX.shake(6 * (W / 960), 0.22);
   275	    } else {
   276	      p.state = 'hurt'; p.hurtT = 0.26; p.vx = dir * 120; p.inv = 0.3;
   277	      Audio.hurt(); FX.shake(4 * (W / 960), 0.15);
   278	    }
   279	    p.atk = null; p.atkT = 0;
   280	  }
   281	
   282	  function smashProp(pr, dir) {
   283	    pr.hp--; pr.hitT = 0.12;
   284	    if (pr.hp > 0) { Audio.noise(0.1, 0.25, 700, 1); return; }
   285	    Audio.noise(0.25, 0.4, 500, 0.7); Audio.tone(150, 0.15, 'square', 0.12, 70);
   286	    FX.burst(pr.x, -BH * 0.25, pr.z, 14, pr.kind === 'bin' ? '#7f8b99' : '#9a6b3a', 240, { up: 140 });
   287	    FX.shake(3 * (W / 960), 0.12);
   288	    G.score += 15;
   289	    const r = Math.random();
   290	    if (r < 0.46) G.items.push(mkItem('food', pr.x, pr.z));
   291	    else if (r < 0.56) G.items.push(mkItem('pipe', pr.x, pr.z));
   292	    const i = G.props.indexOf(pr); if (i >= 0) G.props.splice(i, 1);
   293	  }
   294	
   295	  /* ================= player ================= */
   296	  const ATTACKS = {
   297	    jab: { dur: 0.30, strike: 0.09, reach: BHf(0.58), dmg: 9, kd: false, knock: 1 },
   298	    jab2: { dur: 0.30, strike: 0.09, reach: BHf(0.60), dmg: 10, kd: false, knock: 1 },
   299	    finish: { dur: 0.44, strike: 0.14, reach: BHf(0.68), dmg: 16, kd: true, knock: 1 },
   300	    pipe: { dur: 0.40, strike: 0.13, reach: BHf(1.00), dmg: 20, kd: true, knock: 1 },
   301	    kick: { dur: 0.40, strike: 0.06, reach: BHf(0.72), dmg: 15, kd: true, knock: 1 }
   302	  };
   303	  function BHf(k) { return k; } // reach stored as multiple of BH, resolved at use
   304	
   305	  function reachOf(a) { return a.reach * BH; }
   306	
   307	  function updatePlayer(p, dt) {
   308	    p.anim += dt * 8;
   309	    if (p.inv > 0) p.inv -= dt;
   310	    if (p.comboT > 0) { p.comboT -= dt; if (p.comboT <= 0) p.combo = 0; }
   311	
   312	    /* --- input vector --- */
   313	    let ix = 0, iz = 0;
   314	    if (Input.stick.active && Input.stick.mag > 0.22) { ix = Input.stick.dx; iz = Input.stick.dy; }
   315	    if (Input.key('a', 'arrowleft')) ix -= 1;
   316	    if (Input.key('d', 'arrowright')) ix += 1;
   317	    if (Input.key('w', 'arrowup')) iz -= 1;
   318	    if (Input.key('s', 'arrowdown')) iz += 1;
   319	    const m = Math.hypot(ix, iz);
   320	    if (m > 1) { ix /= m; iz /= m; }
   321	
   322	    const punchDown = Input.tap.punch || Input.hit('j', 'x');
   323	    const jumpDown = Input.tap.jump || Input.hit('k', ' ', 'z');
   324	    let swipe = Input.swipes.length ? Input.swipes[0] : null;
   325	    if (!swipe && (Input.hit('l') || (p.carry && punchDown))) swipe = { dx: p.face * 100, dy: 0 };
   326	
   327	    /* --- downed --- */
   328	    if (p.state === 'down') {
   329	      p.downT += dt;
   330	      p.y += p.vy * dt; p.vy += GRAV * dt; p.x += p.vx * dt; p.vx *= 0.92;
   331	      if (p.y >= 0) { p.y = 0; if (p.vy > 0) p.vy = 0; p.vx *= 0.4; }
   332	      if (p.downT > 1.0) {
   333	        if (p.ko) { loseLife(); return; }
   334	        p.state = 'getup'; p.st = 0.4; p.inv = 0.9;
   335	      }
   336	      clampPlayer(p); return;
   337	    }
   338	    if (p.state === 'getup') { p.st -= dt; if (p.st <= 0) p.state = 'idle'; clampPlayer(p); return; }
   339	    if (p.state === 'hurt') {
   340	      p.hurtT -= dt; p.x += p.vx * dt; p.vx *= 0.86;
   341	      if (p.hurtT <= 0) p.state = 'idle';
   342	      clampPlayer(p); return;
   343	    }
   344	
   345	    /* --- jump physics --- */
   346	    const airborne = p.y < -0.001 || p.vy < 0;
   347	    if (airborne) {
   348	      p.y += p.vy * dt; p.vy += GRAV * dt;
   349	      if (p.y >= 0) { p.y = 0; p.vy = 0; p.jumpKick = false; if (p.state === 'jump') p.state = 'idle'; FX.burst(p.x, 0, p.z, 5, '#6c7480', 120, { up: 30, life: 0.25 }); }
   350	    } else if (jumpDown && p.state !== 'atk') {
   351	      p.vy = -760; p.y = -0.01; p.state = 'jump'; p.jumpKick = false;
   352	      Audio.tone(420, 0.12, 'triangle', 0.13, 620);
   353	    }
   354	
   355	    /* --- attack in progress --- */
   356	    if (p.atk) {
   357	      p.atkT += dt;
   358	      const a = p.atk;
   359	      if (!p.hasHit && p.atkT >= a.strike) {
   360	        p.hasHit = true;
   361	        doPlayerStrike(p, a);
   362	      }
   363	      if (p.atkT >= a.dur) { p.atk = null; if (p.state === 'atk') p.state = 'idle'; }
   364	      if (!airborne) { p.x += p.vx * dt; p.vx *= 0.8; clampPlayer(p); return; }
   365	    }
   366	
   367	    /* --- throwing carried --- */
   368	    if (p.carry && swipe) {
   369	      throwCarry(p, swipe);
   370	    } else if (punchDown && !p.atk) {
   371	      handlePunch(p, airborne);
   372	    }
   373	
   374	    /* --- movement --- */
   375	    if (!p.atk) {
   376	      const spd = BH * (p.carry ? 1.55 : 1.95) * (p.weapon ? 0.95 : 1);
   377	      p.vx = ix * spd;
   378	      p.vz = iz * (p.carry ? 0.48 : 0.62);
   379	      if (Math.abs(ix) > 0.15) p.face = sign(ix);
   380	      p.state = (m > 0.1 && !airborne) ? 'walk' : (airborne ? 'jump' : 'idle');
   381	    }
   382	    p.x += p.vx * dt;
   383	    if (!airborne) p.z = clamp(p.z + p.vz * dt, 0.03, 0.97);
   384	    clampPlayer(p);
   385	
   386	    /* --- pickups by walking over food --- */
   387	    for (let i = G.items.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
   388	      const it = G.items[i];
   389	      if (it.kind !== 'food' || it.held) continue;
   390	      if (Math.abs(it.x - p.x) < BH * 0.4 && Math.abs(it.z - p.z) < 0.14 && p.y > -BH * 0.4) {
   391	        p.hp = Math.min(p.maxhp, p.hp + 40);
   392	        G.score += 25; Audio.heal();
   393	        FX.text(p.x, -BH, p.z, '+40 HP', '#7dffa8');
   394	        FX.burst(p.x, -BH * 0.4, p.z, 10, '#7dffa8', 180, { up: 90 });
   395	        G.items.splice(i, 1);
   396	      }
   397	    }
   398	  }
   399	
   400	  function clampPlayer(p) {
   401	    p.x = clamp(p.x, G.camX + BH * 0.22, G.camX + W - BH * 0.22);
   402	  }
   403	
   404	  function handlePunch(p, airborne) {
   405	    // air kick
   406	    if (airborne) {
   407	      if (!p.jumpKick) { p.jumpKick = true; startAtk(p, ATTACKS.kick); }
   408	      return;
   409	    }
   410	    // pick up ground item
   411	    for (let i = 0; i < G.items.length; i++) {
   412	      const it = G.items[i];
   413	      if (it.kind === 'food' || it.held) continue;
   414	      if (Math.abs(it.x - p.x) < BH * 0.62 && Math.abs(it.z - p.z) < 0.16) {
   415	        if (it.kind === 'pipe') {
   416	          p.weapon = { kind: 'pipe', dur: 12 };
   417	          FX.text(p.x, -BH, p.z, 'PIPE', '#cfd6e0');
   418	        } else {
   419	          p.carry = { kind: 'crate' };
   420	          FX.text(p.x, -BH, p.z, 'CRATE', '#d8a05a');
   421	        }
   422	        G.items.splice(i, 1); Audio.pickup();
   423	        return;
   424	      }
   425	    }
   426	    // grab downed foe
   427	    for (const f of G.foes) {
   428	      if (f.state !== 'down' || f.dying) continue;
   429	      if (Math.abs(f.x - p.x) < BH * 0.75 && Math.abs(f.z - p.z) < 0.2) {
   430	        f.state = 'carried'; f.inv = 0;
   430	        f.state = 'carried'; f.inv = 0;
   431	        p.carry = { kind: 'foe', foe: f };
   432	        Audio.pickup(); FX.text(p.x, -BH * 1.2, p.z, 'GRAB!', '#ffe27a');
   433	        return;
   434	      }
   435	    }
   436	    // attack
   437	    if (p.weapon) { startAtk(p, ATTACKS.pipe); return; }
   438	    p.combo = (p.combo + 1) % 3; p.comboT = 0.75;
   439	    startAtk(p, p.combo === 1 ? ATTACKS.jab : p.combo === 2 ? ATTACKS.jab2 : ATTACKS.finish);
   440	  }
   441	
   442	  function startAtk(p, a) {
   443	    p.atk = a; p.atkT = 0; p.hasHit = false; p.state = 'atk';
   444	    p.vx = p.face * BH * 0.5;
   445	    Audio.whiff();
   446	  }
   447	
   448	  function doPlayerStrike(p, a) {
   449	    const reach = reachOf(a);
   450	    let hitAny = false;
   451	    for (const f of G.foes) {
   452	      if (f.state === 'carried' || f.dying) continue;
   453	      if (!inLane(p, f)) continue;
   454	      if (Math.abs(f.y - p.y) > BH * 0.55) continue;
   455	      if (!hitReach(p, f, reach)) continue;
   456	      const kd = a.kd || (p.weapon != null);
   457	      if (damageFoe(f, a.dmg, kd, p.face, p.combo === 0 ? 'combo' : null)) {
   458	        hitAny = true;
   459	        G.score += 10;
   460	        FX.burst(p.x + p.face * reach * 0.7, -BH * 0.55 + p.y, p.z, 6, '#fff2b0', 220);
   461	      }
   462	    }
   463	    for (let i = G.props.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
   464	      const pr = G.props[i];
   465	      if (Math.abs(pr.z - p.z) > LANE_TOL) continue;
   466	      if (Math.abs(pr.x - p.x) > reach + BH * 0.25) continue;
   467	      if ((p.face > 0 && pr.x < p.x - BH * 0.2) || (p.face < 0 && pr.x > p.x + BH * 0.2)) continue;
   468	      smashProp(pr, p.face); hitAny = true;
   469	    }
   470	    if (hitAny && p.weapon) {
   471	      p.weapon.dur--;
   472	      if (p.weapon.dur <= 0) {
   473	        FX.burst(p.x + p.face * reach * 0.6, -BH * 0.5, p.z, 12, '#cfd6e0', 240, { up: 80 });
   474	        p.weapon = null; FX.text(p.x, -BH, p.z, 'BROKE', '#cfd6e0');
   475	      }
   476	    }
   477	  }
   478	
   479	  function dropCarry(p, forced) {
   480	    if (!p.carry) return;
   481	    if (p.carry.kind === 'foe') {
   482	      const f = p.carry.foe;
   483	      f.state = 'down'; f.downT = 0; f.inv = 0; f.y = 0; f.vy = -160; f.x = p.x + p.face * BH * 0.3; f.z = p.z;
   484	    } else {
   485	      G.items.push(mkItem('crate', p.x + p.face * BH * 0.3, p.z));
   486	    }
   487	    p.carry = null;
   488	  }
   489	
   490	  function throwCarry(p, swipe) {
   491	    const mag = Math.hypot(swipe.dx, swipe.dy) || 1;
   492	    let dx = swipe.dx / mag, dy = swipe.dy / mag;
   493	    if (Math.abs(dx) < 0.2) dx = p.face * 0.2;
   494	    const dir = sign(dx) || p.face;
   495	    p.face = dir;
   496	    const proj = {
   497	      kind: p.carry.kind, foe: p.carry.foe || null,
   498	      x: p.x + dir * BH * 0.25, z: p.z, y: -BH * 0.7,
   499	      vx: dir * BH * 5.2, vz: dy * 0.55, vy: -260, hits: []
   500	    };
   501	    if (proj.foe) { proj.foe.state = 'thrown'; proj.foe.inv = 0; proj.foe.entered = true; }
   502	    G.shots.push({ type: 'thrown', o: proj });
   503	    p.carry = null;
   504	    Audio.tone(300, 0.16, 'square', 0.16, 900);
   505	    FX.shake(3 * (W / 960), 0.1);
   506	  }
   507	
   508	  function loseLife() {
   509	    G.lives--;
   510	    const p = G.player;
   511	    if (G.lives <= 0) {
   512	      G.mode = 'over';
   513	      if (G.score > G.best) { G.best = G.score; try { localStorage.setItem('br_best', String(G.best)); } catch (e) { } }
   514	      Audio.dead();
   515	      return;
   516	    }
   517	    if (p.carry) dropCarry(p, true);
   518	    p.hp = p.maxhp; p.state = 'getup'; p.st = 0.5; p.inv = 2.0; p.ko = false;
   519	    p.y = 0; p.vy = 0; p.vx = 0; p.weapon = null; p.carry = null;
   520	    p.x = G.camX + W * 0.22; p.z = 0.55;
   521	    banner('LIFE LOST — ' + G.lives + ' LEFT', 1.4);
   522	  }
   523	
   524	  /* ================= foe AI ================= */
   525	  function updateFoe(f, dt, p) {
   526	    f.anim += dt * 7;
   527	    if (f.inv > 0 && f.state !== 'carried') f.inv -= dt;
   528	    if (f.armorFlash > 0) f.armorFlash -= dt;
   529	    if (f.hitStackT > 0) { f.hitStackT -= dt; if (f.hitStackT <= 0) f.hitStack = 0; }
   530	
   531	    if (f.state === 'carried') {
   532	      f.x = p.x + p.face * BH * 0.1; f.z = p.z; f.y = -BH * 1.25;
   533	      return;
   534	    }
   535	    if (f.state === 'thrown') { return; } // driven by projectile
   536	
   537	    // keep foes reachable: once on screen they may never leave it
   538	    if (!f.entered && f.x > G.camX + BH * 0.3 && f.x < G.camX + W - BH * 0.3) f.entered = true;
   539	    if (f.entered) f.x = clamp(f.x, G.camX + BH * 0.25, G.camX + W - BH * 0.25);
   540	    else f.x = clamp(f.x, G.camX - W * 0.3, G.camX + W * 1.3);
   541	
   542	    if (f.state === 'down') {
   543	      f.y += f.vy * dt; f.vy += GRAV * dt; f.x += f.vx * dt; f.vx *= 0.9;
   544	      if (f.y >= 0) {
   545	        f.y = 0; if (f.vy > 0) f.vy = 0; f.vx *= 0.3;
   546	        if (f.dying) { f.state = 'dying'; f.st = 0.5; return; }
   547	        f.downT += dt;
   548	      }
   549	      if (!f.dying && f.downT > 0.85 + Math.random() * 0.35) {
   550	        f.state = 'getup'; f.st = 0.42; f.inv = 0.55;
   551	        f.wakeSwing = Math.random() < 0.4;
   552	      }
   553	      return;
   554	    }
   555	    if (f.state === 'dying') {
   556	      f.st -= dt;
   557	      if (f.st <= 0) { const i = G.foes.indexOf(f); if (i >= 0) G.foes.splice(i, 1); if (G.boss === f) G.boss = null; }
   558	      return;
   559	    }
   560	    if (f.state === 'getup') {
   561	      f.st -= dt;
   562	      if (f.st <= 0) {
   563	        f.state = 'idle';
   564	        if (f.wakeSwing) { f.face = sign(p.x - f.x) || f.face; startFoeAtk(f, p, true); }
   565	        f.wakeSwing = false;
   566	      }
   567	      return;
   568	    }
   569	    if (f.state === 'hurt') {
   570	      f.hurtT -= dt; f.x += f.vx * dt; f.vx *= 0.85;
   571	      if (f.hurtT <= 0) f.state = 'idle';
   572	      return;
   573	    }
   574	    if (f.state === 'atk' || f.state === 'wind') {
   575	      f.atkT += dt;
   576	      const a = f.atk;
   577	      if (f.state === 'wind' && f.atkT >= a.wind) { f.state = 'atk'; }
   578	      if (!f.hasHit && f.atkT >= a.wind + a.strike) {
   579	        f.hasHit = true;
   580	        foeStrike(f, a, p);
   581	      }
   582	      if (a.lunge && f.atkT < a.wind + a.strike + 0.1 && f.atkT > a.wind) f.x += f.face * a.lunge * BH * dt;
   583	      if (f.hop) {
   584	        f.y += f.vy * dt; f.vy += GRAV * dt;
   585	        if (f.y >= 0) { f.y = 0; f.vy = 0; f.hop = false; }
   586	      }
   587	      if (f.atkT >= a.wind + a.dur) { f.state = 'idle'; f.atk = null; f.cd = f.cdRange ? f.cdRange[0] + Math.random() * (f.cdRange[1] - f.cdRange[0]) : 1; }
   588	      return;
   589	    }
   590	
   591	    /* --- decide --- */
   592	    f.cd -= dt;
   593	    const pz = p.z, px = p.x;
   594	    const dx = px - f.x, dz = pz - f.z;
   595	    const dist = Math.abs(dx);
   596	    const cfg = FOE[f.type];
   597	    f.cdRange = cfg.cd;
   598	    const playerDown = p.state === 'down' || p.state === 'getup';
   599	
   600	    if (f.think > 0) f.think -= dt;
   601	
   602	    let tgtX, tgtZ = pz, engage = true;
   603	    if (f.type === 'tosser') {
   604	      const want = G.foes.length <= 2 ? BH * 1.15 : BH * 2.0;
   605	      if (dist < want * 0.7) tgtX = px - sign(dx) * want;
   606	      else if (dist > want * 1.5) tgtX = px - sign(dx) * want;
   607	      else { tgtX = f.x; }
   608	      if (f.cd <= 0 && Math.abs(f.z - pz) < 0.10 && dist > BH * 0.9 && !playerDown) {
   609	        f.face = sign(dx) || f.face;
   610	        throwKnife(f, p); f.cd = cfg.cd[0] + Math.random() * (cfg.cd[1] - cfg.cd[0]);
   611	        return;
   612	      }
   613	    } else if (f.type === 'acro') {
   614	      tgtX = px - (f.side || (f.side = Math.random() < 0.5 ? 1 : -1)) * BH * cfg.atkR * 0.85;
   615	      tgtZ = pz + (f.side * 0.02);
   616	    } else {
   617	      tgtX = px - (sign(dx) || 1) * BH * cfg.atkR * 0.8;
   618	    }
   619	    if (playerDown && f.type !== 'boss') { tgtX = px - (sign(dx) || 1) * BH * 1.6; engage = false; }
   620	
   621	    // boss special
   622	    if (f.type === 'boss') {
   623	      if (!f.phase2 && f.hp < f.maxhp * 0.5) {
   624	        f.phase2 = true;
   625	        banner('HE CALLS FOR BACKUP', 1.2);
   626	        spawnFoe('rusher', 1); spawnFoe('rusher', -1);
   627	      }
   628	      if (f.cd <= 0 && dist < BH * 3.2 && Math.abs(dz) < 0.5 && Math.random() < 0.35) {
   629	        // ground pound: full-lane shock
   630	        startFoeAtk(f, p, false, 'pound');
   631	        return;
   632	      }
   633	    }
   634	
   635	    const spd = BH * f.spd * (f.type === 'acro' ? (1 + 0.3 * Math.sin(f.anim * 0.4)) : 1);
   636	    const mvx = tgtX - f.x, mvz = tgtZ - f.z;
   637	    const mm = Math.hypot(mvx, mvz * (GB - HZ));
   638	    if (mm > 4) {
   639	      const nx = mvx / mm, nz = (mvz * (GB - HZ)) / mm;
   640	      f.x += nx * spd * dt;
   641	      f.z = clamp(f.z + (nz * spd * dt) / (GB - HZ), 0.04, 0.96);
   642	      f.state = 'walk';
   643	    } else f.state = 'idle';
   644	    if (Math.abs(dx) > 4) f.face = sign(dx);
   645	
   646	    // separation
   647	    for (const o of G.foes) {
   648	      if (o === f || o.state === 'down' || o.state === 'carried' || o.state === 'thrown') continue;
   649	      const ddx = o.x - f.x, ddz = (o.z - f.z) * (GB - HZ);
   650	      const d = Math.hypot(ddx, ddz);
   651	      const minD = BH * 0.5;
   652	      if (d < minD && d > 0.01) {
   653	        f.x -= (ddx / d) * (minD - d) * 0.6 * dt * 8;
   654	        f.z = clamp(f.z - (ddz / d) * (minD - d) * 0.6 * dt * 8 / (GB - HZ), 0.04, 0.96);
   655	      }
   656	    }
   657	
   658	    if (engage && f.cd <= 0 && f.type !== 'tosser' &&
   659	      Math.abs(p.z - f.z) < 0.13 && Math.abs(p.x - f.x) < BH * cfg.atkR * 1.25 && p.y > -BH * 0.5) {
   660	      f.face = sign(p.x - f.x) || f.face;
   661	      startFoeAtk(f, p, false);
   662	    }
   663	  }
   664	
   665	  function startFoeAtk(f, p, wake, special) {
   666	    const cfg = FOE[f.type];
   667	    let a;
   668	    if (special === 'pound') {
   669	      a = { wind: 0.45, strike: 0.05, dur: 0.6, reach: 1.6, dmg: f.dmg + 4, kd: true, pound: true };
   670	    } else if (f.type === 'heavy') {
   671	      a = { wind: 0.42, strike: 0.06, dur: 0.5, reach: cfg.atkR * 1.15, dmg: f.dmg, kd: true, lunge: 1.2 };
   672	    } else if (f.type === 'acro') {
   673	      a = { wind: 0.16, strike: 0.06, dur: 0.34, reach: cfg.atkR * 1.1, dmg: f.dmg, kd: false, lunge: 3.4 };
   674	      f.hop = true; f.vy = -430;
   675	    } else if (f.type === 'boss') {
   676	      a = { wind: 0.28, strike: 0.06, dur: 0.42, reach: cfg.atkR * 1.15, dmg: f.dmg, kd: Math.random() < 0.5, lunge: 1.6 };
   677	    } else {
   678	      a = { wind: 0.24, strike: 0.05, dur: 0.34, reach: cfg.atkR * 1.05, dmg: f.dmg, kd: false, lunge: 1.4 };
   679	    }
   680	    if (wake) { a = Object.assign({}, a, { wind: Math.max(0.1, a.wind * 0.5), kd: true }); }
   681	    f.atk = a; f.atkT = 0; f.hasHit = false; f.state = 'wind';
   682	  }
   683	
   684	  function foeStrike(f, a, p) {
   685	    const reach = a.reach * BH;
   686	    if (a.pound) {
   687	      FX.shake(10 * (W / 960), 0.35);
   688	      Audio.heavy();
   689	      for (let i = 0; i < 22; i++) FX.burst(f.x + (Math.random() - 0.5) * reach * 2, 0, f.z, 1, '#e6b45a', 260, { up: 160 });
   690	      if (Math.abs(p.x - f.x) < reach && Math.abs(p.z - f.z) < 0.5 && p.y > -BH * 0.4) damagePlayer(p, a.dmg, true, sign(p.x - f.x) || f.face);
   691	      return;
   692	    }
   693	    Audio.whiff();
   694	    if (!inLane(f, p, 0.14)) return;
   695	    if (Math.abs(p.y - f.y) > BH * 0.55) return;
   696	    if (!hitReach(f, p, reach)) return;
   697	    damagePlayer(p, a.dmg, a.kd, f.face);
   698	  }
   699	
   700	  function throwKnife(f, p) {
   701	    G.shots.push({
   702	      type: 'knife', x: f.x + f.face * BH * 0.3, z: f.z, y: -BH * 0.62,
   703	      vx: f.face * BH * 3.6, life: 3, spin: 0
   704	    });
   705	    Audio.knife();
   706	    f.state = 'wind'; f.atk = { wind: 0.18, strike: 0.02, dur: 0.2, reach: 0, dmg: 0, kd: false }; f.atkT = 0; f.hasHit = true;
   707	  }
   708	
   709	  /* ================= projectiles ================= */
   710	  function updateShots(dt, p) {
   711	    for (let i = G.shots.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
   712	      const s = G.shots[i];
   713	      if (s.type === 'knife') {
   714	        s.x += s.vx * dt; s.life -= dt; s.spin += dt * 22;
   715	        if (s.life <= 0 || s.x < G.camX - 60 || s.x > G.camX + W + 60) { G.shots.splice(i, 1); continue; }
   716	        if (Math.abs(s.x - p.x) < BH * 0.3 && Math.abs(s.z - p.z) < 0.11 && p.y > -BH * 0.55 && p.state !== 'down') {
   717	          damagePlayer(p, 9, false, sign(s.vx));
   718	          FX.burst(s.x, s.y, s.z, 6, '#ff8b6a', 180);
   719	          G.shots.splice(i, 1);
   720	        }
   721	        continue;
   722	      }
   723	      // thrown crate / foe
   724	      const o = s.o;
   725	      o.x += o.vx * dt; o.y += o.vy * dt; o.vy += GRAV * 0.8 * dt;
   726	      o.z = clamp(o.z + o.vz * dt, 0.04, 0.96);
   727	      if (o.foe) { o.foe.x = o.x; o.foe.y = o.y; o.foe.z = o.z; }
   728	      FX.burst(o.x, o.y, o.z, 1, '#ffd76b', 40, { life: 0.2, g: 0 });
   729	      // collide with foes
   730	      for (const f of G.foes) {

   759	  /* ================= camera / progress ================= */
   760	  function updateCamera(dt, p) {
   761	    const segStart = G.seg * L;
   762	    const maxCam = G.seg * L + (L - W);
   763	    const minCam = Math.max(0, segStart - W);
   764	    let target = p.x - W * 0.42;
   765	    target = clamp(target, minCam, maxCam);
   766	    if (G.camLock !== null) target = Math.min(target, G.camLock);
   767	    G.camX = lerp(G.camX, target, Math.min(1, dt * 5));
   768	    G.camX = clamp(G.camX, minCam, maxCam);
   769	
   770	    checkWaves();
   771	
   772	    if (G.camLock === null && G.camX >= maxCam - 2) {
   773	      if (G.seg < SEGS - 1) {
   774	        G.seg++;
   775	        G.goT = 0;
   776	        banner('BLOCK ' + (G.seg + 1) + (G.seg === SEGS - 1 ? ' — BOSS ALLEY' : ''), 1.4);
   777	        G.score += 200;
   778	        Audio.clear();
   779	      }
   780	    }
   781	    if (G.goT > 0) G.goT -= dt * 0;
   782	  }
   783	
   784	  /* ================= main update ================= */
   785	  function update(dt) {
   786	    G.t += dt;
   787	    if (G.hintT > 0 && Input.anyInput) G.hintT -= dt;
   788	    if (G.bannerT > 0) { G.bannerT -= dt; if (G.bannerT <= 0) G.banner = null; }
   789	    if (G.flash > 0) G.flash -= dt;
   790	
   791	    if (G.mode === 'over') {
   792	      if (Input.tap.punch || Input.tap.jump || Input.hit('enter', ' ', 'j', 'k', 'r')) { newGame(); }
   793	      FX.update(dt);
   794	      return;
   795	    }
   796	    if (G.mode === 'stageclear') {
   797	      G.st -= dt;
   798	      FX.update(dt);
   799	      if (G.st <= 0) {
   800	        const p = G.player;
   801	        buildStage(G.stage + 1);
   802	        p.x = W * 0.25; p.z = 0.55; p.hp = Math.min(p.maxhp, p.hp + 45); p.carry = null; p.weapon = null;
   803	        p.state = 'idle'; p.inv = 1.2; p.y = 0; p.vy = 0;
   804	        G.mode = 'play';
   805	      }
   806	      return;
   807	    }
   808	
   809	    const p = G.player;
   810	    updatePlayer(p, dt);
   811	    for (let i = G.foes.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) updateFoe(G.foes[i], dt, p);
   812	    updateShots(dt, p);
   813	
   814	    // items physics
   815	    for (const it of G.items) { if (it.y < 0) { it.y += it.vy * dt; it.vy += GRAV * dt; if (it.y > 0) { it.y = 0; it.vy = 0; } } }
   816	
   817	    updateCamera(dt, p);
   818	    FX.update(dt);
   819	
   820	    // boss defeat -> stage clear
   821	    if (G.seg === SEGS - 1 && G.waves[G.waves.length - 1].done && !G.boss && G.foes.length === 0 && G.pending.length === 0) {
   822	      G.mode = 'stageclear'; G.st = 2.6;
   823	      G.score += 1000 + G.stage * 250;
   824	      banner('STAGE ' + G.stage + ' CLEAR  +' + (1000 + G.stage * 250), 2.4);
   825	      Audio.clear();
   826	      if (G.score > G.best) { G.best = G.score; try { localStorage.setItem('br_best', String(G.best)); } catch (e) { } }
   827	    }
   828	  }
   829	
   830	  /* ================= rendering ================= */
   831	  let SKY = [];
   832	  function buildSky() {
   833	    const layers = [
   834	      { par: 0.14, wMul: 0.30, hMin: 0.35, hMax: 0.85, col: '#1d2033', win: 'rgba(255,214,120,0.13)' },
   835	      { par: 0.32, wMul: 0.24, hMin: 0.25, hMax: 0.62, col: '#282b42', win: 'rgba(255,200,110,0.16)' }
   836	    ];
   837	    SKY = [];
   838	    for (let li = 0; li < layers.length; li++) {
   839	      const cfg = layers[li];
   840	      const rng = makeRng(G.seed + G.stage * 7717 + li * 331);
   841	      const arr = [];
   842	      for (let i = 0; i < 24; i++) {
   843	        const rows = 5, wins = [];
   844	        for (let c = 0; c < 3; c++) for (let r = 1; r < rows; r++) if (rng() < 0.42) wins.push([c, r]);
   845	        arr.push({ hf: cfg.hMin + rng() * (cfg.hMax - cfg.hMin), rows: rows, wins: wins });
   846	      }
   847	      SKY.push({ cfg: cfg, arr: arr });
   848	    }
   849	  }
   850	
   851	  function drawBackground() {
   852	    const g = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, 0, 0, HZ);
   853	    g.addColorStop(0, '#141826'); g.addColorStop(0.6, '#26243c'); g.addColorStop(1, '#4a3350');
   854	    ctx.fillStyle = g; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, HZ + 1);
   855	
   856	    // far skyline (precomputed per stage; tiled + parallax)
   857	    for (let li = 0; li < SKY.length; li++) {
   858	      const layer = SKY[li], cfg = layer.cfg, arr = layer.arr;
   859	      const bw = W * cfg.wMul;
   860	      const off = G.camX * cfg.par;
   861	      const first = Math.floor(off / bw) - 1;
   862	      const count = Math.ceil(W / bw) + 3;
   863	      for (let i = first; i < first + count; i++) {
   864	        const b = arr[((i % arr.length) + arr.length) % arr.length];
   865	        const bh2 = HZ * b.hf;
   866	        const x = i * bw - off;
   867	        if (x > W || x + bw * 0.86 < 0) continue;
   868	        ctx.fillStyle = cfg.col;
   869	        ctx.fillRect(x, HZ + 2 - bh2, bw * 0.86, bh2);
   870	        ctx.fillStyle = cfg.win;
   871	        const rh = bh2 / b.rows;
   872	        for (let k = 0; k < b.wins.length; k++) {
   873	          const wn = b.wins[k];
   874	          ctx.fillRect(x + bw * (0.12 + wn[0] * 0.26), HZ + 2 - bh2 + H * 0.015 + wn[1] * rh, bw * 0.13, rh * 0.42);
   875	        }
   876	      }
   877	    }
   878	
   879	    // street
   880	    const gr = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, HZ, 0, H);
   881	    gr.addColorStop(0, '#3a3345'); gr.addColorStop(0.25, '#2c2a35'); gr.addColorStop(1, '#1b1a22');
   882	    ctx.fillStyle = gr; ctx.fillRect(0, HZ, W, H - HZ);
   883	
   884	    // sidewalk band at top of ground
   885	    ctx.fillStyle = '#3f3b4c';
   886	    ctx.fillRect(0, HZ, W, (GB - HZ) * 0.10);
   887	    ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(0,0,0,0.25)';
   888	    ctx.fillRect(0, HZ + (GB - HZ) * 0.10, W, 2);
   889	
   890	    // lane guide stripes (subtle, readability for lane-honest hits)
   891	    for (let i = 0; i < LANES.length; i++) {
   892	      const y = groundY(LANES[i]);
   893	      ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.045)';
   894	      ctx.lineWidth = Math.max(1, H * 0.004);
   895	      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(0, y); ctx.lineTo(W, y); ctx.stroke();
   896	    }
   897	    // road dashes
   898	    const dashOff = -(G.camX * 1.0) % (W * 0.16);
   899	    ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(240,220,140,0.10)';
   900	    for (let x = dashOff - W * 0.16; x < W + W * 0.16; x += W * 0.16) {
   901	      ctx.fillRect(x, groundY(0.5) - H * 0.006, W * 0.075, H * 0.012);
   902	    }
   903	    // bottom vignette
   904	    ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(0,0,0,0.35)'; ctx.fillRect(0, GB + (H - GB) * 0.3, W, H);
   905	  }
   906	
   907	  function shadow(x, y, z, w) {
   908	    const s = dscale(z);
   909	    ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(0,0,0,0.34)';
   910	    ctx.beginPath();
   911	    ctx.ellipse(x, groundY(z), w * BH * 0.5 * s, w * BH * 0.16 * s, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2);
   912	    ctx.fill();
   913	  }
   914	
   915	  function limb(x1, y1, x2, y2, w, col) {
   916	    ctx.strokeStyle = col; ctx.lineWidth = w; ctx.lineCap = 'round';
   917	    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(x1, y1); ctx.lineTo(x2, y2); ctx.stroke();
   918	  }
   919	
   920	  function drawFighter(e, opts) {
   921	    const sx = e.x - G.camX;
   922	    if (sx < -BH * 2 || sx > W + BH * 2) return;
   923	    const s = dscale(e.z);
   924	    const gy = groundY(e.z);
   925	    const by = gy + e.y * s;              // body base y
   926	    const h = BH * s;
   927	    const bw = e.w * BH * s;
   928	    const col = opts.col, col2 = opts.col2, skin = opts.skin || '#e8c39a';
   929	
   930	    shadow(sx, 0, e.z, e.w * (e.y < -2 ? 0.75 : 1));
   931	
   932	    const down = e.state === 'down' || e.state === 'dying';
   933	    const getup = e.state === 'getup';
   934	    ctx.save();
   935	    ctx.translate(sx, by);
   936	    if (down) {
   937	      const t = e.state === 'dying' ? clamp(1 - e.st / 0.5, 0, 1) : 1;
   938	      ctx.rotate(e.face * Math.PI * 0.5 * clamp(e.y < -2 ? 0.5 : 1, 0, 1));
   939	      ctx.globalAlpha = e.state === 'dying' ? 1 - t * 0.85 : 1;
   940	    } else if (getup) {
   941	      ctx.rotate(e.face * (Math.PI * 0.5) * (e.st / 0.42));
   942	    }
   943	
   944	    const flash = (e.inv > 0 && Math.floor(e.inv * 20) % 2 === 0) || (e.armorFlash > 0);
   945	    const bodyCol = flash ? '#ffffff' : col;
   946	    const darkCol = flash ? '#dddddd' : col2;
   947	
   948	    // legs
   949	    const walkP = (e.state === 'walk') ? Math.sin(e.anim) : 0;
   950	    const legW = Math.max(2, bw * 0.30);
   951	    limb(-bw * 0.20, -h * 0.42, -bw * 0.22 + walkP * bw * 0.35, 0, legW, darkCol);
   952	    limb(bw * 0.20, -h * 0.42, bw * 0.22 - walkP * bw * 0.35, 0, legW, darkCol);
   953	
   954	    // torso
   955	    ctx.fillStyle = bodyCol;
   956	    roundRect(-bw * 0.44, -h * 0.80, bw * 0.88, h * 0.42, bw * 0.18);
   957	    ctx.fill();
   958	    // belt
   959	    ctx.fillStyle = darkCol; ctx.fillRect(-bw * 0.44, -h * 0.44, bw * 0.88, h * 0.05);
   960	
   961	    // arms
   962	    const armW = Math.max(2, bw * 0.24);
   963	    let punchExt = 0;
   964	    if (e.state === 'atk' && e.atk) {
   965	      const a = e.atk;
   966	      const t0 = e.kind === 'player' ? e.atkT / a.dur : (e.atkT - a.wind) / Math.max(0.001, a.dur);
   967	      punchExt = Math.sin(clamp(t0, 0, 1) * Math.PI) * (opts.reach || 0.9);
   968	    } else if (e.state === 'wind') {
   969	      punchExt = -0.25 * (e.atkT / Math.max(0.001, e.atk ? e.atk.wind : 0.2));
   970	    }
   971	    const ax = e.face * (bw * 0.42 + punchExt * h * 0.60);
   972	    limb(-e.face * bw * 0.30, -h * 0.70, -e.face * bw * 0.42, -h * 0.46, armW, bodyCol);
   973	    limb(e.face * bw * 0.30, -h * 0.70, ax, -h * 0.62 - punchExt * h * 0.04, armW, bodyCol);
   974	
   975	    // held stuff
   976	    if (opts.weapon === 'pipe') {
   977	      ctx.save(); ctx.translate(ax, -h * 0.62);
   978	      ctx.rotate(e.face * (punchExt > 0 ? -0.2 : -1.1));
   979	      ctx.fillStyle = '#c9d2dc'; ctx.fillRect(0, -h * 0.03, e.face * h * 0.52, h * 0.06);
   980	      ctx.fillStyle = '#8b96a3'; ctx.fillRect(0, -h * 0.03, e.face * h * 0.10, h * 0.06);
   980	      ctx.fillStyle = '#8b96a3'; ctx.fillRect(0, -h * 0.03, e.face * h * 0.10, h * 0.06);
   981	      ctx.restore();
   982	    }
   983	    if (opts.knife) {
   984	      ctx.fillStyle = '#dfe7ef';
   985	      ctx.fillRect(ax, -h * 0.66, e.face * h * 0.16, h * 0.035);
   986	    }
   987	
   988	    // head
   989	    ctx.fillStyle = skin;
   990	    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(e.face * bw * 0.06, -h * 0.90, bw * 0.30, 0, Math.PI * 2); ctx.fill();
   991	    ctx.fillStyle = darkCol;
   992	    ctx.beginPath();
   993	    ctx.arc(e.face * bw * 0.06, -h * 0.95, bw * 0.31, Math.PI * 1.05, Math.PI * 2.05); ctx.fill();
   994	    // face marker (direction)
   995	    ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(20,15,25,0.75)';
   996	    ctx.fillRect(e.face * bw * 0.16, -h * 0.93, e.face * bw * 0.16, bw * 0.09);
   997	
   998	    ctx.restore();
   999	    ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
  1000	  }
  1001	
  1002	  function roundRect(x, y, w, h, r) {
  1003	    r = Math.min(r, w * 0.5, h * 0.5);
  1004	    ctx.beginPath();
  1005	    ctx.moveTo(x + r, y);
  1006	    ctx.arcTo(x + w, y, x + w, y + h, r);
  1007	    ctx.arcTo(x + w, y + h, x, y + h, r);
  1008	    ctx.arcTo(x, y + h, x, y, r);
  1009	    ctx.arcTo(x, y, x + w, y, r);
  1010	    ctx.closePath();
  1011	  }
  1012	
  1013	  function drawProp(pr) {
  1014	    const sx = pr.x - G.camX;
  1015	    if (sx < -100 || sx > W + 100) return;
  1016	    const s = dscale(pr.z), gy = groundY(pr.z);
  1017	    const h = BH * (pr.kind === 'bin' ? 0.46 : 0.52) * s;
  1018	    const w = BH * 0.42 * s;
  1019	    shadow(sx, 0, pr.z, 0.42);
  1020	    ctx.save(); ctx.translate(sx, gy);
  1021	    if (pr.hitT > 0) { ctx.translate((Math.random() - 0.5) * 6, 0); pr.hitT -= 0.016; }
  1022	    if (pr.kind === 'bin') {
  1023	      ctx.fillStyle = '#5c6674'; roundRect(-w / 2, -h, w, h, w * 0.12); ctx.fill();
  1024	      ctx.fillStyle = '#78848f'; ctx.fillRect(-w / 2, -h, w, h * 0.14);
  1025	      ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(0,0,0,0.25)'; ctx.fillRect(-w * 0.1, -h * 0.86, w * 0.06, h * 0.7);
  1026	    } else {
  1027	      ctx.fillStyle = '#8a5f33'; roundRect(-w / 2, -h, w, h, w * 0.16); ctx.fill();
  1028	      ctx.fillStyle = '#a5763f'; ctx.fillRect(-w / 2, -h * 0.72, w, h * 0.12);
  1029	      ctx.fillStyle = '#a5763f'; ctx.fillRect(-w / 2, -h * 0.34, w, h * 0.12);
  1030	    }
  1031	    ctx.restore();
  1032	  }
  1033	
  1034	  function drawItem(it) {
  1035	    const sx = it.x - G.camX;
  1036	    if (sx < -80 || sx > W + 80) return;
  1037	    const s = dscale(it.z), gy = groundY(it.z) + it.y * s;
  1038	    shadow(sx, 0, it.z, 0.34);
  1039	    const pulse = 1 + Math.sin(G.t * 6 + it.x) * 0.06;
  1040	    ctx.save(); ctx.translate(sx, gy);
  1041	    if (it.kind === 'pipe') {
  1042	      ctx.fillStyle = '#c9d2dc'; ctx.fillRect(-BH * 0.26 * s, -BH * 0.05 * s, BH * 0.52 * s, BH * 0.06 * s);
  1043	      ctx.fillStyle = '#8b96a3'; ctx.fillRect(-BH * 0.26 * s, -BH * 0.05 * s, BH * 0.12 * s, BH * 0.06 * s);
  1044	    } else if (it.kind === 'crate') {
  1045	      const w = BH * 0.34 * s;
  1046	      ctx.fillStyle = '#a5763f'; roundRect(-w / 2, -w, w, w, w * 0.1); ctx.fill();
  1047	      ctx.strokeStyle = '#6f4c25'; ctx.lineWidth = Math.max(1, w * 0.08);
  1048	      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(-w / 2, -w); ctx.lineTo(w / 2, 0); ctx.moveTo(w / 2, -w); ctx.lineTo(-w / 2, 0); ctx.stroke();
  1049	    } else {
  1050	      const r = BH * 0.13 * s * pulse;
  1051	      ctx.fillStyle = '#2fbf6b'; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(0, -r, r, 0, Math.PI * 2); ctx.fill();
  1052	      ctx.fillStyle = '#eafff0';
  1053	      ctx.fillRect(-r * 0.5, -r * 1.16, r, r * 0.32);
  1054	      ctx.fillRect(-r * 0.16, -r * 1.5, r * 0.32, r);
  1055	    }
  1056	    ctx.restore();
  1057	  }
  1058	
  1059	  function drawShot(s) {
  1060	    if (s.type === 'knife') {
  1061	      const sx = s.x - G.camX, sy = groundY(s.z) + s.y * dscale(s.z);
  1062	      ctx.save(); ctx.translate(sx, sy); ctx.rotate(s.spin);
  1063	      ctx.fillStyle = '#e6eef7'; ctx.fillRect(-BH * 0.11, -BH * 0.02, BH * 0.22, BH * 0.04);
  1064	      ctx.fillStyle = '#8d97a3'; ctx.fillRect(-BH * 0.11, -BH * 0.02, BH * 0.07, BH * 0.04);
  1065	      ctx.restore();
  1066	    } else {
  1067	      const o = s.o;
  1068	      if (!o.foe) {
  1069	        const sx = o.x - G.camX, sy = groundY(o.z) + o.y * dscale(o.z);
  1070	        const w = BH * 0.34 * dscale(o.z);
  1071	        ctx.save(); ctx.translate(sx, sy); ctx.rotate(G.t * 9);
  1072	        ctx.fillStyle = '#a5763f'; roundRect(-w / 2, -w / 2, w, w, w * 0.1); ctx.fill();
  1073	        ctx.restore();
  1074	      }
  1075	    }
  1076	  }
  1077	
  1078	  const drawList = [];
  1079	  const FOE_OPT = { col: '#e4643c', col2: '#8d3320', skin: '#d8b48c', knife: false, reach: 0.9 };
  1080	  const PL_OPT = { col: '#3f8ee0', col2: '#1f4f88', skin: '#f0c9a0', weapon: null, reach: 1.0 };
  1081	  const byZ = (a, b) => a.z - b.z;
  1082	
  1083	  function drawScene() {
  1084	    drawBackground();
  1085	
  1086	    drawList.length = 0;
  1087	    for (let i = 0; i < G.props.length; i++) drawList.push({ z: G.props[i].z, t: 0, o: G.props[i] });
  1088	    for (let i = 0; i < G.items.length; i++) drawList.push({ z: G.items[i].z, t: 1, o: G.items[i] });
  1089	    for (let i = 0; i < G.shots.length; i++) {
  1090	      const s = G.shots[i];
  1091	      if (s.type === 'knife') drawList.push({ z: s.z, t: 2, o: s });
  1092	      else if (!s.o.foe) drawList.push({ z: s.o.z, t: 2, o: s });
  1093	    }
  1094	    for (let i = 0; i < G.foes.length; i++) drawList.push({ z: G.foes[i].z, t: 3, o: G.foes[i] });
  1095	    const p = G.player;
  1096	    drawList.push({ z: p.z, t: 4, o: p });
  1097	
  1098	    drawList.sort(byZ);
  1099	    for (let i = 0; i < drawList.length; i++) {
  1100	      const e = drawList[i];
  1101	      switch (e.t) {
  1102	        case 0: drawProp(e.o); break;
  1103	        case 1: drawItem(e.o); break;
  1104	        case 2: drawShot(e.o); break;
  1105	        case 3: {
  1106	          const f = e.o;
  1107	          FOE_OPT.col = f.col; FOE_OPT.col2 = f.col2;
  1108	          FOE_OPT.knife = f.type === 'tosser';
  1109	          FOE_OPT.reach = f.type === 'heavy' ? 1.1 : 0.9;
  1110	          drawFighter(f, FOE_OPT); drawFoeBar(f);
  1111	          break;
  1112	        }
  1113	        case 4:
  1114	          PL_OPT.weapon = p.weapon ? 'pipe' : null;
  1115	          PL_OPT.reach = p.weapon ? 1.3 : 1.0;
  1116	          drawFighter(p, PL_OPT);
  1117	          if (p.carry) drawCarried(p);
  1118	          break;
  1119	      }
  1120	    }
  1121	    for (let i = 0; i < FX.parts.length; i++) drawPart(FX.parts[i]);
  1122	
  1123	    for (const t of FX.texts) {
  1124	      const sx = t.x - G.camX, sy = groundY(t.z) + t.y * dscale(t.z);
  1125	      ctx.globalAlpha = clamp(t.life / t.max, 0, 1);
  1126	      ctx.fillStyle = t.c;
  1127	      ctx.font = 'bold ' + Math.round(H * 0.045) + 'px system-ui, sans-serif';
  1128	      ctx.textAlign = 'center';
  1129	      ctx.fillText(t.s, sx, sy);
  1130	      ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
  1131	    }
  1132	  }
  1133	
  1134	  function drawPart(q) {
  1135	    const s = dscale(q.z);
  1136	    ctx.fillStyle = q.c;
  1137	    ctx.globalAlpha = clamp(q.life / q.max, 0, 1);
  1138	    ctx.fillRect(q.x - G.camX - q.r * s * 0.5, groundY(q.z) + q.y * s - q.r * s * 0.5, q.r * s, q.r * s);
  1139	    ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
  1140	  }
  1141	
  1142	  function drawCarried(p) {
  1143	    const sx = p.x - G.camX, s = dscale(p.z);
  1144	    const y = groundY(p.z) + p.y * s - BH * s * 1.25;
  1145	    if (p.carry.kind === 'crate') {
  1146	      const w = BH * 0.36 * s;
  1147	      ctx.fillStyle = '#a5763f'; roundRect(sx - w / 2, y - w * 0.5, w, w, w * 0.1); ctx.fill();
  1148	      ctx.strokeStyle = '#6f4c25'; ctx.lineWidth = Math.max(1, w * 0.08);
  1149	      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(sx - w / 2, y - w * 0.5); ctx.lineTo(sx + w / 2, y + w * 0.5); ctx.stroke();
  1150	    }
  1151	  }
  1152	
  1153	  function drawFoeBar(f) {
  1154	    if (f.state === 'dying' || f.type === 'boss') return;
  1155	    if (f.hp >= f.maxhp) return;
  1156	    const s = dscale(f.z), sx = f.x - G.camX;
  1157	    const y = groundY(f.z) + f.y * s - BH * s * 1.18;
  1158	    const w = BH * 0.5 * s, h = Math.max(2, H * 0.009);
  1159	    ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(0,0,0,0.55)'; ctx.fillRect(sx - w / 2, y, w, h);
  1160	    ctx.fillStyle = f.hp / f.maxhp > 0.35 ? '#ffcf5c' : '#ff6a6a';
  1161	    ctx.fillRect(sx - w / 2, y, w * (f.hp / f.maxhp), h);
  1162	  }
  1163	
  1164	  /* ================= HUD ================= */
  1165	  function drawHUD() {
  1166	    const p = G.player;
  1167	    const pad = H * 0.03;
  1168	    const bw = W * 0.30, bh = H * 0.042;
  1169	    ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(0,0,0,0.45)';
  1170	    roundRect(pad, pad, bw, bh, bh * 0.3); ctx.fill();
  1171	    const hp = clamp(p.hp / p.maxhp, 0, 1);
  1172	    const hg = ctx.createLinearGradient(pad, 0, pad + bw, 0);
  1173	    hg.addColorStop(0, hp > 0.3 ? '#4de08a' : '#ff5f5f'); hg.addColorStop(1, hp > 0.3 ? '#a8f06a' : '#ff9c5f');
  1174	    ctx.fillStyle = hg;
  1175	    ctx.fillRect(pad + 2, pad + 2, (bw - 4) * hp, bh - 4);
  1176	    ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.35)'; ctx.lineWidth = Math.max(1, H * 0.003);
  1177	    roundRect(pad, pad, bw, bh, bh * 0.3); ctx.stroke();
  1178	
  1179	    ctx.font = 'bold ' + Math.round(H * 0.032) + 'px system-ui, sans-serif';
  1180	    ctx.textAlign = 'left'; ctx.fillStyle = '#fff';
  1181	    ctx.fillText('YOU', pad + bw * 0.03, pad + bh * 0.74);
  1182	
  1183	    // lives
  1184	    for (let i = 0; i < G.lives; i++) {
  1185	      ctx.fillStyle = '#3f8ee0';
  1186	      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(pad + bw + H * 0.05 + i * H * 0.05, pad + bh * 0.5, H * 0.016, 0, Math.PI * 2); ctx.fill();
  1187	      ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.5)'; ctx.lineWidth = Math.max(1, H * 0.004); ctx.stroke();
  1188	    }
  1189	
  1190	    // score
  1191	    ctx.textAlign = 'right'; ctx.fillStyle = '#fff';

  1322	  function frame(now) {
  1323	    let dt = (now - last) / 1000;
  1324	    last = now;

  1326	    if (dt < 0) dt = 0;

   188	  function checkWaves() {
   189	    const segStart = G.seg * L;
   190	    const prog = (G.camX - segStart) / Math.max(1, L - W);
   191	    let pending = false;
   192	    for (const w of G.waves) {
   193	      if (w.done || w.seg !== G.seg) { if (!w.done && w.seg === G.seg) pending = true; continue; }
   194	      if (prog >= w.f - 0.001) {
   195	        w.done = true;
   196	        for (let i = 0; i < w.list.length; i++) G.pending.push(w.list[i]);
   197	        G.camLock = G.camX;
   198	      } else pending = true;
   199	    }
   200	    // trickle-spawn: never more than MAX_ACTIVE brawlers on screen at once
   201	    let side = G.spawnSide || 1;
   202	    while (G.pending.length && G.foes.length < MAX_ACTIVE) {
   203	      const t = G.pending.shift();
   204	      spawnFoe(t, side);
   205	      side = -side;
   206	      if (t === 'BOSS') { banner(G.bossName, 2.0); Audio.tone(90, 0.6, 'sawtooth', 0.25, 55); }
   207	    }
   208	    G.spawnSide = side;
   209	
   210	    const alive = G.foes.length > 0 || G.pending.length > 0;
   211	    if (alive) { if (G.camLock === null) G.camLock = G.camX; }
   212	    else if (!pending) { G.camLock = null; G.goT = 1; }
   213	    else if (!alive) { G.camLock = null; }
   214	  }

     8	  V.clamp = function (v, a, b) { return v < a ? a : (v > b ? b : v); };
     9	  V.lerp = function (a, b, t) { return a + (b - a) * t; };
    10	  V.rand = function (a, b) { return a + Math.random() * (b - a); };
    11	  V.randi = function (a, b) { return Math.floor(a + Math.random() * (b - a)); };
    12	  V.pick = function (arr) { return arr[Math.floor(Math.random() * arr.length)]; };
    13	  V.dist2 = function (a, b) { var dx = a.x - b.x, dy = a.y - b.y; return dx * dx + dy * dy; };
    14	  V.dist = function (a, b) { return Math.sqrt(V.dist2(a, b)); };
