   127	    keys: {},
   128	    mx: 0, my: 0,
   129	    stick: { on: false, id: null, ox: 0, oy: 0, x: 0, y: 0, dx: 0, dy: 0, mag: 0 },
   130	    strike: false, superBtn: false,
   131	    superEdge: false, swapReq: -1, anyTap: false, pausePress: false
   132	  };
   133	  V.in = IN;
   134	  V.layout = { W: 390, H: 700, strike: { x: 0, y: 0, r: 46 }, sup: { x: 0, y: 0, r: 34 }, ports: [] };
   135	
   136	  var pointers = {};
   137	
   138	  function hitCircle(p, x, y) { var dx = x - p.x, dy = y - p.y; return dx * dx + dy * dy <= p.r * p.r; }
   139	
   140	  function onDown(id, x, y) {
   141	    V.initAudio();
   142	    IN.anyTap = true;
   143	    var L = V.layout;
   144	    if (hitCircle(L.strike, x, y)) { pointers[id] = 'strike'; IN.strike = true; return; }
   145	    if (hitCircle(L.sup, x, y)) { pointers[id] = 'super'; IN.superBtn = true; IN.superEdge = true; return; }
   146	    for (var i = 0; i < L.ports.length; i++) {
   147	      var p = L.ports[i];
   148	      if (x >= p.x - 6 && x <= p.x + p.w + 6 && y >= p.y - 6 && y <= p.y + p.h + 10) {
   149	        pointers[id] = 'ui'; IN.swapReq = i; return;
   150	      }
   151	    }
   152	    if (L.mute && x >= L.mute.x && x <= L.mute.x + L.mute.w && y >= L.mute.y && y <= L.mute.y + L.mute.h) {
   153	      pointers[id] = 'ui'; V.toggleMute(); return;
   154	    }
   155	    if (!IN.stick.on) {
   156	      pointers[id] = 'stick';
   157	      IN.stick.on = true; IN.stick.id = id;
   158	      IN.stick.ox = x; IN.stick.oy = y; IN.stick.x = x; IN.stick.y = y;
   159	      IN.stick.dx = 0; IN.stick.dy = 0; IN.stick.mag = 0;
   160	      return;
   161	    }
   162	    pointers[id] = 'ui';
   163	  }
   164	  function onMove(id, x, y) {
   165	    var r = pointers[id];
   166	    if (r === 'stick' && IN.stick.id === id) {
   167	      IN.stick.x = x; IN.stick.y = y;
   168	      var dx = x - IN.stick.ox, dy = y - IN.stick.oy;
   169	      var m = Math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy), MAXR = 52;
   170	      if (m > MAXR) { // drag the base along
   171	        IN.stick.ox += dx * (1 - MAXR / m);
   172	        IN.stick.oy += dy * (1 - MAXR / m);
   173	        dx = x - IN.stick.ox; dy = y - IN.stick.oy; m = MAXR;
   174	      }
   175	      IN.stick.mag = V.clamp(m / MAXR, 0, 1);
   176	      if (m > 0.001) { IN.stick.dx = dx / (m || 1); IN.stick.dy = dy / (m || 1); }
   177	      if (m < 6) { IN.stick.mag = 0; }
   178	    }
   179	  }
   180	  function onUp(id) {
   181	    var r = pointers[id];
   182	    if (r === 'strike') IN.strike = false;
   183	    else if (r === 'super') IN.superBtn = false;
   184	    else if (r === 'stick') {
   185	      IN.stick.on = false; IN.stick.id = null; IN.stick.mag = 0; IN.stick.dx = 0; IN.stick.dy = 0;
   186	    }
   187	    delete pointers[id];
   188	  }
   189	
   190	  V.bindInput = function (canvas) {
   191	    function rel(e) {
   192	      var r = canvas.getBoundingClientRect();
   193	      return { x: e.clientX - r.left, y: e.clientY - r.top };
   194	    }
   195	    canvas.addEventListener('touchstart', function (e) {
   196	      e.preventDefault();
   197	      var r = canvas.getBoundingClientRect();
   198	      for (var i = 0; i < e.changedTouches.length; i++) {
   199	        var t = e.changedTouches[i];
   200	        onDown(t.identifier, t.clientX - r.left, t.clientY - r.top);
   201	      }
   202	    }, { passive: false });
   203	    canvas.addEventListener('touchmove', function (e) {
   204	      e.preventDefault();
   205	      var r = canvas.getBoundingClientRect();
   206	      for (var i = 0; i < e.changedTouches.length; i++) {
   207	        var t = e.changedTouches[i];
   208	        onMove(t.identifier, t.clientX - r.left, t.clientY - r.top);
   209	      }
   210	    }, { passive: false });
   211	    function end(e) {
   212	      e.preventDefault();
   213	      for (var i = 0; i < e.changedTouches.length; i++) onUp(e.changedTouches[i].identifier);
   214	    }
   215	    canvas.addEventListener('touchend', end, { passive: false });
   216	    canvas.addEventListener('touchcancel', end, { passive: false });
   217	
   218	    var mouseDown = false;
   219	    canvas.addEventListener('mousedown', function (e) { e.preventDefault(); mouseDown = true; var p = rel(e); onDown('m', p.x, p.y); });
   220	    root.addEventListener('mousemove', function (e) { if (!mouseDown) return; var p = rel(e); onMove('m', p.x, p.y); });
   221	    root.addEventListener('mouseup', function () { if (mouseDown) { mouseDown = false; onUp('m'); } });
   222	
   223	    root.addEventListener('keydown', function (e) {
   224	      V.initAudio();
   225	      var k = e.key.toLowerCase();
   226	      if (!IN.keys[k]) {
   227	        if (k === 'k' || k === 'shift') IN.superEdge = true;
   228	        if (k >= '1' && k <= '4') IN.swapReq = parseInt(k, 10) - 1;
   229	        if (k === 'm') V.toggleMute();
   230	        if (k === 'p') IN.pausePress = true;
   231	        IN.anyTap = true;
   232	      }
   233	      IN.keys[k] = true;
   234	      if ([' ', 'arrowup', 'arrowdown', 'arrowleft', 'arrowright'].indexOf(k) >= 0) e.preventDefault();
   235	    });
   236	    root.addEventListener('keyup', function (e) { IN.keys[e.key.toLowerCase()] = false; });
   237	    root.addEventListener('blur', function () { IN.keys = {}; IN.strike = false; IN.superBtn = false; pointers = {}; IN.stick.on = false; IN.stick.mag = 0; });
   238	  };
   239	
   240	  /* keyboard-merged movement vector */
   241	  V.moveVec = function () {
   242	    var k = IN.keys, dx = 0, dy = 0;
   243	    if (k['a'] || k['arrowleft']) dx -= 1;
   244	    if (k['d'] || k['arrowright']) dx += 1;
   245	    if (k['w'] || k['arrowup']) dy -= 1;
   246	    if (k['s'] || k['arrowdown']) dy += 1;
   247	    if (dx || dy) {
   248	      var m = Math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);
   249	      return { x: dx / m, y: dy / m, mag: 1 };
   250	    }
   251	    if (IN.stick.mag > 0.05) return { x: IN.stick.dx, y: IN.stick.dy, mag: IN.stick.mag };
   252	    return { x: 0, y: 0, mag: 0 };
   253	  };
   254	  V.strikeHeld = function () { return IN.strike || !!IN.keys['j'] || !!IN.keys[' ']; };
   255	  V.takeSuper = function () { var s = IN.superEdge; IN.superEdge = false; return s; };
   256	  V.takeSwap = function () { var s = IN.swapReq; IN.swapReq = -1; return s; };
   257	  V.takeTap = function () { var s = IN.anyTap; IN.anyTap = false; return s; };
   258	})(window);
     1	/* Vanguard Four - world, simulation, rendering */
     2	(function (root) {
     3	  'use strict';
     4	  var V = root.V;
     5	  var canvas = document.getElementById('c');
     6	  var ctx = canvas.getContext('2d', { alpha: false });
     7	
     8	  var W = 390, H = 700, SC = 1;
     9	  var AR = { x: 10, y: 88, w: 370, h: 500 };
    10	
    11	  function resize() {
    12	    W = Math.max(280, root.innerWidth);
    13	    H = Math.max(420, root.innerHeight);
    14	    var dpr = Math.min(2, root.devicePixelRatio || 1);
    15	    var lim = 960 / Math.max(W, H);
    16	    SC = Math.min(dpr, dpr * Math.max(0.4, lim));
    17	    if (Math.max(W, H) * SC > 960) SC = 960 / Math.max(W, H);
    18	    canvas.width = Math.round(W * SC);
    19	    canvas.height = Math.round(H * SC);
    20	    canvas.style.width = W + 'px';
    21	    canvas.style.height = H + 'px';
    22	    ctx.setTransform(SC, 0, 0, SC, 0, 0);
    23	
    24	    var band = Math.max(96, Math.min(120, H * 0.17));
    25	    AR.x = 10; AR.y = 88; AR.w = W - 20; AR.h = Math.max(200, H - 88 - band - 6);
    26	
    27	    var L = V.layout;
    28	    L.W = W; L.H = H;
    29	    L.strike = { x: W - 62, y: H - band * 0.5, r: 48 };
    30	    L.sup = { x: W - 158, y: H - band * 0.42, r: 36 };
    31	    L.ports = [];
    32	    var m = 8, gap = 6, pw = (W - m * 2 - gap * 3) / 4, ph = 56;
    33	    for (var i = 0; i < 4; i++) L.ports.push({ x: m + i * (pw + gap), y: 6, w: pw, h: ph });
    34	    L.mute = { x: W - 34, y: 68, w: 26, h: 18 };
    35	  }
    36	
    37	  /* ===================== WORLD ===================== */
    38	  var G = {
    39	    heroes: [], enemies: [], projs: [], strikes: [], bolts: [], wells: [], novas: [],
    40	    arcs: [], spawns: [], ctrl: 0, room: 1, kills: 0, score: 0, best: 0,
    41	    state: 'play', stateT: 0, t: 0, flash: null, hint: 6, banner: null, roomsCleared: 0
    42	  };
    43	  root.G = G;
    44	
    45	  function loadBest() {
    46	    try { G.best = parseInt(root.localStorage.getItem('vanguard4_best') || '0', 10) || 0; } catch (e) { G.best = 0; }
    47	  }
    48	  function saveBest() {
    49	    try { root.localStorage.setItem('vanguard4_best', String(G.best)); } catch (e) { }
    50	  }
    51	
    52	  G.fx = {
    53	    slashArc: function (h, big) {
    54	      G.arcs.push({ x: h.x, y: h.y, a: h.a, r: big ? 62 : 44, arc: big ? 2.5 : 1.5, t: 0.16, max: 0.16, col: h.col, owner: h });
    55	    },
    56	    flash: function (col, t) { G.flash = { col: col, t: t, max: t }; }
    57	  };
    58	
    59	  G.addStrike = function (o) {
    60	    o.life = o.life || 0.1; o.max = o.life; o.hit = [];
    61	    if (o.owner) { o.x = o.owner.x + Math.cos(o.owner.a) * (o.ox || 0); o.y = o.owner.y + Math.sin(o.owner.a) * (o.ox || 0); o.a = o.owner.a; }
    62	    if (o.a === undefined) o.a = 0;
    63	    G.strikes.push(o);
    64	    return o;
    65	  };
    66	  G.addProj = function (o) { o.max = o.life; G.projs.push(o); return o; };
    67	
    68	  G.randomEnemy = function () {
    69	    if (!G.enemies.length) return null;
    70	    return G.enemies[V.randi(0, G.enemies.length)];
    71	  };
    72	  G.chainTargets = function (x, y, rad, n) {
    73	    var out = [], src = { x: x, y: y }, used = {};
    74	    for (var k = 0; k < n; k++) {
    75	      var best = null, bd = rad * rad;
    76	      for (var i = 0; i < G.enemies.length; i++) {
    77	        if (used[i]) continue;
    78	        var e = G.enemies[i], d = V.dist2(src, e);
    79	        if (d < bd) { bd = d; best = i; }
    80	      }
    81	      if (best === null) break;
    82	      used[best] = 1; out.push(G.enemies[best]); src = G.enemies[best];
    83	      rad = 120;
    84	    }
    85	    return out;
    86	  };
    87	
    88	  function meterGain(h, amt) {
    89	    if (h.sup) return;
    90	    h.meter = V.clamp(h.meter + amt, 0, 100);
    91	  }
    92	
    93	  G.damageEnemy = function (e, dmg, src, knock, ang, stun) {
    94	    if (e.dead) return;
    95	    if (e.type === 'bracer' && ang !== undefined) {
    96	      if (Math.abs(V.angDiff(ang + Math.PI, e.a)) < 1.05) dmg *= 0.3;
    97	    }
    98	    e.hp -= dmg;
    99	    e.flash = 0.12;
   100	    if (knock) { e.vx += Math.cos(ang) * knock * (e.def.boss ? 0.18 : 1); e.vy += Math.sin(ang) * knock * (e.def.boss ? 0.18 : 1); }
   101	    if (stun) e.stun = Math.max(e.stun, stun * (e.def.boss ? 0.4 : 1));
   102	    if (src && src.isHero) meterGain(src, dmg * 0.55);
   103	    V.burst(e.x, e.y, 3, '#fff', 130, 0.2, 2);
   104	    V.sfx('hit');
   105	    if (e.hp <= 0) killEnemy(e, src);
   106	  };
   107	
   108	  function killEnemy(e, src) {
   109	    e.dead = true;
   110	    G.kills++;
   111	    G.score += e.def.score;
   112	    V.burst(e.x, e.y, e.def.boss ? 40 : 12, e.def.col, e.def.boss ? 330 : 200, 0.55, e.def.boss ? 6 : 3.5);
   113	    V.ring(e.x, e.y, e.r + 8, e.def.col, 0.35);
   114	    V.shake(e.def.boss ? 14 : 2.5);
   115	    V.sfx('kill');
   116	    if (e.def.boss) { G.banner = { txt: 'WARDEN DOWN', t: 2.0, col: '#e8437a' }; G.fx.flash('#ffffff', 0.25); }
   117	    if (src && src.isHero) meterGain(src, 6);
   118	  }
   119	
   120	  G.damageHero = function (h, dmg, ang, knock) {
   121	    if (h.downed || h.inv > 0) return;
   122	    h.hp -= dmg;
   123	    h.flash = 0.14;
   124	    h.inv = 0.28;
   125	    if (knock) { h.vx += Math.cos(ang) * knock; h.vy += Math.sin(ang) * knock; }
   126	    meterGain(h, dmg * 0.9);
   127	    V.burst(h.x, h.y, 5, '#ff5f5f', 150, 0.25, 2.5);
   128	    if (h.i === G.ctrl) { V.shake(4); G.fx.flash('#ff2f4f', 0.14); }
   129	    V.sfx('hit');
   130	    if (h.hp <= 0) downHero(h);
   131	  };
   132	
   133	  function downHero(h) {
   134	    h.hp = 0; h.downed = true; h.reviveP = 0; h.sup = null; h.vx = 0; h.vy = 0;
   135	    V.sfx('down'); V.shake(7);
   136	    V.ring(h.x, h.y, 30, '#ff4040', 0.5);
   137	    G.banner = { txt: h.name + ' IS DOWN', t: 1.6, col: '#ff6a6a' };
   138	    if (h.i === G.ctrl) {
   139	      var alt = nearestLiving(h);
   140	      if (alt) setCtrl(alt.i, true);
   141	    }
   142	    if (allDown()) gameOver();
   143	  }
   144	
   145	  G.reviveHero = function (h, frac) {
   146	    h.downed = false; h.hp = Math.max(1, Math.round(h.max * (frac || 0.5)));
   147	    h.inv = 1.2; h.reviveP = 0;
   148	    V.ring(h.x, h.y, 34, h.col, 0.5);
   149	    V.burst(h.x, h.y, 14, h.col, 180, 0.45, 3);
   150	    V.sfx('revive');
   151	  };
   152	
   153	  function allDown() {
   154	    for (var i = 0; i < G.heroes.length; i++) if (!G.heroes[i].downed) return false;
   155	    return true;
   156	  }
   157	  function nearestLiving(from) {
   158	    var best = null, bd = 1e9;
   159	    for (var i = 0; i < G.heroes.length; i++) {
   160	      var h = G.heroes[i];
   161	      if (h === from || h.downed) continue;
   162	      var d = V.dist2(from, h);
   163	      if (d < bd) { bd = d; best = h; }
   164	    }
   165	    return best;
   166	  }
   167	  function setCtrl(i, silent) {
   168	    if (i < 0 || i > 3) return;
   169	    var h = G.heroes[i];
   170	    if (!h || h.downed || i === G.ctrl) return;
   171	    G.ctrl = i;
   172	    V.ring(h.x, h.y, 30, h.col, 0.35);
   173	    if (!silent) V.sfx('swap');
   174	    G.banner = { txt: h.name + ' - ' + h.def.title.toUpperCase(), t: 1.1, col: h.col };
   175	  }
   176	
   177	  function gameOver() {
   178	    if (G.state === 'over') return;
   179	    G.state = 'over'; G.stateT = 0;
   180	    if (G.score > G.best) { G.best = G.score; saveBest(); }
   181	    V.sfx('over'); V.shake(14);
   182	    G.fx.flash('#ff2f4f', 0.5);
   183	  }
   184	
   185	  /* ===================== SETUP ===================== */
   186	  function makeHero(i) {
   187	    var d = V.HEROES[i];
   188	    return {
   189	      isHero: true, i: i, def: d, name: d.name, col: d.col, kit: d.kit,
   190	      x: AR.x + AR.w * (0.3 + 0.14 * i), y: AR.y + AR.h * 0.72,
   191	      vx: 0, vy: 0, a: -Math.PI / 2, r: 13,
   192	      hp: d.max, max: d.max, meter: 0, sup: null,
   193	      downed: false, reviveP: 0, cd: 0, combo: 0, comboT: 0,
   194	      dashT: 0, dvx: 0, dvy: 0, inv: 0, flash: 0, stun: 0,
   195	      botT: 0, botJit: Math.random() * 6.28, aiTarget: null
   196	    };
   197	  }
   198	
   199	  function newRun() {
   200	    G.heroes = [makeHero(0), makeHero(1), makeHero(2), makeHero(3)];
   201	    G.enemies = []; G.projs = []; G.strikes = []; G.bolts = []; G.wells = []; G.novas = [];
   202	    G.arcs = []; G.spawns = []; V.parts.length = 0;
   203	    G.ctrl = 0; G.room = 1; G.kills = 0; G.score = 0; G.roomsCleared = 0;
   204	    G.state = 'play'; G.stateT = 0; G.hint = 7; G.flash = null;
   205	    G.banner = { txt: 'ROOM 1', t: 1.4, col: '#cfe6ff' };
   206	    spawnRoom(G.room);
   207	  }
   208	
   209	  function spawnRoom(r) {
   210	    var boss = (r % 5 === 0);
   211	    var pool = ['husk', 'husk', 'skitter'];
   212	    if (r >= 2) pool.push('lobber', 'skitter');
   213	    if (r >= 3) pool.push('bracer', 'husk');
   214	    if (r >= 4) pool.push('sapper', 'lobber');
   215	    if (r >= 7) pool.push('bracer', 'sapper');
   216	    var n = Math.min(26, 4 + Math.floor(r * 1.9));
   217	    if (boss) n = Math.min(12, 3 + Math.floor(r * 0.7));
   218	    var pace = Math.max(0.10, 0.28 - r * 0.010);
   219	    G.spawns = [];
   220	    for (var i = 0; i < n; i++) {
   221	      G.spawns.push({ type: V.pick(pool), t: 0.25 + i * pace + (i > 7 ? (i - 7) * pace * 1.6 : 0), x: 0, y: 0, set: false });
   222	    }
   223	    if (boss) {
   224	      G.spawns.push({ type: 'warden', t: 1.0, x: AR.x + AR.w / 2, y: AR.y + 70, set: true });
   225	      G.banner = { txt: 'WARDEN APPROACHES', t: 2.0, col: '#e8437a' };
   226	      V.sfx('boss');
   227	    }
   228	    for (var j = 0; j < G.spawns.length; j++) {
   229	      var s = G.spawns[j];
   230	      if (s.set) continue;
   231	      var edge = V.randi(0, 4);
   232	      if (edge === 0) { s.x = V.rand(AR.x + 24, AR.x + AR.w - 24); s.y = AR.y + 22; }
   233	      else if (edge === 1) { s.x = AR.x + AR.w - 24; s.y = V.rand(AR.y + 24, AR.y + AR.h - 24); }
   234	      else if (edge === 2) { s.x = V.rand(AR.x + 24, AR.x + AR.w - 24); s.y = AR.y + AR.h - 22; }
   235	      else { s.x = AR.x + 24; s.y = V.rand(AR.y + 24, AR.y + AR.h - 24); }
   236	      s.tel = 0;
   237	    }
   238	  }
   239	
   240	  function spawnEnemy(type, x, y, r) {
   240	  function spawnEnemy(type, x, y, r) {
   241	    var d = V.ENEMIES[type];
   242	    var scale = 1 + (G.room - 1) * 0.21;
   243	    var hp = Math.round(d.hp * (d.boss ? (1 + (G.room / 5 - 1) * 0.85) : scale));
   244	    G.enemies.push({
   245	      type: type, def: d, x: x, y: y, vx: 0, vy: 0, a: Math.PI / 2,
   246	      hp: hp, max: hp, r: d.r, dmg: Math.round(d.dmg * (1 + (G.room - 1) * 0.145)),
   247	      state: 'walk', windT: 0, cd: V.rand(0.3, 1.0), stun: 0, flash: 0,
   248	      dead: false, tgt: null, fuse: type === 'sapper' ? V.rand(7, 10) : 0,
   249	      phase: 0, pcd: 2.0, jit: Math.random() * 6.28, spawnT: 0.35
   250	    });
   251	    V.ring(x, y, 26, d.col, 0.35);
   252	  }
   253	
   254	  /* ===================== UPDATE ===================== */
   255	  function clampToArena(e) {
   256	    var pad = e.r || 10;
   257	    if (e.x < AR.x + pad) { e.x = AR.x + pad; e.vx = Math.abs(e.vx) * 0.3; }
   258	    if (e.x > AR.x + AR.w - pad) { e.x = AR.x + AR.w - pad; e.vx = -Math.abs(e.vx) * 0.3; }
   259	    if (e.y < AR.y + pad) { e.y = AR.y + pad; e.vy = Math.abs(e.vy) * 0.3; }
   260	    if (e.y > AR.y + AR.h - pad) { e.y = AR.y + AR.h - pad; e.vy = -Math.abs(e.vy) * 0.3; }
   261	  }
   262	
   263	  function nearestHero(e) {
   264	    var best = null, bd = 1e9;
   265	    for (var i = 0; i < G.heroes.length; i++) {
   266	      var h = G.heroes[i];
   267	      if (h.downed) continue;
   268	      var d = V.dist2(e, h);
   269	      if (d < bd) { bd = d; best = h; }
   270	    }
   271	    return best;
   272	  }
   273	  function nearestEnemy(p, maxd) {
   274	    var best = null, bd = maxd ? maxd * maxd : 1e9;
   275	    for (var i = 0; i < G.enemies.length; i++) {
   276	      var d = V.dist2(p, G.enemies[i]);
   277	      if (d < bd) { bd = d; best = G.enemies[i]; }
   278	    }
   279	    return best;
   280	  }
   281	
   282	  function tryAttack(h) {
   283	    if (h.cd > 0 || h.stun > 0 || h.downed || h.sup) return;
   284	    h.cd = h.kit.cd;
   285	    h.kit.atk(h, G);
   286	  }
   287	  function trySuper(h) {
   288	    if (h.meter < 100 || h.downed || h.sup) return;
   289	    h.meter = 0;
   290	    h.kit.sup(h, G);
   291	    G.banner = { txt: h.name + ' SUPER', t: 1.1, col: h.col };
   292	  }
   293	
   294	  function updateHero(h, dt, isPlayer) {
   295	    h.cd -= dt; h.comboT -= dt; h.inv -= dt; h.flash -= dt; h.stun -= dt;
   296	    if (h.kit.tick) h.kit.tick(h, G, dt);
   297	
   298	    if (h.downed) {
   299	      h.vx *= 0.85; h.vy *= 0.85;
   300	      h.x += h.vx * dt; h.y += h.vy * dt;
   301	      // revive by nearby living ally
   302	      var helper = null;
   303	      for (var i = 0; i < G.heroes.length; i++) {
   304	        var a = G.heroes[i];
   305	        if (a === h || a.downed) continue;
   306	        if (V.dist2(a, h) < 46 * 46) { helper = a; break; }
   307	      }
   308	      if (helper) {
   309	        h.reviveP += dt / 1.6;
   310	        if (h.reviveP >= 1) { G.reviveHero(h, 0.5); G.banner = { txt: h.name + ' REVIVED', t: 1.2, col: h.col }; }
   311	        else if (Math.random() < 0.25) V.burst(h.x, h.y, 1, h.col, 60, 0.3, 2);
   312	      } else h.reviveP = Math.max(0, h.reviveP - dt * 0.35);
   313	      clampToArena(h);
   314	      return;
   315	    }
   316	
   317	    var mv = { x: 0, y: 0, mag: 0 };
   318	    if (isPlayer) {
   319	      mv = V.moveVec();
   320	      if (V.strikeHeld()) tryAttack(h);
   321	    } else {
   322	      mv = botThink(h, dt);
   323	    }
   324	
   325	    if (h.dashT > 0) {
   326	      h.dashT -= dt;
   327	      h.x += h.dvx * dt; h.y += h.dvy * dt;
   328	      if (Math.random() < 0.6) V.burst(h.x, h.y, 1, h.col, 40, 0.25, 2.5);
   329	    } else if (h.stun <= 0) {
   330	      var sp = h.def.speed * (h.sup ? 0.4 : 1);
   331	      h.vx = V.lerp(h.vx, mv.x * sp * mv.mag, 1 - Math.pow(0.001, dt));
   332	      h.vy = V.lerp(h.vy, mv.y * sp * mv.mag, 1 - Math.pow(0.001, dt));
   333	      if (mv.mag > 0.1) h.a = Math.atan2(mv.y, mv.x);
   334	    }
   335	    // face nearest enemy when standing still / attacking
   336	    if (mv.mag < 0.12) {
   337	      var e = nearestEnemy(h, h.kit.ranged ? 320 : 150);
   338	      if (e) h.a = V.lerp(h.a, h.a + V.angDiff(V.angTo(h, e), h.a), Math.min(1, dt * 14));
   339	    }
   340	    h.x += h.vx * dt; h.y += h.vy * dt;
   341	    h.vx *= Math.pow(0.02, dt); h.vy *= Math.pow(0.02, dt);
   342	    clampToArena(h);
   343	  }
   344	
   345	  function botThink(h, dt) {
   346	    h.botT -= dt;
   347	    var mv = { x: 0, y: 0, mag: 0 };
   348	    // 1. revive priority: closest living hero to a downed ally goes for it
   349	    var target = null, mode = 'fight';
   350	    var bestD = 1e9, downed = null;
   351	    for (var i = 0; i < G.heroes.length; i++) {
   352	      var d = G.heroes[i];
   353	      if (!d.downed) continue;
   354	      var mine = V.dist2(h, d);
   355	      var closer = false;
   356	      for (var j = 0; j < G.heroes.length; j++) {
   357	        var o = G.heroes[j];
   358	        if (o === h || o.downed || o.i === G.ctrl) continue;
   359	        if (V.dist2(o, d) < mine) closer = true;
   360	      }
   361	      if (!closer && mine < bestD) { bestD = mine; downed = d; }
   362	    }
   363	    if (downed) { mode = 'revive'; target = downed; }
   364	    else {
   365	      target = h.aiTarget;
   366	      if (h.botT <= 0 || !target || target.dead || G.enemies.indexOf(target) < 0) {
   367	        h.botT = V.rand(0.6, 1.2);
   368	        // prefer nearest, boss weighted
   369	        var best = null, bd = 1e9;
   370	        for (var k = 0; k < G.enemies.length; k++) {
   371	          var e = G.enemies[k];
   372	          var dd = V.dist2(h, e) * (e.def.boss ? 0.5 : 1) * (e.type === 'sapper' ? 0.55 : 1);
   373	          if (dd < bd) { bd = dd; best = e; }
   374	        }
   375	        target = best; h.aiTarget = best;
   376	      }
   377	    }
   378	    if (!target) {
   379	      // idle: drift toward centre-ish of the pack
   380	      var p = G.heroes[G.ctrl];
   381	      if (p && p !== h && V.dist(h, p) > 90) {
   382	        var ai = V.angTo(h, p);
   383	        return { x: Math.cos(ai), y: Math.sin(ai), mag: 0.7 };
   384	      }
   385	      return mv;
   386	    }
   387	
   388	    var dist = V.dist(h, target);
   389	    var ang = V.angTo(h, target);
   390	    h.a = ang;
   391	
   392	    if (mode === 'revive') {
   393	      if (dist > 32) return { x: Math.cos(ang), y: Math.sin(ang), mag: 1 };
   394	      return mv;
   395	    }
   396	
   397	    // super usage
   398	    if (h.meter >= 100) {
   399	      var near = 0;
   400	      for (var m = 0; m < G.enemies.length; m++) if (V.dist2(h, G.enemies[m]) < 200 * 200) near++;
   401	      if (near >= 3 || (target.def.boss && dist < 260) || G.enemies.length >= 5) trySuper(h);
   402	    }
   403	
   404	    var want = h.kit.prefer;
   405	    h.botJit += dt * 1.4;
   406	    var strafe = Math.sin(h.botJit) * 0.55;
   407	    if (dist > want + 12) {
   408	      mv.x = Math.cos(ang) - Math.sin(ang) * strafe * 0.4;
   409	      mv.y = Math.sin(ang) + Math.cos(ang) * strafe * 0.4;
   410	      mv.mag = 1;
   411	    } else if (dist < want - 34 && h.kit.ranged) {
   412	      mv.x = -Math.cos(ang); mv.y = -Math.sin(ang); mv.mag = 0.9;
   413	    } else {
   414	      mv.x = -Math.sin(ang) * strafe; mv.y = Math.cos(ang) * strafe; mv.mag = Math.abs(strafe);
   415	    }
   416	    var n = Math.sqrt(mv.x * mv.x + mv.y * mv.y) || 1;
   417	    mv.x /= n; mv.y /= n;
   418	
   419	    if (dist < h.kit.botRange) tryAttack(h);
   420	    return mv;
   421	  }
   422	
   423	  function updateEnemy(e, dt) {
   424	    e.flash -= dt; e.stun -= dt; e.cd -= dt;
   425	    if (e.spawnT > 0) { e.spawnT -= dt; e.vx *= 0.8; e.vy *= 0.8; e.x += e.vx * dt; e.y += e.vy * dt; return; }
   426	    var h = nearestHero(e);
   427	    if (!h) { e.vx *= 0.9; e.vy *= 0.9; e.x += e.vx * dt; e.y += e.vy * dt; clampToArena(e); return; }
   428	    var dist = V.dist(e, h), ang = V.angTo(e, h);
   429	    var moving = true, sp = e.def.speed;
   430	
   431	    if (e.stun > 0) { moving = false; }
   432	    else if (e.state === 'wind') {
   433	      e.windT -= dt; moving = false;
   434	      e.a = V.lerp(e.a, e.a + V.angDiff(ang, e.a), Math.min(1, dt * 4));
   435	      if (e.windT <= 0) {
   436	        e.state = 'walk'; e.cd = e.def.atkcd;
   437	        doEnemyAttack(e, h, ang);
   438	      }
   439	    } else {
   440	      e.a = V.lerp(e.a, e.a + V.angDiff(ang, e.a), Math.min(1, dt * 6));
   441	      switch (e.type) {
   442	        case 'lobber':
   443	          if (dist < 150) { e.mvang = ang + Math.PI; sp *= 1.1; }
   444	          else e.mvang = ang;
   445	          if (dist < e.def.reach && e.cd <= 0) { e.state = 'wind'; e.windT = e.def.wind; }
   446	          break;
   447	        case 'sapper':
   448	          e.fuse -= dt; e.mvang = ang; sp *= 1.15;
   449	          if (dist < e.def.reach || e.fuse <= 0) { e.state = 'wind'; e.windT = e.def.wind; e.arming = true; }
   450	          break;
   451	        case 'warden':
   452	          updateWarden(e, h, dist, ang, dt);
   453	          return;
   454	        default:
   455	          e.mvang = ang;
   456	          if (dist < e.def.reach + e.r + h.r - 10 && e.cd <= 0) { e.state = 'wind'; e.windT = e.def.wind; }
   457	          break;
   458	      }
   459	    }
   460	
   461	    if (moving && e.mvang !== undefined) {
   462	      e.jit += dt * 2;
   463	      var jx = Math.cos(e.mvang) + Math.sin(e.mvang) * Math.sin(e.jit) * 0.25;
   464	      var jy = Math.sin(e.mvang) - Math.cos(e.mvang) * Math.sin(e.jit) * 0.25;
   465	      var n = Math.sqrt(jx * jx + jy * jy) || 1;
   466	      e.vx = V.lerp(e.vx, jx / n * sp, 1 - Math.pow(0.005, dt));
   467	      e.vy = V.lerp(e.vy, jy / n * sp, 1 - Math.pow(0.005, dt));
   468	    }
   469	    e.x += e.vx * dt; e.y += e.vy * dt;
   470	    e.vx *= Math.pow(0.06, dt); e.vy *= Math.pow(0.06, dt);
   471	    clampToArena(e);
   472	  }
   473	
   474	  function updateWarden(e, h, dist, ang, dt) {
   475	    e.pcd -= dt;
   476	    if (e.chargeT > 0) {
   477	      e.chargeT -= dt;
   478	      e.x += e.cvx * dt; e.y += e.cvy * dt;
   479	      V.burst(e.x, e.y, 1, e.def.col, 60, 0.3, 4);
   480	      for (var i = 0; i < G.heroes.length; i++) {
   481	        var hh = G.heroes[i];
   482	        if (!hh.downed && V.dist2(e, hh) < (e.r + hh.r + 4) * (e.r + hh.r + 4)) {
   483	          G.damageHero(hh, e.dmg, V.angTo(e, hh), 340);
   484	        }
   485	      }
   486	      clampToArena(e);
   487	      if (e.chargeT <= 0) { e.pcd = 1.4; V.shake(4); }
   488	      return;
   489	    }
   490	    if (e.state === 'wind') {
   491	      e.windT -= dt;
   492	      if (e.windT <= 0) {
   493	        e.state = 'walk';
   494	        if (e.next === 'slam') {
   495	          for (var k = 0; k < 3; k++) {
   496	            (function (kk) {
   497	              setTimeout(function () {
   498	                if (e.dead) return;
   499	                G.addStrike({ x: e.x, y: e.y, r: 70 + kk * 55, arc: 6.3, dmg: 18, knock: 300, life: 0.12, col: e.def.col, team: 0 });
   500	                V.ring(e.x, e.y, 70 + kk * 55, e.def.col, 0.4);
   501	                V.shake(5); V.sfx('heavy');
   502	              }, kk * 190);
   503	            })(k);
   504	          }
   505	        } else if (e.next === 'summon') {
   506	          for (var s = 0; s < 3; s++) {
   507	            var a2 = Math.random() * 6.28;
   508	            spawnEnemy(Math.random() < 0.5 ? 'skitter' : 'husk', V.clamp(e.x + Math.cos(a2) * 60, AR.x + 20, AR.x + AR.w - 20), V.clamp(e.y + Math.sin(a2) * 60, AR.y + 20, AR.y + AR.h - 20));
   509	          }
   510	          V.sfx('boss');
   511	        } else {
   512	          e.chargeT = 0.55;
   513	          e.cvx = Math.cos(ang) * 480; e.cvy = Math.sin(ang) * 480;
   514	          V.sfx('heavy');
   515	        }
   516	        e.pcd = 2.3;
   517	      }
   518	      return;
   519	    }
   520	    if (e.pcd <= 0) {
   521	      e.state = 'wind'; e.windT = e.def.wind;
   522	      var roll = Math.random();
   523	      e.next = dist < 110 ? 'slam' : (roll < 0.4 ? 'charge' : (roll < 0.7 ? 'summon' : 'slam'));
   524	      if (e.next === 'summon' && G.enemies.length > 12) e.next = 'slam';
   525	      return;
   526	    }
   527	    e.a = V.lerp(e.a, e.a + V.angDiff(ang, e.a), Math.min(1, dt * 3));
   528	    e.vx = V.lerp(e.vx, Math.cos(ang) * e.def.speed, 1 - Math.pow(0.02, dt));
   529	    e.vy = V.lerp(e.vy, Math.sin(ang) * e.def.speed, 1 - Math.pow(0.02, dt));
   530	    e.x += e.vx * dt; e.y += e.vy * dt;
   531	    clampToArena(e);
   532	  }
   533	
   534	  function doEnemyAttack(e, h, ang) {
   535	    if (e.type === 'lobber') {
   536	      var a = V.angTo(e, h);
   537	      G.addProj({ x: e.x, y: e.y, vx: Math.cos(a) * 230, vy: Math.sin(a) * 230, r: 7, dmg: e.dmg, team: 0, col: e.def.col, life: 2.0, split: 0 });
   538	      V.sfx('shot');
   539	    } else if (e.type === 'sapper') {
   540	      G.addStrike({ x: e.x, y: e.y, r: 62, arc: 6.3, dmg: e.dmg, knock: 300, life: 0.12, col: '#ff7a3c', team: 0 });
   541	      V.burst(e.x, e.y, 22, '#ff7a3c', 300, 0.5, 4);
   542	      V.ring(e.x, e.y, 62, '#ffb03c', 0.4);
   543	      V.shake(7); V.sfx('heavy');
   544	      e.hp = 0; killEnemy(e, null); G.score -= e.def.score; G.kills--;
   545	      if (G.kills < 0) G.kills = 0;
   546	    } else {
   547	      G.addStrike({ x: e.x + Math.cos(e.a) * (e.r + 8), y: e.y + Math.sin(e.a) * (e.r + 8), r: e.def.reach, arc: 1.9, a: e.a, dmg: e.dmg, knock: 190, life: 0.11, col: e.def.col, team: 0 });
   548	      V.sfx('slash');
   549	    }
   550	  }
   551	
   552	  function updateStrikes(dt) {
   553	    for (var i = G.strikes.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
   554	      var s = G.strikes[i];
   555	      s.life -= dt;
   556	      if (s.owner && s.follow) {
   557	        s.x = s.owner.x + Math.cos(s.owner.a) * (s.ox || 0);
   558	        s.y = s.owner.y + Math.sin(s.owner.a) * (s.ox || 0);
   559	        s.a = s.owner.a;
   560	      }
   680	        if (!h1.downed) { h1.x -= ddx / dd * pp; h1.y -= ddy / dd * pp; }
   681	        if (!h2.downed) { h2.x += ddx / dd * pp; h2.y += ddy / dd * pp; }
   682	      }
   683	    }
   684	  }
   685	
   686	  function update(dt) {
   687	    G.t += dt;
   688	    V.updateParts(dt);
   689	    V.shakeAmt *= Math.pow(0.02, dt);
   690	    if (G.flash) { G.flash.t -= dt; if (G.flash.t <= 0) G.flash = null; }
   691	    if (G.banner) { G.banner.t -= dt; if (G.banner.t <= 0) G.banner = null; }
   692	    if (G.hint > 0) G.hint -= dt;
   693	
   694	    var swap = V.takeSwap();
   695	    var sup = V.takeSuper();
   696	
   697	    if (G.state === 'over') {
   698	      G.stateT += dt;
   699	      if (V.takeTap() && G.stateT > 0.7) newRun();
   700	      // let particles settle
   701	      for (var q = 0; q < G.enemies.length; q++) { G.enemies[q].vx *= 0.9; G.enemies[q].vy *= 0.9; }
   702	      return;
   703	    }
   704	
   705	    if (swap >= 0) setCtrl(swap);
   706	    if (sup) trySuper(G.heroes[G.ctrl]);
   707	
   708	    for (var i = 0; i < G.heroes.length; i++) updateHero(G.heroes[i], dt, i === G.ctrl);
   709	    for (var j = G.enemies.length - 1; j >= 0; j--) {
   710	      if (G.enemies[j].dead) { G.enemies.splice(j, 1); continue; }
   711	      updateEnemy(G.enemies[j], dt);
   712	    }
   713	    separate();
   714	    updateStrikes(dt);
   715	    updateProjs(dt);
   716	    updateFields(dt);
   717	
   718	    // spawn queue
   719	    for (var s = G.spawns.length - 1; s >= 0; s--) {
   720	      var sp = G.spawns[s];
   721	      sp.t -= dt;
   722	      if (sp.t <= 0) { spawnEnemy(sp.type, sp.x, sp.y); G.spawns.splice(s, 1); }
   723	      else if (sp.t < 0.45 && Math.random() < 0.4) V.burst(sp.x, sp.y, 1, '#ffffff', 40, 0.3, 2);
   724	    }
   725	
   726	    if (G.state === 'play') {
   727	      if (!G.enemies.length && !G.spawns.length) {
   728	        G.state = 'clear'; G.stateT = 0;
   729	        G.roomsCleared++;
   730	        G.score += 100 + G.room * 10;
  1010	  function drawPortraits(c) {
  1011	    var L = V.layout;
  1012	    for (var i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
  1013	      var p = L.ports[i], h = G.heroes[i];
  1014	      if (!h) continue;
  1015	      var active = i === G.ctrl;
  1016	      c.fillStyle = active ? 'rgba(255,255,255,0.13)' : 'rgba(255,255,255,0.05)';
  1017	      rr(c, p.x, p.y, p.w, p.h, 7); c.fill();
  1018	      c.strokeStyle = h.downed ? 'rgba(255,80,80,0.85)' : (active ? h.col : 'rgba(255,255,255,0.18)');
  1019	      c.lineWidth = active ? 2.5 : 1.2;
  1020	      rr(c, p.x, p.y, p.w, p.h, 7); c.stroke();
  1021	
  1022	      c.fillStyle = h.downed ? '#ff7676' : h.col;
  1023	      c.font = '700 10px system-ui,sans-serif'; c.textAlign = 'left';
  1024	      c.fillText(h.name, p.x + 6, p.y + 14);
  1025	      c.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.35)';
  1026	      c.font = '600 8px system-ui,sans-serif';
  1027	      c.fillText(String(i + 1), p.x + p.w - 10, p.y + 14);
  1028	
  1029	      // hp bar
  1030	      var bw = p.w - 12;
  1031	      c.fillStyle = 'rgba(0,0,0,0.5)'; rr(c, p.x + 6, p.y + 20, bw, 6, 3); c.fill();
  1032	      if (!h.downed) {
  1033	        c.fillStyle = h.hp / h.max > 0.3 ? '#66e08a' : '#ff5252';
  1034	        rr(c, p.x + 6, p.y + 20, bw * V.clamp(h.hp / h.max, 0, 1), 6, 3); c.fill();
  1035	      } else {
  1036	        c.fillStyle = '#ff5252';
  1037	        rr(c, p.x + 6, p.y + 20, bw * V.clamp(h.reviveP, 0, 1), 6, 3); c.fill();
  1038	      }
  1039	      // super meter
  1040	      c.fillStyle = 'rgba(0,0,0,0.5)'; rr(c, p.x + 6, p.y + 29, bw, 5, 2.5); c.fill();
  1041	      var full = h.meter >= 100;
  1042	      c.fillStyle = full ? (Math.floor(G.t * 8) % 2 ? '#ffffff' : h.col) : 'rgba(180,220,255,0.75)';
  1043	      rr(c, p.x + 6, p.y + 29, bw * V.clamp(h.meter / 100, 0, 1), 5, 2.5); c.fill();
  1044	
  1045	      c.fillStyle = h.downed ? '#ff7676' : 'rgba(255,255,255,0.5)';
  1046	      c.font = '600 8px system-ui,sans-serif'; c.textAlign = 'left';
  1047	      c.fillText(h.downed ? 'DOWNED' : (full ? 'SUPER READY' : h.def.title), p.x + 6, p.y + 45);
  1048	      if (active) {
  1049	        c.fillStyle = h.col;
  1050	        rr(c, p.x + 6, p.y + 49, p.w - 12, 3, 1.5); c.fill();
  1051	      }
  1052	    }
  1053	  }
  1054	
  1055	  function drawHUD(c) {
  1056	    drawPortraits(c);

  1161	  function render() {
  1162	    var c = ctx;
  1163	    c.setTransform(SC, 0, 0, SC, 0, 0);
  1164	    c.fillStyle = '#080b12';
  1165	    c.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
  1166	
  1167	    var sh = V.shakeAmt;
  1168	    c.save();
  1169	    if (sh > 0.3) c.translate(V.rand(-sh, sh) * 0.6, V.rand(-sh, sh) * 0.6);
  1170	
  1171	    drawArena(c);
  1172	    c.save();
  1173	    rr(c, AR.x, AR.y, AR.w, AR.h, 14); c.clip();
  1174	    drawEffects(c);
  1175	    for (var i = 0; i < G.enemies.length; i++) drawEnemy(c, G.enemies[i]);
  1176	    for (var j = 0; j < G.heroes.length; j++) if (G.heroes[j].downed) drawHero(c, G.heroes[j]);
  1177	    for (var k = 0; k < G.heroes.length; k++) if (!G.heroes[k].downed) drawHero(c, G.heroes[k]);
  1178	    V.drawParts(c);
  1179	    c.restore();
  1180	    drawBossBar(c);
  1181	    c.restore();
  1182	
  1183	    if (G.flash) {
  1184	      c.globalAlpha = V.clamp(G.flash.t / G.flash.max, 0, 1) * 0.35;
  1185	      c.fillStyle = G.flash.col;
  1186	      c.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
  1187	      c.globalAlpha = 1;
  1188	    }
  1189	
  1190	    drawHUD(c);
  1191	    drawControls(c);
  1192	    drawOverlays(c);
  1193	  }
  1194	
  1195	  /* ===================== LOOP ===================== */
  1196	  var last = 0, acc = 0;
  1197	  function frame(ts) {
  1198	    if (!last) last = ts;
  1199	    var dt = (ts - last) / 1000;
  1200	    last = ts;
  1201	    if (dt > 0.05) dt = 0.05;
  1202	    if (dt < 0) dt = 0;
  1203	    update(dt);
  1204	    render();
  1205	    requestAnimationFrame(frame);
  1206	  }
  1207	
  1208	  resize();
  1209	  root.addEventListener('resize', function () { resize(); });
  1210	  root.addEventListener('orientationchange', function () { setTimeout(resize, 120); });
  1211	  V.bindInput(canvas);
  1212	  loadBest();
  1213	  newRun();
  1214	  requestAnimationFrame(frame);
  1215	})(window);

   255	  function clampToArena(e) {
   256	    var pad = e.r || 10;
   257	    if (e.x < AR.x + pad) { e.x = AR.x + pad; e.vx = Math.abs(e.vx) * 0.3; }
   258	    if (e.x > AR.x + AR.w - pad) { e.x = AR.x + AR.w - pad; e.vx = -Math.abs(e.vx) * 0.3; }
   259	    if (e.y < AR.y + pad) { e.y = AR.y + pad; e.vy = Math.abs(e.vy) * 0.3; }
   260	    if (e.y > AR.y + AR.h - pad) { e.y = AR.y + AR.h - pad; e.vy = -Math.abs(e.vy) * 0.3; }
   261	  }

   263	  function nearestHero(e) {
   264	    var best = null, bd = 1e9;
   265	    for (var i = 0; i < G.heroes.length; i++) {
   266	      var h = G.heroes[i];

   268	      var d = V.dist2(e, h);
   269	      if (d < bd) { bd = d; best = h; }

   273	  function nearestEnemy(p, maxd) {

   282	  function tryAttack(h) {
   283	    if (h.cd > 0 || h.stun > 0 || h.downed || h.sup) return;
   284	    h.cd = h.kit.cd;
   285	    h.kit.atk(h, G);
   286	  }
   287	  function trySuper(h) {
   288	    if (h.meter < 100 || h.downed || h.sup) return;
   289	    h.meter = 0;

   291	    G.banner = { txt: h.name + ' SUPER', t: 1.1, col: h.col };
   292	  }

   294	  function updateHero(h, dt, isPlayer) {
   295	    h.cd -= dt; h.comboT -= dt; h.inv -= dt; h.flash -= dt; h.stun -= dt;
   296	    if (h.kit.tick) h.kit.tick(h, G, dt);
   297	
   298	    if (h.downed) {
   299	      h.vx *= 0.85; h.vy *= 0.85;
   300	      h.x += h.vx * dt; h.y += h.vy * dt;
   301	      // revive by nearby living ally
   302	      var helper = null;
   303	      for (var i = 0; i < G.heroes.length; i++) {
   304	        var a = G.heroes[i];
   305	        if (a === h || a.downed) continue;
   306	        if (V.dist2(a, h) < 46 * 46) { helper = a; break; }
   307	      }
   308	      if (helper) {
   309	        h.reviveP += dt / 1.6;
   310	        if (h.reviveP >= 1) { G.reviveHero(h, 0.5); G.banner = { txt: h.name + ' REVIVED', t: 1.2, col: h.col }; }
   311	        else if (Math.random() < 0.25) V.burst(h.x, h.y, 1, h.col, 60, 0.3, 2);
   312	      } else h.reviveP = Math.max(0, h.reviveP - dt * 0.35);
   313	      clampToArena(h);
   314	      return;
   315	    }
   316	
   317	    var mv = { x: 0, y: 0, mag: 0 };
   318	    if (isPlayer) {
   319	      mv = V.moveVec();
   320	      if (V.strikeHeld()) tryAttack(h);
   321	    } else {
   322	      mv = botThink(h, dt);
   323	    }
   324	
   325	    if (h.dashT > 0) {
   326	      h.dashT -= dt;
   327	      h.x += h.dvx * dt; h.y += h.dvy * dt;
   328	      if (Math.random() < 0.6) V.burst(h.x, h.y, 1, h.col, 40, 0.25, 2.5);
   329	    } else if (h.stun <= 0) {
   330	      var sp = h.def.speed * (h.sup ? 0.4 : 1);
   331	      h.vx = V.lerp(h.vx, mv.x * sp * mv.mag, 1 - Math.pow(0.001, dt));
   332	      h.vy = V.lerp(h.vy, mv.y * sp * mv.mag, 1 - Math.pow(0.001, dt));
   333	      if (mv.mag > 0.1) h.a = Math.atan2(mv.y, mv.x);
   334	    }
   335	    // face nearest enemy when standing still / attacking
   336	    if (mv.mag < 0.12) {
   337	      var e = nearestEnemy(h, h.kit.ranged ? 320 : 150);
   338	      if (e) h.a = V.lerp(h.a, h.a + V.angDiff(V.angTo(h, e), h.a), Math.min(1, dt * 14));
   339	    }
   340	    h.x += h.vx * dt; h.y += h.vy * dt;
   720	      var sp = G.spawns[s];
   721	      sp.t -= dt;
   722	      if (sp.t <= 0) { spawnEnemy(sp.type, sp.x, sp.y); G.spawns.splice(s, 1); }
   723	      else if (sp.t < 0.45 && Math.random() < 0.4) V.burst(sp.x, sp.y, 1, '#ffffff', 40, 0.3, 2);
   724	    }
   725	
   726	    if (G.state === 'play') {
   727	      if (!G.enemies.length && !G.spawns.length) {
   728	        G.state = 'clear'; G.stateT = 0;
   729	        G.roomsCleared++;
   730	        G.score += 100 + G.room * 10;
   731	        V.sfx('clear');
   732	        G.banner = { txt: 'ROOM CLEAR', t: 1.8, col: '#9ef7c3' };
   733	      }
   734	    } else if (G.state === 'clear') {
   735	      G.stateT += dt;
   736	      if (G.stateT > 2.4) {
   737	        for (var h = 0; h < G.heroes.length; h++) {
   738	          var hero = G.heroes[h];
   739	          var healf = Math.max(0.10, 0.22 - G.room * 0.006);
   740	          if (hero.downed) G.reviveHero(hero, Math.max(0.28, 0.45 - G.room * 0.008));
   741	          else hero.hp = Math.min(hero.max, hero.hp + hero.max * healf);
   742	          hero.meter = Math.min(100, hero.meter + 15);
   743	        }
   744	        if (G.score > G.best) { G.best = G.score; saveBest(); }
   745	        G.room++;
   746	        G.state = 'play';
   747	        spawnRoom(G.room);
   748	        if (G.room % 5 !== 0) G.banner = { txt: 'ROOM ' + G.room, t: 1.3, col: '#cfe6ff' };
   749	      }
   750	    }
   751	
   752	  }

   756	    c.beginPath();
   757	    c.moveTo(x + r, y);
   758	    c.arcTo(x + w, y, x + w, y + h, r);
   759	    c.arcTo(x + w, y + h, x, y + h, r);
   760	    c.arcTo(x, y + h, x, y, r);

    92	function fit() {
    93	  var w = window.innerWidth, h = window.innerHeight;
    94	  var s = Math.min(w / VW, h / VH);
    95	  var cssW = Math.floor(VW * s), cssH = Math.floor(VH * s);
    96	  cv.style.width = cssW + 'px';
    97	  cv.style.height = cssH + 'px';
    98	  var dpr = Math.min(2, window.devicePixelRatio || 1);
    99	  var bw = cssW * dpr, bh = cssH * dpr;
   100	  if (bh > 960) { var k = 960 / bh; bw *= k; bh *= k; }
   101	  cv.width = Math.max(1, Math.round(bw));
   102	  cv.height = Math.max(1, Math.round(bh));
   103	  SCALE = cv.width / VW;
   104	}
   105	window.addEventListener('resize', fit);
   106	window.addEventListener('orientationchange', function () { setTimeout(fit, 120); });
   107	fit();

   119	function toLocal(clientX, clientY) {
   120	  var r = cv.getBoundingClientRect();
   121	  return {
   122	    x: (clientX - r.left) / r.width * VW,
   123	    y: (clientY - r.top) / r.height * VH
   124	  };

   127	function pressAt(x, y) {
   128	  Audio2.init();
   129	  Input.down = true;
   130	  Input.sx = Input.cx = x; Input.sy = Input.cy = y;
   131	  Input.st = performance.now();
   132	  if (Input.onPress) Input.onPress(x, y);
   133	}
   134	function releaseAt(x, y) {
   135	  if (!Input.down) return;
   136	  Input.down = false;
   137	  var dx = x - Input.sx, dy = y - Input.sy;
   138	  var ad = Math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);
   139	  var dir = 'tap';
   140	  if (ad > 22) {
   141	    if (Math.abs(dx) > Math.abs(dy)) dir = dx > 0 ? 'right' : 'left';
   142	    else dir = dy > 0 ? 'down' : 'up';
   143	  }
   144	  if (Input.onTap) Input.onTap(Input.sx, Input.sy, dir, x, y);
   145	  if (Input.onRelease) Input.onRelease(x, y);
   146	}

    72	/* ---------------- wave generation (seeded per night) ---------------- */
    73	function startNight(n) {
    74	  var r = makeRng(1337 + n * 7919);
    75	  var dur = 22 + n * 3.2;
    76	  var ev = [];
    77	  function add(kind, count, t0, t1) {
    78	    for (var i = 0; i < count; i++) {
    79	      var s = Math.floor(r() * 3);
    80	      ev.push({ t: t0 + (t1 - t0) * r(), kind: kind, seg: s });
    81	    }
    82	  }
    83	  add('grunt', 6 + n * 3, 1.0, dur);
    84	  if (n >= 2) add('grap', 1 + (n - 1) * 2, 5, dur);
    85	  if (n >= 4) add('elite', 1 + Math.floor((n - 4) * 1.2), 8, dur);
    86	  if (n >= 3) add('ram', n >= 8 ? 2 : 1, 6, dur * 0.65);
    87	  if (n >= 6) add('tower', n >= 9 ? 2 : 1, 9, dur * 0.7);
    88	  ev.sort(function (a, b) { return a.t - b.t; });
    89	  G.wave = { ev: ev, i: 0, dur: dur };
    90	  G.waveT = 0;
    91	  G.nightTitle = 2.4;
    92	  G.ladders.length = 0;
    93	  G.enemies.length = 0;
    94	  G.shots.length = 0;
    95	  G.pours.length = 0;
    96	  G.gateOil = 100;
    97	}

   100	function segAt(x) { return x < 130 ? 0 : (x < 260 ? 1 : 2); }
   101	function aliveSegs() { var c = 0; for (var i = 0; i < 3; i++) if (G.segs[i].hp > 0) c++; return c; }
   102	function nearestOpenSeg(from) {
   103	  var bi = -1, bd = 99;
   104	  for (var i = 0; i < 3; i++) if (G.segs[i].hp > 0 && Math.abs(i - from) < bd) { bd = Math.abs(i - from); bi = i; }
   105	  return bi;
   106	}
   107	function addPart(x, y, n, col, spd, life) {
   108	  for (var i = 0; i < n; i++) {
   109	    var a = rand(0, Math.PI * 2), s = rand(spd * 0.3, spd);
   110	    G.parts.push({ x: x, y: y, vx: Math.cos(a) * s, vy: Math.sin(a) * s - 20, l: life || rand(0.25, 0.6), m: life || 0.6, c: col, r: rand(1.4, 3.2) });
   111	  }
   112	  if (G.parts.length > 260) G.parts.splice(0, G.parts.length - 260);
   113	}
   114	function floatText(x, y, s, col) {
   115	  G.texts.push({ x: x, y: y, s: s, c: col || '#fff', l: 0.9 });
   116	  if (G.texts.length > 22) G.texts.shift();
   117	}

   123	  grap: { hp: 20, gs: 58, cs: 50, w: 10, h: 20, col: C.grap, dps: 4.5, valor: 4 },
   124	  elite: { hp: 70, gs: 26, cs: 19, w: 14, h: 26, col: C.elite, dps: 10, valor: 9, shield: true },

   129	function spawn(kind, seg) {
   130	  var d = EDEF[kind];
   131	  var hpScale = 1 + (G.night - 1) * 0.12;
   132	  var e = {
   133	    k: kind, x: 0, y: SPAWN_Y + rand(0, 14), hp: d.hp * hpScale, max: d.hp * hpScale,
   134	    st: 'ground', seg: seg, ladder: null, vy: 0, shield: !!d.shield,
   135	    tx: 0, cd: 0, unload: 2.2, wob: rand(0, 6.28), hurt: 0
   136	  };
   137	  if (kind === 'ram') { e.x = 195 + rand(-16, 16); e.tx = 195; e.seg = 1; }
   138	  else { e.x = clamp(SEGC[seg] + rand(-46, 46), 14, VW - 14); e.tx = e.x; }
   139	  if (kind === 'tower') { e.tx = clamp(SEGC[seg] + rand(-20, 20), 40, VW - 40); }
   140	  G.enemies.push(e);
   141	  return e;
   142	}

   144	function findLadder(seg, x) {
   145	  var b = null, bd = 1e9;
   146	  for (var i = 0; i < G.ladders.length; i++) {
   147	    var L = G.ladders[i];
   148	    if (L.hp <= 0) continue;
   149	    var d = Math.abs(L.x - x) + (L.seg === seg ? 0 : 90);
   150	    if (d < bd) { bd = d; b = L; }
   151	  }
   152	  return bd < 150 ? b : null;
   153	}

   155	function plantLadder(x, seg, kind) {
   156	  var L = { x: clamp(x, 16, VW - 16), seg: seg, hp: kind === 'grap' ? 1 : 3, grap: kind === 'grap', build: 0 };
   157	  G.ladders.push(L);
   158	  Audio2.ladder();
   159	  addPart(L.x, GROUND_Y, 8, '#8a7a55', 60);
   160	  return L;
   161	}

   163	function damage(e, dmg, kind) {
   164	  if (e.shield && kind !== 'kick' && kind !== 'banner') dmg *= 0.25;
   165	  e.hp -= dmg;
   166	  e.hurt = 0.12;
   167	  if (e.hp <= 0) killEnemy(e);
   168	}
   169	
   170	function killEnemy(e) {
   171	  e.dead = true;
   172	  var d = EDEF[e.k];
   173	  G.valor += d.valor;
   174	  addPart(e.x, e.y - 8, e.k === 'ram' || e.k === 'tower' ? 26 : 10, d.col, e.k === 'tower' ? 150 : 90);
   175	  if (e.k === 'ram' || e.k === 'tower') { shake(9); floatText(e.x, e.y - 30, '+' + d.valor, C.gold); }
   176	  Audio2.kill();
   177	}

   179	/* ---------------- hero ---------------- */
   180	function heroCommand(x, dir) {
   181	  var h = G.hero;
   182	  var tx = clamp(x, 16, VW - 16);
   183	  var d = Math.abs(tx - h.x);
   184	  h.face = tx >= h.x ? 1 : -1;
   185	  h.leap = { x0: h.x, x1: tx, t: 0, dur: clamp(d / 950, 0.09, 0.30) };
   186	  h.pending = (dir === 'down') ? 'kick' : (dir === 'left' || dir === 'right') ? 'sweep' : 'over';
   187	}
   188	
   189	function doAttack(type) {
   190	  var h = G.hero;
   191	  h.atk = 0.26; h.atkType = type;
   192	  Audio2.swing();
   193	  var hits = 0;
   194	  var R = type === 'sweep' ? 74 : type === 'kick' ? 58 : 48;
   195	  var dmgBase = (type === 'over' ? 38 : type === 'sweep' ? 21 : 16) * G.heroDmg;
   196	
   197	  // enemies on rampart or near the top of the wall face
   198	  for (var i = 0; i < G.enemies.length; i++) {
   199	    var e = G.enemies[i];
   200	    if (e.dead || e.k === 'ram') continue;
   201	    if (e.k === 'tower') {
   202	      if (e.arrived && Math.abs(e.x - h.x) < R + 26) {
   203	        damage(e, dmgBase * 0.7, type); hits++;
   204	        addPart(e.x, WALL_TOP + 10, 6, '#cfd6e6', 90, 0.3);
   205	      }
   206	      continue;
   207	    }
   208	    if (e.st === 'ground' || e.st === 'fall') continue;
   209	    var reach = (e.st === 'top') ? 0 : (e.y - WALL_TOP);
   210	    if (reach > (type === 'kick' ? 84 : 44)) continue;
   211	    if (Math.abs(e.x - h.x) > R) continue;
   212	    hits++;
   213	    if (type === 'kick') {
   214	      if (e.shield) { e.shield = false; addPart(e.x, e.y - 12, 8, '#dcd6ff', 90); floatText(e.x, e.y - 26, 'SHIELD!', '#dcd6ff'); }
   215	      damage(e, dmgBase, type);
   216	      if (!e.dead && e.st === 'climb') { e.st = 'fall'; e.vy = -40; e.ladder = null; }
   217	      else if (!e.dead) { e.x += h.face * 16; }
   218	    } else if (type === 'sweep') {
   219	      damage(e, dmgBase, type);
   220	      if (!e.dead) {
   221	        e.x += (e.x > h.x ? 1 : -1) * 20;
   222	        if (e.st === 'climb' && Math.random() < 0.4) { e.st = 'fall'; e.vy = -20; e.ladder = null; }
   223	      }
   224	    } else {
   225	      damage(e, dmgBase, type);
   226	    }
   227	    addPart(e.x, e.y - 10, 5, type === 'kick' ? '#ffd9a0' : '#fff0c0', 80, 0.3);
   228	  }
   229	
   230	  // kick knocks ladders off the wall
   231	  if (type === 'kick') {
   232	    for (var j = 0; j < G.ladders.length; j++) {
   233	      var L = G.ladders[j];
   234	      if (L.hp <= 0) continue;
   235	      if (Math.abs(L.x - h.x) < R) {
   236	        L.hp = 0; hits++;
   237	        addPart(L.x, WALL_TOP + 30, 14, '#9a854f', 120);
   238	        Audio2.kick();
   239	        floatText(L.x, WALL_TOP + 20, L.grap ? 'ROPE CUT' : 'LADDER DOWN', '#ffd9a0');
   240	        for (var q = 0; q < G.enemies.length; q++) {
   241	          var c = G.enemies[q];
   242	          if (!c.dead && c.st === 'climb' && c.ladder === L) { c.st = 'fall'; c.vy = -30; c.ladder = null; }
   243	        }
   244	      }
   245	    }
   246	  }
   247	
   248	  if (hits > 0) {
   249	    Audio2.hit();
   250	    shake(type === 'over' ? 5 : 3.5);
   251	    h.comboT = 1.6;
   252	    h.combo = Math.min(9, h.combo + 1);
   253	    G.banner = Math.min(100, G.banner + (5 + h.combo * 1.6) * G.bannerMul);
   254	  }
   255	}
   256	
   257	/* ---------------- rally / squads ---------------- */
   258	function orderSquad(i, seg) {
   259	  var s = G.squads[i];
   260	  if (s.seg === seg && s.moveTo < 0) return;
   261	  s.moveTo = seg; s.moveT = 1.0;
   262	  Audio2.rally();
   263	  floatText(SEGC[seg], WALL_TOP - 40, s.type.toUpperCase() + ' →', C[s.type]);
   264	}
   265	
   266	function bannerBurst() {
   267	  if (G.banner < 100) return;
   268	  G.banner = 0;
   269	  Audio2.banner();
   270	  shake(14); flash = 0.5;
   271	  for (var i = 0; i < 3; i++) G.pours.push({ seg: i, t: 1.1, big: true });
   272	  for (var j = 0; j < G.enemies.length; j++) {
   273	    var e = G.enemies[j];
   274	    if (e.dead) continue;
   275	    if (e.st === 'climb') { e.st = 'fall'; e.vy = -50; e.ladder = null; damage(e, 40, 'banner'); }
   276	    else damage(e, e.k === 'ram' || e.k === 'tower' ? 55 : 45, 'banner');
   277	  }
   278	  for (var k = 0; k < G.ladders.length; k++) if (G.ladders[k].grap) G.ladders[k].hp = 0;
   279	  floatText(195, 200, 'RALLY!', C.gold);
   280	}
   281	
   282	function gateMash(x, y) {
   283	  if (G.gateOil < 16) { return; }
   284	  G.gateOil -= 16;
   285	  Audio2.oil();
   286	  G.pours.push({ gate: true, t: 0.45 });
   287	  addPart(rand(GX0, GX1), GY0, 10, C.oil, 110, 0.5);
   288	  shake(3);
   289	  var hit = false;
   290	  for (var i = 0; i < G.enemies.length; i++) {
   291	    var e = G.enemies[i];
   292	    if (e.dead) continue;
   293	    if (e.y > GROUND_Y - 30 && e.x > GX0 - 40 && e.x < GX1 + 40) { damage(e, 24 * G.heroDmg, 'oil'); hit = true; }
   294	  }
   295	  if (hit) { G.banner = Math.min(100, G.banner + 3 * G.bannerMul); }
   296	}
   297	
   298	/* ---------------- update ---------------- */
   299	function update(dt) {
   300	  if (G.shake > 0) G.shake = Math.max(0, G.shake - dt * 34);
   301	  if (flash > 0) flash = Math.max(0, flash - dt * 1.6);
   302	  if (hint > 0) hint -= dt;
   303	  if (G.nightTitle > 0) G.nightTitle -= dt;
   304	
   305	  for (var t = G.texts.length - 1; t >= 0; t--) {
   306	    var T = G.texts[t]; T.l -= dt; T.y -= dt * 22; if (T.l <= 0) G.texts.splice(t, 1);
   307	  }
   308	  for (var p = G.parts.length - 1; p >= 0; p--) {
   309	    var P = G.parts[p];
   310	    P.l -= dt; P.x += P.vx * dt; P.y += P.vy * dt; P.vy += 320 * dt;
   311	    if (P.l <= 0) G.parts.splice(p, 1);
   312	  }
   313	  for (var s = G.shots.length - 1; s >= 0; s--) {
   314	    var S = G.shots[s]; S.t += dt * S.sp;
   315	    if (S.t >= 1) G.shots.splice(s, 1);
   316	  }
   317	  for (var o = G.pours.length - 1; o >= 0; o--) { G.pours[o].t -= dt; if (G.pours[o].t <= 0) G.pours.splice(o, 1); }
   318	
   319	  if (G.mode !== 'play') return;
   320	
   321	  G.gateOil = Math.min(100, G.gateOil + dt * 15);
   322	
   323	  /* --- keyboard movement --- */
   324	  var h = G.hero;
   325	  var kx = 0;
   326	  if (Input.keys['arrowleft'] || Input.keys['a']) kx -= 1;
   327	  if (Input.keys['arrowright'] || Input.keys['d']) kx += 1;
   328	  if (kx !== 0 && !h.leap) { h.x = clamp(h.x + kx * 250 * dt, 16, VW - 16); h.face = kx; }
   329	
   330	  if (h.leap) {
   331	    h.leap.t += dt;
   332	    var k = clamp(h.leap.t / h.leap.dur, 0, 1);
   333	    h.x = lerp(h.leap.x0, h.leap.x1, k);
   334	    h.y = WALL_TOP - Math.sin(k * Math.PI) * Math.min(30, Math.abs(h.leap.x1 - h.leap.x0) * 0.28);
   335	    if (k >= 1) { h.leap = null; h.y = WALL_TOP; if (h.pending) { doAttack(h.pending); h.pending = null; } }
   336	  }
   337	  if (h.atk > 0) h.atk -= dt;
   338	  if (h.comboT > 0) { h.comboT -= dt; if (h.comboT <= 0) h.combo = 0; }
   339	
   340	  /* --- wave spawns --- */
   340	  /* --- wave spawns --- */
   341	  G.waveT += dt;
   342	  var w = G.wave;
   343	  while (w.i < w.ev.length && w.ev[w.i].t <= G.waveT) {
   344	    var ev = w.ev[w.i++];
   345	    spawn(ev.kind, ev.seg);
   346	  }
   347	
   348	  /* --- ladders --- */
   349	  for (var li = G.ladders.length - 1; li >= 0; li--) if (G.ladders[li].hp <= 0) G.ladders.splice(li, 1);
   350	
   351	  /* --- enemies --- */
   352	  G.ramMash = 0;
   353	  for (var i = G.enemies.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
   354	    var e = G.enemies[i];
   355	    if (e.dead) { G.enemies.splice(i, 1); continue; }
   356	    if (e.hurt > 0) e.hurt -= dt;
   357	    var d = EDEF[e.k];
   358	
   359	    if (e.k === 'ram') {
   360	      if (e.y > GROUND_Y - 6) { e.y -= d.gs * dt; e.x += (e.tx - e.x) * Math.min(1, dt * 1.6); }
   361	      else {
   362	        e.y = GROUND_Y - 6;
   363	        G.ramMash = 1;
   364	        G.gate.hp -= d.dps * dt * (1 + (G.night - 1) * 0.05);
   365	        e.wob += dt * 6;
   366	        if (Math.random() < dt * 2.2) { addPart(195, GY0 + 6, 4, '#c9a06a', 70); shake(2.2); Audio2.gate(); }
   367	        if (G.gate.hp <= 0) { G.gate.hp = 0; loseGame(); return; }
   368	      }
   369	      continue;
   370	    }
   371	
   372	    if (e.k === 'tower') {
   373	      if (!e.arrived) {
   374	        e.y -= d.gs * dt;
   375	        e.x += (e.tx - e.x) * Math.min(1, dt * 1.2);
   376	        if (e.y <= GROUND_Y + 4) { e.arrived = true; e.y = GROUND_Y + 4; shake(6); }
   377	      } else {
   378	        e.unload -= dt;
   379	        if (e.unload <= 0) {
   380	          e.unload = 3.0;
   381	          var el = spawn(G.night >= 7 ? 'elite' : 'grunt', segAt(e.x));
   382	          el.st = 'top'; el.y = WALL_TOP; el.x = clamp(e.x + rand(-14, 14), 14, VW - 14);
   383	          addPart(el.x, WALL_TOP, 8, '#cfd6e6', 70);
   384	        }
   385	      }
   386	      continue;
   387	    }
   388	
   389	    if (e.st === 'ground') {
   390	      // walk up to the wall base then to a ladder / plant one
   391	      if (e.y > GROUND_Y) {
   392	        e.y -= d.gs * dt;
   393	        e.x += (e.tx - e.x) * Math.min(1, dt * 1.3);
   394	      } else {
   395	        e.y = GROUND_Y;
   396	        var L = findLadder(e.seg, e.x);
   397	        if (!L) {
   398	          if (e.k === 'grap') { L = plantLadder(e.x, e.seg, 'grap'); }
   399	          else {
   400	            e.build = (e.build || 0) + dt;
   401	            if (e.build > 0.7) L = plantLadder(e.x, e.seg, 'grunt');
   402	          }
   403	        }
   404	        if (L) {
   405	          if (Math.abs(e.x - L.x) > 3) e.x += Math.sign(L.x - e.x) * Math.min(Math.abs(L.x - e.x), d.gs * dt);
   406	          else { e.st = 'climb'; e.ladder = L; e.off = rand(-4, 4); }
   407	        }
   408	      }
   409	      continue;
   410	    }
   411	
   412	    if (e.st === 'climb') {
   413	      if (!e.ladder || e.ladder.hp <= 0) { e.st = 'fall'; e.vy = 0; continue; }
   414	      e.x = e.ladder.x + (e.off || 0);
   415	      e.y -= d.cs * dt;
   416	      if (e.y <= WALL_TOP) { e.y = WALL_TOP; e.st = 'top'; e.ladder = null; }
   417	      continue;
   418	    }
   419	
   420	    if (e.st === 'fall') {
   421	      e.vy += 620 * dt;
   422	      e.y += e.vy * dt;
   423	      if (e.y >= GROUND_Y) {
   424	        damage(e, 26, 'fall');
   425	        if (!e.dead) { e.st = 'ground'; e.y = GROUND_Y; e.tx = e.x; e.build = 0; }
   426	        else { addPart(e.x, GROUND_Y, 10, d.col, 90); }
   427	      }
   428	      continue;
   429	    }
   430	
   431	    if (e.st === 'top') {
   432	      var sg = segAt(e.x);
   433	      if (G.segs[sg].hp > 0) {
   434	        G.segs[sg].hp -= d.dps * dt;
   435	        e.wob += dt * 8;
   436	        if (Math.random() < dt * 1.4) addPart(e.x, WALL_TOP + 6, 3, '#9aa2b8', 50, 0.3);
   437	        if (G.segs[sg].hp <= 0) {
   438	          G.segs[sg].hp = 0;
   439	          shake(11); Audio2.gate();
   440	          floatText(SEGC[sg], WALL_TOP - 30, 'BREACHED', C.bad);
   441	          addPart(SEGC[sg], WALL_TOP + 10, 24, '#6b7385', 160);
   442	          if (aliveSegs() === 0) { loseGame(); return; }
   443	        }
   444	      } else {
   445	        var tsg = nearestOpenSeg(sg);
   446	        if (tsg >= 0) {
   447	          var tx = SEGC[tsg];
   448	          e.x += Math.sign(tx - e.x) * 34 * dt;
   449	        }
   450	      }
   451	    }
   452	  }
   453	
   454	  /* --- squads --- */
   455	  for (var q = 0; q < G.squads.length; q++) {
   456	    var sq = G.squads[q];
   457	    if (sq.moveTo >= 0) {
   458	      sq.moveT -= dt;
   459	      if (sq.moveT <= 0) { sq.seg = sq.moveTo; sq.moveTo = -1; }
   460	      continue;
   461	    }
   462	    sq.cd -= dt;
   463	    if (sq.cd > 0) continue;
   464	    var lo = SEG[sq.seg][0], hi = SEG[sq.seg][1];
   465	    if (sq.type === 'spear') {
   466	      sq.cd = 0.55;
   467	      var tgt = null;
   468	      for (var a = 0; a < G.enemies.length; a++) {
   469	        var en = G.enemies[a];
   470	        if (en.dead || en.k === 'ram' || en.k === 'tower') continue;
   471	        if (en.x < lo || en.x > hi) continue;
   472	        if (en.st === 'top' || (en.st === 'climb' && en.y < WALL_TOP + 62)) { tgt = en; break; }
   473	      }
   474	      if (tgt) {
   475	        damage(tgt, 9 + 6 * G.squadLvl, 'spear');
   476	        G.shots.push({ x0: SEGC[sq.seg], y0: WALL_TOP - 6, x1: tgt.x, y1: tgt.y - 8, t: 0, sp: 7, c: C.spear, w: 2.5 });
   477	      }
   478	    } else if (sq.type === 'archer') {
   479	      sq.cd = 0.7;
   480	      var t2 = null, bd = 1e9;
   481	      for (var b = 0; b < G.enemies.length; b++) {
   482	        var e2 = G.enemies[b];
   483	        if (e2.dead) continue;
   484	        if (e2.x < lo - 20 || e2.x > hi + 20) continue;
   485	        if (e2.st !== 'ground' && e2.k !== 'ram' && e2.k !== 'tower') continue;
   486	        var dd = Math.abs(e2.x - SEGC[sq.seg]);
   487	        if (dd < bd) { bd = dd; t2 = e2; }
   488	      }
   489	      if (t2) {
   490	        damage(t2, 7 + 5 * G.squadLvl, 'arrow');
   491	        G.shots.push({ x0: SEGC[sq.seg], y0: WALL_TOP - 8, x1: t2.x, y1: t2.y - 8, t: 0, sp: 3.2, c: C.archer, w: 2, arc: 1 });
   492	      }
   493	    } else {
   494	      sq.cd = 4.6;
   495	      var any = false;
   496	      for (var cc = 0; cc < G.enemies.length; cc++) {
   497	        var e3 = G.enemies[cc];
   498	        if (e3.dead) continue;
   499	        if (e3.x < lo - 12 || e3.x > hi + 12) continue;
   500	        if (e3.st === 'climb') { damage(e3, 22 + 12 * G.squadLvl, 'oil'); any = true; if (Math.random() < 0.5 && !e3.dead) { e3.st = 'fall'; e3.vy = 0; e3.ladder = null; } }
   501	        else if (e3.st === 'ground' && e3.y <= GROUND_Y + 6) { damage(e3, 12 + 7 * G.squadLvl, 'oil'); any = true; }
   502	      }
   503	      if (any) { G.pours.push({ seg: sq.seg, t: 0.8 }); Audio2.oil(); }
   504	      else sq.cd = 1.2;
   505	    }
   506	  }
   507	
   508	  /* --- night complete? --- */
   509	  if (G.wave.i >= G.wave.ev.length && G.enemies.length === 0) {
   510	    G.valor += 20 + G.night * 10;
   511	    if (G.night > best) { best = G.night; saveBest(best); }
   512	    if (G.night >= TOTAL_NIGHTS) { G.mode = 'win'; Audio2.win(); if (TOTAL_NIGHTS > best) { best = TOTAL_NIGHTS; saveBest(best); } }
   513	    else { G.mode = 'shop'; selChip = -1; Audio2.rally(); }
   514	  }
   515	}
   516	
   517	function loseGame() {
   518	  G.mode = 'lose';
   519	  var score = G.night - 1;
   520	  if (score > best) { best = score; saveBest(best); }
   521	  shake(16); flash = 0.6;
   522	  Audio2.lose();
   523	}
   524	
   525	/* ---------------- drawing ---------------- */
   526	function rr(x, y, w, h, r) {
   527	  ctx.beginPath();
   528	  r = Math.min(r, w / 2, h / 2);
   529	  ctx.moveTo(x + r, y);
   530	  ctx.arcTo(x + w, y, x + w, y + h, r);
   990	  var bf = G.banner / 100;
   991	  bar(8, 48, 304, 6, bf, bf >= 1 ? C.gold : '#6d5fd6');
   992	  txt('BANNER', 340, 51, 9, bf >= 1 ? C.gold : '#7d859b');
   993	
   994	  // wave progress
   995	  var wp = G.wave ? clamp(G.waveT / G.wave.dur, 0, 1) : 0;
   996	  ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,.12)';
   997	  ctx.fillRect(0, HUD_H - 3, VW * wp, 2);
   998	}
   999	
  1000	var BTN = [];
  1001	function drawBar(time) {
  1002	  BTN.length = 0;
  1003	  ctx.fillStyle = '#080b16';
  1004	  ctx.fillRect(0, BAR_Y, VW, VH - BAR_Y);
  1005	  ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,.07)';
  1006	  ctx.fillRect(0, BAR_Y, VW, 1);
  1007	
  1008	  var w = 92, gap = 5, x0 = 6, y = BAR_Y + 6, h = 56;
  1009	  for (var i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
  1010	    var sq = G.squads[i];
  1011	    var x = x0 + i * (w + gap);
  1012	    BTN.push({ x: x, y: y, w: w, h: h, kind: 'chip', i: i });
  1013	    var sel = selChip === i;
  1014	    ctx.fillStyle = sel ? 'rgba(255,255,255,.16)' : 'rgba(255,255,255,.06)';
  1015	    rr(x, y, w, h, 8); ctx.fill();
  1016	    ctx.strokeStyle = sel ? C[sq.type] : 'rgba(255,255,255,.14)';
  1017	    ctx.lineWidth = sel ? 2.5 : 1;
  1018	    rr(x, y, w, h, 8); ctx.stroke();
  1019	    txt(sq.type.toUpperCase(), x + w / 2, y + 17, 13, C[sq.type]);
  1020	    var segn = (sq.moveTo >= 0 ? sq.moveTo : sq.seg) + 1;
  1021	    txt('WALL ' + segn + (sq.moveTo >= 0 ? ' …' : ''), x + w / 2, y + 35, 10, '#aeb6cc');
  1022	    if (sq.type === 'oil') {
  1023	      bar(x + 14, y + 45, w - 28, 4, 1 - clamp(sq.cd / 4.6, 0, 1), C.oil);
  1024	    } else {
  1025	      txt('R' + G.squadLvl, x + w / 2, y + 46, 9, '#7d859b');
  1026	    }
  1027	  }
  1028	  // banner button
  1029	  var bx = x0 + 3 * (w + gap);
  1030	  BTN.push({ x: bx, y: y, w: VW - bx - 6, h: h, kind: 'banner' });
  1031	  var full = G.banner >= 100;
  1032	  ctx.fillStyle = full ? 'rgba(255,209,102,' + (0.25 + 0.15 * Math.sin(time * 8)) + ')' : 'rgba(255,255,255,.05)';
  1033	  rr(bx, y, VW - bx - 6, h, 8); ctx.fill();
  1034	  ctx.strokeStyle = full ? C.gold : 'rgba(255,255,255,.12)';
  1035	  ctx.lineWidth = full ? 2.5 : 1;
  1036	  rr(bx, y, VW - bx - 6, h, 8); ctx.stroke();
  1037	  txt('RALLY', bx + (VW - bx - 6) / 2, y + 22, 13, full ? C.gold : '#5f6780');
  1038	  txt(full ? 'READY!' : Math.floor(G.banner) + '%', bx + (VW - bx - 6) / 2, y + 40, 10, full ? C.gold : '#5f6780');
  1039	
  1040	  // segment targeting overlay when a chip is selected
  1041	  if (selChip >= 0 && G.mode === 'play') {
  1042	    for (var s = 0; s < 3; s++) {
  1043	      var lo = SEG[s][0], sw = SEG[s][1] - SEG[s][0];
  1044	      ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,.07)';
  1045	      ctx.fillRect(lo + 3, HUD_H + 6, sw - 6, GROUND_Y - HUD_H - 12);
  1046	      ctx.strokeStyle = C[G.squads[selChip].type]; ctx.lineWidth = 2;
  1047	      ctx.setLineDash([6, 5]);
  1048	      ctx.strokeRect(lo + 3, HUD_H + 6, sw - 6, GROUND_Y - HUD_H - 12);
  1049	      ctx.setLineDash([]);
  1050	      txt('WALL ' + (s + 1), SEGC[s], HUD_H + 24, 15, C[G.squads[selChip].type]);
  1051	    }
  1052	    txt('tap a wall to send ' + G.squads[selChip].type.toUpperCase(), VW / 2, GROUND_Y + 24, 12, '#e7ecff');
  1053	  }
  1054	}
  1055	
  1056	/* ---------------- shop / end screens ---------------- */
  1057	function drawShop() {
  1058	  ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(6,8,16,.90)';
  1059	  ctx.fillRect(0, 0, VW, VH);
  1060	  txt('NIGHT ' + G.night + ' HELD', VW / 2, 74, 26, C.gold);
  1061	  txt('VALOR: ' + Math.floor(G.valor), VW / 2, 104, 15, '#dfe5f5');
  1062	  txt('spend before the horns sound again', VW / 2, 124, 11, '#8f97ad');
  1063	
  1064	  shopBtns.length = 0;
  1065	  var y = 150;
  1066	  for (var i = 0; i < UP_DEFS.length; i++) {
  1067	    var u = UP_DEFS[i], cost = G.upCost[u.k];
  1068	    var can = G.valor >= cost;
  1069	    var b = { x: 24, y: y, w: VW - 48, h: 62, k: u.k, cost: cost, can: can };
  1070	    shopBtns.push(b);
  1071	    ctx.fillStyle = can ? 'rgba(255,255,255,.08)' : 'rgba(255,255,255,.03)';
  1072	    rr(b.x, b.y, b.w, b.h, 10); ctx.fill();
  1073	    ctx.strokeStyle = can ? C.gold : 'rgba(255,255,255,.10)';
  1074	    ctx.lineWidth = can ? 2 : 1;
  1075	    rr(b.x, b.y, b.w, b.h, 10); ctx.stroke();
  1076	    txt(u.name, b.x + 14, b.y + 21, 14, can ? '#fff' : '#5f6780', 'left');
  1077	    txt(u.desc, b.x + 14, b.y + 41, 11, can ? '#aeb6cc' : '#4e556b', 'left');
  1078	    txt(cost + 'v', b.x + b.w - 14, b.y + 31, 15, can ? C.gold : '#5f6780', 'right');
  1079	    y += 70;
  1080	  }
  1081	  var cb = { x: 24, y: 552, w: VW - 48, h: 58, k: 'go' };
  1082	  shopBtns.push(cb);
  1083	  ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(216,80,70,.22)';
  1084	  rr(cb.x, cb.y, cb.w, cb.h, 10); ctx.fill();
  1085	  ctx.strokeStyle = C.bad; ctx.lineWidth = 2;
  1086	  rr(cb.x, cb.y, cb.w, cb.h, 10); ctx.stroke();
  1087	  txt('BEGIN NIGHT ' + (G.night + 1), VW / 2, 581, 17, '#ffd8d2');
  1088	  txt(nightBlurb(G.night + 1), VW / 2, 624, 11, '#8f97ad');
  1089	}
  1090	
  1091	function drawEnd(won) {
  1092	  ctx.fillStyle = won ? 'rgba(10,20,14,.92)' : 'rgba(16,6,8,.92)';
  1093	  ctx.fillRect(0, 0, VW, VH);
  1094	  var score = won ? TOTAL_NIGHTS : G.night - 1;

  1177	Input.onKey = function (k) {

  1179	    if (k === ' ' || k === 'enter' || k === 'r') newGame();

  1186	      var b = shopBtns[idx];

  1188	    }
  1189	    return;
  1190	  }

  1192	  else if (k === 'arrowdown' || k === 's') doAttack('kick');
  1193	  else if (k === 'arrowup' || k === 'w') doAttack('sweep');

  1197	    var seg = k === 'q' ? 0 : (k === 'e' ? 1 : 2);
  1198	    if (selChip >= 0) { orderSquad(selChip, seg); selChip = -1; }
  1199	  }

  1203	var last = 0;
  1204	function frame(ms) {
  1205	  var t = ms / 1000;
  1206	  var dt = Math.min(0.05, t - last || 0.016);
  1207	  last = t;
  1208	  update(dt);
  1209	  draw(t);
  1210	  requestAnimationFrame(frame);

     2	var SS = (function () {
     3	  'use strict';
     4	
     5	  /* ---------- deterministic rng ---------- */
     6	  function rng(seed) {
     7	    var s = (seed >>> 0) || 1;
     8	    return function () {
     9	      s ^= s << 13; s >>>= 0;
    10	      s ^= s >> 17;
    11	      s ^= s << 5; s >>>= 0;
    12	      return s / 4294967296;
    13	    };
    14	  }
    15	  function hashSeed(str) {
    16	    var h = 2166136261 >>> 0;
    17	    for (var i = 0; i < str.length; i++) { h ^= str.charCodeAt(i); h = Math.imul(h, 16777619) >>> 0; }
    18	    return h >>> 0;
    19	  }
    20	
    21	  /* ---------- small math ---------- */
    22	  var clamp = function (v, a, b) { return v < a ? a : v > b ? b : v; };
    23	  var lerp = function (a, b, t) { return a + (b - a) * t; };
    24	  var TAU = Math.PI * 2;
    25	
    26	  function closestOnSeg(px, py, x1, y1, x2, y2) {
    27	    var dx = x2 - x1, dy = y2 - y1, l2 = dx * dx + dy * dy, t = 0;
    28	    if (l2 > 1e-9) t = ((px - x1) * dx + (py - y1) * dy) / l2;
    29	    t = t < 0 ? 0 : t > 1 ? 1 : t;
    30	    return { x: x1 + t * dx, y: y1 + t * dy, t: t };
    31	  }
    32	
    33	  /* ellipse arc -> polyline points */
    34	  function arcPts(cx, cy, rx, ry, a0, a1, n) {
    35	    var out = [];
    36	    for (var i = 0; i <= n; i++) {
    37	      var a = lerp(a0, a1, i / n);
    38	      out.push([cx + rx * Math.cos(a), cy + ry * Math.sin(a)]);
    39	    }
    40	    return out;
    41	  }
    42	
    43	  /* catmull-rom through points -> dense polyline */
    44	  function smooth(pts, per) {
    45	    per = per || 8;
    46	    var p = [pts[0]].concat(pts, [pts[pts.length - 1]]), out = [];
    47	    for (var i = 1; i < p.length - 2; i++) {
    48	      for (var j = 0; j < per; j++) {
    49	        var t = j / per, t2 = t * t, t3 = t2 * t;
    50	        var x = 0.5 * ((2 * p[i][0]) + (-p[i - 1][0] + p[i + 1][0]) * t +
    51	          (2 * p[i - 1][0] - 5 * p[i][0] + 4 * p[i + 1][0] - p[i + 2][0]) * t2 +
    52	          (-p[i - 1][0] + 3 * p[i][0] - 3 * p[i + 1][0] + p[i + 2][0]) * t3);
    53	        var y = 0.5 * ((2 * p[i][1]) + (-p[i - 1][1] + p[i + 1][1]) * t +
    54	          (2 * p[i - 1][1] - 5 * p[i][1] + 4 * p[i + 1][1] - p[i + 2][1]) * t2 +
    55	          (-p[i - 1][1] + 3 * p[i][1] - 3 * p[i + 1][1] + p[i + 2][1]) * t3);
    56	        out.push([x, y]);
    57	      }
    58	    }
    59	    out.push(pts[pts.length - 1]);
    60	    return out;
    61	  }
    62	
    63	  /* ---------- static geometry builders ---------- */
    64	  function seg(list, x1, y1, x2, y2, opt) {
    65	    var s = { x1: x1, y1: y1, x2: x2, y2: y2, r: 4, rest: 0.32, kind: 'wall' };
    66	    if (opt) for (var k in opt) s[k] = opt[k];
    67	    list.push(s);
    68	    return s;
    69	  }
    70	  function chain(list, pts, opt) {
    71	    for (var i = 0; i < pts.length - 1; i++)
    72	      seg(list, pts[i][0], pts[i][1], pts[i + 1][0], pts[i + 1][1], opt);
    73	  }
    74	
    75	  /* ---------- collision ---------- */
    76	  // returns null or {nx,ny,speed,obj}
    77	  function hitSeg(b, s) {
    78	    if (s.down) return null;
    79	    var c = closestOnSeg(b.x, b.y, s.x1, s.y1, s.x2, s.y2);
    80	    var nx = b.x - c.x, ny = b.y - c.y;
    81	    var d = Math.sqrt(nx * nx + ny * ny);
    82	    var R = b.r + (s.r || 0);
    83	    if (d >= R) return null;
    84	    if (d < 1e-6) { nx = 0; ny = -1; d = 1e-6; } else { nx /= d; ny /= d; }
    85	    if (s.oneWay && (b.vx * s.oneWay.x + b.vy * s.oneWay.y) > 0) return null;
    86	    b.x += nx * (R - d + 0.05);
    87	    b.y += ny * (R - d + 0.05);
    88	    var vn = b.vx * nx + b.vy * ny;
    89	    if (vn > 0) return null;
    90	    var e = s.rest;
    91	    if (e === undefined) e = 0.32;
    92	    var impact = -vn;
    93	    b.vx -= (1 + e) * vn * nx;
    94	    b.vy -= (1 + e) * vn * ny;
    95	    // tangential friction
    96	    var tx = -ny, ty = nx;
    97	    var vt = b.vx * tx + b.vy * ty;
    98	    var f = s.fric === undefined ? 0.02 : s.fric;
    99	    b.vx -= vt * f * tx; b.vy -= vt * f * ty;
   100	    if (s.kick) {
   101	      var k = s.kick;
   102	      b.vx = nx * k + tx * (vt * 0.25);
   103	      b.vy = ny * k + ty * (vt * 0.25);
   104	    }
   105	    return { nx: nx, ny: ny, speed: impact, obj: s };
   106	  }
   107	
   108	  function hitCircle(b, c) {
   109	    if (c.down) return null;
   110	    var nx = b.x - c.x, ny = b.y - c.y;
   111	    var d = Math.sqrt(nx * nx + ny * ny);
   112	    var R = b.r + c.r;
   113	    if (d >= R) return null;
   114	    if (d < 1e-6) { nx = 0; ny = -1; d = 1e-6; } else { nx /= d; ny /= d; }
   115	    b.x += nx * (R - d + 0.05);
   116	    b.y += ny * (R - d + 0.05);
   117	    var vn = b.vx * nx + b.vy * ny;
   118	    if (vn > 0) return null;
   119	    var impact = -vn;
   120	    var e = c.rest === undefined ? 0.45 : c.rest;
   121	    b.vx -= (1 + e) * vn * nx;
   122	    b.vy -= (1 + e) * vn * ny;
   123	    if (c.kick) {
   124	      b.vx = nx * c.kick + b.vx * 0.12;
   125	      b.vy = ny * c.kick + b.vy * 0.12;
   126	    }
   127	    return { nx: nx, ny: ny, speed: impact, obj: c };
   128	  }
   129	
   130	  // moving capsule (flipper)
   131	  function hitFlipper(b, f) {
   132	    var px = f.x, py = f.y;
   133	    var a = f.angle;
   134	    var ex = px + Math.cos(a) * f.len, ey = py + Math.sin(a) * f.len;
   135	    var c = closestOnSeg(b.x, b.y, px, py, ex, ey);
   136	    var nx = b.x - c.x, ny = b.y - c.y;
   137	    var d = Math.sqrt(nx * nx + ny * ny);
   138	    var R = b.r + f.r;
   139	    if (d >= R) return null;
   140	    if (d < 1e-6) { nx = 0; ny = -1; d = 1e-6; } else { nx /= d; ny /= d; }
   141	    b.x += nx * (R - d + 0.05);
   142	    b.y += ny * (R - d + 0.05);
   143	    // surface point velocity
   144	    var rx = c.x - px, ry = c.y - py;
   145	    var pvx = -f.omega * ry, pvy = f.omega * rx;
   146	    var rvx = b.vx - pvx, rvy = b.vy - pvy;
   147	    var vn = rvx * nx + rvy * ny;
   148	    if (vn > 0) return null;
   149	    var e = 0.42;
   150	    rvx -= (1 + e) * vn * nx;
   151	    rvy -= (1 + e) * vn * ny;
   152	    var tx = -ny, ty = nx;
   153	    var vt = rvx * tx + rvy * ty;
   154	    rvx -= vt * 0.10 * tx; rvy -= vt * 0.10 * ty;
   155	    b.vx = rvx + pvx; b.vy = rvy + pvy;
   156	    return { nx: nx, ny: ny, speed: -vn, obj: f };
   157	  }

   159	  /* ---------- particles ---------- */
   160	  function Particles(max) {
   161	    var p = [], i;
   162	    for (i = 0; i < max; i++) p.push({ life: 0 });
   163	    return {
   164	      list: p,
   165	      burst: function (x, y, n, col, spd, life) {
   166	        var made = 0;
   167	        for (var i = 0; i < p.length && made < n; i++) {
   168	          if (p[i].life > 0) continue;
   169	          var a = Math.random() * TAU, s = spd * (0.35 + Math.random() * 0.9);
   170	          p[i].x = x; p[i].y = y;
   171	          p[i].vx = Math.cos(a) * s; p[i].vy = Math.sin(a) * s;
   172	          p[i].life = p[i].max = life * (0.6 + Math.random() * 0.7);
   173	          p[i].c = col; p[i].sz = 1.6 + Math.random() * 2.4;
   174	          made++;
   175	        }
   176	      },
   177	      update: function (dt) {
   178	        for (var i = 0; i < p.length; i++) {
   179	          var q = p[i]; if (q.life <= 0) continue;
   180	          q.life -= dt;
   181	          q.x += q.vx * dt; q.y += q.vy * dt;
   182	          q.vy += 900 * dt; q.vx *= 0.98; q.vy *= 0.98;
   183	        }
   184	      },
   185	      draw: function (g) {
   186	        for (var i = 0; i < p.length; i++) {
   187	          var q = p[i]; if (q.life <= 0) continue;
   188	          g.globalAlpha = Math.max(0, q.life / q.max);
   189	          g.fillStyle = q.c;
   190	          g.fillRect(q.x - q.sz * 0.5, q.y - q.sz * 0.5, q.sz, q.sz);
   191	        }
   192	        g.globalAlpha = 1;
   193	      }
   194	    };
   195	  }

   198	  var actx = null, master = null, muted = false;
   199	  function audioInit() {
   200	    if (actx) { if (actx.state === 'suspended') actx.resume(); return; }

   210	  function tone(freq, dur, type, vol, slideTo) {
   211	    if (!actx || muted) return;
   212	    var t = actx.currentTime;
   213	    var o = actx.createOscillator(), g = actx.createGain();
   214	    o.type = type || 'square';
   215	    o.frequency.setValueAtTime(freq, t);
   216	    if (slideTo) o.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(Math.max(30, slideTo), t + dur);
   217	    g.gain.setValueAtTime(0.0001, t);
   218	    g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(vol === undefined ? 0.5 : vol, t + 0.008);
   219	    g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.0001, t + dur);
   220	    o.connect(g); g.connect(master);
   221	    o.start(t); o.stop(t + dur + 0.02);
   222	  }
   223	  function noise(dur, vol) {
   224	    if (!actx || muted) return;
   225	    var n = Math.floor(actx.sampleRate * dur);
   226	    var buf = actx.createBuffer(1, n, actx.sampleRate), d = buf.getChannelData(0);
   227	    for (var i = 0; i < n; i++) d[i] = (Math.random() * 2 - 1) * (1 - i / n);
   228	    var src = actx.createBufferSource(); src.buffer = buf;
   229	    var g = actx.createGain(); g.gain.value = vol || 0.3;
   230	    src.connect(g); g.connect(master); src.start();
   231	  }
