     8	  var G = 1500, VMAX = 1750, BALLR = 9.5;
     9	  var COL = {
    10	    bg0: '#111a2b', bg1: '#070a12', wall: '#46597a', rail: '#8fa8cc',
    11	    cy: '#4fd8ff', mg: '#ff5aa8', am: '#ffc24f', gr: '#6ef07a', wt: '#eaf2ff'
    12	  };
    13	
    14	  var MISSIONS = [
    15	    { k: 'ramp', need: 3, label: 'SHOOT 3 RAMPS' },
    16	    { k: 'pop', need: 14, label: 'HIT 14 BUMPERS' },
    17	    { k: 'spin', need: 24, label: 'SPIN 24 TIMES' },
    18	    { k: 'bank', need: 2, label: 'CLEAR 2 BANKS' },
    19	    { k: 'hole', need: 2, label: 'LOCK 2 IN THE HOLE' }
    20	  ];
    21	
    22	  var T, ball, S, parts, pops = [], shake = 0, nudgeOff = { x: 0, y: 0, vx: 0, vy: 0 };
    23	  var seedNum = 0;
    24	
    25	  /* ---------------- setup ---------------- */
    26	  function newGame(seed) {
    27	    seedNum = seed >>> 0;
    28	    SS.saveSeed(seedNum);
    29	    T = SS.genTable(seedNum);
    30	    parts = SS.Particles(180);
    31	    S = {
    32	      score: 0, balls: 3, ball: 1, over: false, level: 1, tilt: false,
    33	      mode: { on: false, idx: 0, prog: 0, time: 0 }, nudges: [], msg: '', msgT: 0,
    34	      best: SS.best(seedNum), launchPow: 0, plunging: false, t: 0
    35	    };
    36	    pops = [];
    37	    serve();
    38	  }
    39	  function serve() {
    40	    ball = {
    41	      x: 401, y: 714.5 - 0, vx: 0, vy: 0, r: BALLR, state: 'ready',
    42	      trail: [], stillT: 0, rd: 0, rs: 0, hold: 0
    43	    };
    44	    S.tilt = false; S.nudges = []; S.launchPow = 0;
    45	    T.flipL.on = T.flipR.on = false;
    46	    T.kick.charged = true;
    47	    T.banks.forEach(function (b) {
    48	      b.targets.forEach(function (t) { t.down = false; t.seg.down = false; });
    49	      b.done = false; b.resetAt = 0;
    50	    });
    51	    if (T.hole) { T.hole.lit = false; }
    52	  }
    53	
    54	  /* ---------------- scoring ---------------- */
    55	  function addScore(n, x, y, txt) {
    56	    if (S.tilt) return;
    57	    S.score += n;
    58	    if (txt) popup(x, y, txt);
    59	  }
    60	  function popup(x, y, txt, col) {
    61	    pops.push({ x: x, y: y, txt: txt, life: 1.1, col: col || COL.wt });
    62	    if (pops.length > 14) pops.shift();
    63	  }
    64	  function banner(t, dur) { S.msg = t; S.msgT = dur || 2.2; }
    65	
    66	  function progress(kind, x, y) {
    67	    var m = S.mode;
    68	    if (!m.on) return false;
    69	    if (MISSIONS[m.idx].k !== kind) return false;
    70	    m.prog++;
    71	    addScore(3000 * S.level, x, y, '+' + (3000 * S.level));
    72	    if (m.prog >= MISSIONS[m.idx].need) {
    73	      var jack = 25000 * S.level;
    74	      addScore(jack, 195, 300, 'JACKPOT ' + jack);
    75	      banner('JACKPOT! ' + jack.toLocaleString(), 3);
    76	      m.on = false; S.level++;
    77	      shake = Math.max(shake, 16);
    78	      parts.burst(195, 300, 60, COL.am, 460, 0.9);
    79	      SS.SFX.jack();
    80	    }
    81	    return true;
    82	  }
    83	
    84	  function bankDone(b) {
    85	    b.done = true; b.resetAt = S.t + 1.5; b.flash = 1;
    86	    addScore(5000 * S.level, b.x, b.y, 'BANK ' + (5000 * S.level));
    87	    SS.SFX.bank();
    88	    shake = Math.max(shake, 9);
    89	    parts.burst(b.x, b.y, 26, COL.gr, 340, 0.7);
    90	    T.kick.charged = true;
    91	    if (!progress('bank', b.x, b.y) && !S.mode.on && T.hole) {
    92	      T.hole.lit = true;
    93	      banner('MISSION LIT - SHOOT THE HOLE', 2.6);
    94	    }
    95	  }
    96	
    97	  function startMission() {
    98	    var m = S.mode;
    99	    m.idx = Math.floor(Math.random() * MISSIONS.length);
   100	    m.on = true; m.prog = 0; m.time = 60;
   101	    T.hole.lit = false;
   102	    banner(MISSIONS[m.idx].label, 3);
   103	    SS.SFX.jack();
   104	  }
   105	
   106	  /* ---------------- physics ---------------- */
   107	  function flipperStep(f, dt) {
   108	    var target = (f.on && !S.tilt) ? f.up : f.rest;
   109	    var sp = 27, d = target - f.angle;
   110	    var step = sp * dt;
   111	    if (Math.abs(d) <= step) { f.omega = d / Math.max(dt, 1e-4); f.angle = target; }
   112	    else { var s = d > 0 ? 1 : -1; f.angle += s * step; f.omega = s * sp; }
   113	    f.omega = SS.clamp(f.omega, -sp, sp);
   114	  }
   115	
   116	  function collide() {
   117	    var i, h, s;
   118	    for (i = 0; i < T.segs.length; i++) {
   119	      s = T.segs[i];
   120	      h = SS.hitSeg(ball, s);
   121	      if (!h) continue;
   122	      if (s.kind === 'target' && s.target && !s.target.down) {
   123	        s.target.down = true; s.down = true; s.target.flash = 1;
   124	        addScore(500 * S.level, s.target.cx, s.target.cy, '500');
   125	        SS.SFX.target();
   126	        parts.burst(s.target.cx, s.target.cy, 10, COL.gr, 240, 0.45);
   127	        var b = s.target.bank, all = true;
   128	        for (var k = 0; k < b.targets.length; k++) if (!b.targets[k].down) all = false;
   129	        if (all) bankDone(b);
   130	      } else if (s.kind === 'sling') {
   131	        s.ref.flash = 1;
   132	        addScore(50, (s.x1 + s.x2) / 2, (s.y1 + s.y2) / 2, '');
   133	        SS.SFX.sling();
   134	        parts.burst(ball.x, ball.y, 6, COL.mg, 260, 0.35);
   135	        shake = Math.max(shake, 3);
   136	      } else if (h.speed > 260) {
   137	        SS.SFX.wall(h.speed);
   138	      }
   139	    }
   140	    for (i = 0; i < T.circles.length; i++) {
   141	      var c = T.circles[i];
   142	      h = SS.hitCircle(ball, c);
   143	      if (!h) continue;
   144	      if (c.kind === 'pop') {
   145	        c.flash = 1; c.hits++;
   146	        addScore(100 * S.level, c.x, c.y, '');
   147	        SS.SFX.pop();
   148	        parts.burst(c.x, c.y, 8, COL.cy, 300, 0.4);
   149	        shake = Math.max(shake, 4);
   150	        progress('pop', c.x, c.y);
   151	      } else if (c.kind === 'post') { c.flash = 1; }
   152	    }
   153	    h = SS.hitFlipper(ball, T.flipL); if (h && h.speed > 200) SS.SFX.wall(h.speed);
   154	    h = SS.hitFlipper(ball, T.flipR); if (h && h.speed > 200) SS.SFX.wall(h.speed);
   155	  }
   156	
   157	  function sensors(px, py) {
   158	    // ramp mouth (crossing upward)
   159	    var R = T.ramp;
   160	    if (R && ball.state === 'live') {
   161	      var m = R.mouth;
   162	      if (py > m.y1 && ball.y <= m.y1 && ball.vy < 0 &&
   163	        ball.x > m.x1 - 4 && ball.x < m.x2 + 4) {
   164	        var sp = Math.hypot(ball.vx, ball.vy);
   165	        if (sp > 520) {
   166	          ball.state = 'ramp'; ball.rd = 0; ball.rs = SS.clamp(sp * 0.85, 520, 1150);
   167	          R.flash = 1; SS.SFX.ramp();
   168	        } else {
   169	          ball.y = m.y1 + 2; ball.vy = Math.abs(ball.vy) * 0.4 + 40;
   170	        }
   171	      }
   172	    }
   173	    // spinner
   174	    var sp2 = T.spinner;
   175	    if (sp2 && ball.state === 'live') {
   176	      var d = Math.hypot(ball.x - sp2.x, ball.y - sp2.y);
   177	      if (d < sp2.r + ball.r) {
   178	        if (!sp2.inside) {
   179	          sp2.inside = true;
   180	          sp2.vel += Math.min(46, Math.hypot(ball.vx, ball.vy) * 0.045) * (ball.vy > 0 ? 1 : -1);
   181	          sp2.flash = 1;
   182	        }
   183	      } else sp2.inside = false;
   184	    }
   185	    // mode hole
   186	    var ho = T.hole;
   187	    if (ho && ball.state === 'live' && !(ball.holeCool > 0)) {
   188	      if (Math.hypot(ball.x - ho.x, ball.y - ho.y) < ho.r) {
   189	        ball.state = 'saucer'; ball.hold = 0.85;
   190	        ball.vx = ball.vy = 0; ball.x = ho.x; ball.y = ho.y;
   191	        ho.flash = 1; SS.SFX.hole();
   192	        if (ho.lit && !S.mode.on) startMission();
   193	        else if (!progress('hole', ho.x, ho.y)) addScore(1000 * S.level, ho.x, ho.y, '1000');
   194	      }
   195	    }
   196	    // kickback
   197	    var kb = T.kick;
   198	    if (kb && ball.state === 'live' && Math.hypot(ball.x - kb.x, ball.y - kb.y) < kb.r + ball.r) {
   199	      if (kb.charged && !S.tilt) {
   200	        kb.charged = false; kb.flash = 1;
   201	        ball.x = 52; ball.y = 654;
   202	        ball.vx = -420; ball.vy = -1290;
   203	        SS.SFX.kickb(); shake = Math.max(shake, 8);
   204	        banner('KICKBACK!', 1.4);
   205	        parts.burst(kb.x, kb.y, 18, COL.am, 380, 0.6);
   206	      }
   207	    }
   208	  }
   209	
   210	  function drain() {
   211	    SS.SFX.drain();
   212	    shake = Math.max(shake, 10);
   213	    S.mode.on = false;
   214	    S.ball++;
   215	    if (S.ball > S.balls) {
   216	      S.over = true;
   217	      if (S.score > S.best) { S.best = S.score; SS.setBest(seedNum, S.score); }
   218	    } else {
   219	      banner('BALL ' + S.ball, 1.6);
   220	      serve();
   221	    }
   222	  }
   223	
   224	  function physics(dt) {
   225	    flipperStep(T.flipL, dt);
   226	    flipperStep(T.flipR, dt);
   227	
   228	    if (ball.state === 'ready') {
   229	      ball.x = 401; ball.y = 714.5;
   230	      ball.trail.length = 0;
   231	    } else if (ball.state === 'saucer') {
   232	      ball.hold -= dt;
   233	      if (ball.hold <= 0) {
   234	        ball.state = 'live';
   235	        var a = T.hole.ejectAng;
   236	        ball.vx = Math.cos(a) * 820; ball.vy = Math.sin(a) * 820;
   237	        ball.x = T.hole.x + Math.cos(a) * 34; ball.y = T.hole.y + Math.sin(a) * 34;
   238	        ball.holeCool = 0.45;
   239	        parts.burst(T.hole.x, T.hole.y, 12, '#ff5aa8', 320, 0.5);
   240	      }
   240	      }
   241	    } else if (ball.state === 'ramp') {
   242	      var R = T.ramp;
   243	      ball.rs = Math.max(360, ball.rs - 150 * dt);
   244	      ball.rd += ball.rs * dt;
   245	      if (ball.rd >= R.len) {
   246	        var n = R.path.length;
   247	        var a1 = R.path[n - 2], a2 = R.path[n - 1];
   248	        var dx = a2[0] - a1[0], dy = a2[1] - a1[1], L = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
   249	        ball.x = a2[0]; ball.y = a2[1];
   250	        ball.vx = dx / L * 300; ball.vy = dy / L * 300 + 60;
   251	        ball.state = 'live';
   252	        addScore(2500 * S.level, ball.x, ball.y, 'RAMP ' + (2500 * S.level));
   253	        progress('ramp', ball.x, ball.y);
   254	        parts.burst(ball.x, ball.y, 12, COL.cy, 260, 0.5);
   255	      } else {
   256	        var p = samplePath(R, ball.rd);
   257	        ball.x = p[0]; ball.y = p[1];
   258	      }
   259	      ball.trail.push([ball.x, ball.y]);
   260	      if (ball.trail.length > 9) ball.trail.shift();
   261	    } else {
   262	      if (ball.holeCool > 0) ball.holeCool -= dt;
   263	      var STEPS = 8, h = dt / STEPS;
   264	      for (var i = 0; i < STEPS; i++) {
   265	        var px = ball.x, py = ball.y;
   266	        ball.vy += G * h;
   267	        var damp = 1 - 0.16 * h;
   268	        ball.vx *= damp; ball.vy *= damp;
   269	        var sp = Math.hypot(ball.vx, ball.vy);
   270	        if (sp > VMAX) { ball.vx *= VMAX / sp; ball.vy *= VMAX / sp; }
   271	        ball.x += ball.vx * h; ball.y += ball.vy * h;
   272	        collide();
   273	        sensors(px, py);
   274	        if (ball.state !== 'live') break;
   275	      }
   276	      ball.trail.push([ball.x, ball.y]);
   277	      if (ball.trail.length > 9) ball.trail.shift();
   278	      // anti-wedge: if the ball has barely moved, shake it loose
   279	      var moved = Math.hypot(ball.x - (ball.lx === undefined ? 1e9 : ball.lx), ball.y - (ball.ly === undefined ? 0 : ball.ly));
   280	      ball.lx = ball.x; ball.ly = ball.y;
   281	      if (moved < 0.9) {
   282	        ball.stillT += dt;
   283	        if (ball.stillT > 1.4) {
   284	          ball.vx = (Math.random() - 0.5) * 420; ball.vy = -260; ball.stillT = 0;
   285	          ball.y -= 2;
   286	        }
   287	      } else ball.stillT = 0;
   288	      // a weak plunge rolls back into the lane: allow a re-plunge
   289	      if (ball.x > 386 && ball.y > 688 && Math.hypot(ball.vx, ball.vy) < 70) {
   290	        ball.state = 'ready'; ball.vx = ball.vy = 0; S.launchPow = 0; ball.stillT = 0;
   291	      }
   292	      if (ball.y > 716 && ball.x < 372) drain();
   293	      if (ball.y > 900) drain();
   294	    }
   295	  }
   296	
   297	  function samplePath(R, d) {
   298	    var c = R.cum, p = R.path;
   299	    var lo = 0, hi = c.length - 1;
   300	    while (lo < hi - 1) { var mid = (lo + hi) >> 1; if (c[mid] <= d) lo = mid; else hi = mid; }
   301	    var t = (d - c[lo]) / Math.max(1e-6, c[hi] - c[lo]);
   302	    return [SS.lerp(p[lo][0], p[hi][0], t), SS.lerp(p[lo][1], p[hi][1], t)];
   303	  }
   304	
   305	  /* ---------------- update ---------------- */
   306	  function update(dt) {
   307	    S.t += dt;
   308	    if (S.over) { parts.update(dt); decay(dt); return; }
   309	
   310	    physics(dt);
   311	    parts.update(dt);
   312	    decay(dt);
   313	
   314	    // spinner rotation & scoring
   315	    var sp = T.spinner;
   316	    if (sp) {
   317	      var prev = sp.rot;
   318	      sp.rot += sp.vel * dt;
   319	      sp.vel *= (1 - 1.15 * dt);
   320	      if (Math.abs(sp.vel) < 0.25) sp.vel = 0;
   321	      var half = Math.PI;
   322	      if (Math.floor(Math.abs(sp.rot) / half) !== Math.floor(Math.abs(prev) / half)) {
   323	        sp.spins++;
   324	        addScore(130 * S.level, sp.x, sp.y, '');
   325	        SS.SFX.spin();
   326	        progress('spin', sp.x, sp.y);
   327	      }
   328	    }
   329	    // bank auto-reset
   330	    for (var i = 0; i < T.banks.length; i++) {
   331	      var b = T.banks[i];
   332	      if (b.done && S.t > b.resetAt) {
   333	        b.done = false;
   334	        for (var k = 0; k < b.targets.length; k++) { b.targets[k].down = false; b.targets[k].seg.down = false; }
   335	      }
   336	    }
   337	    // mission timer
   338	    if (S.mode.on) {
   339	      S.mode.time -= dt;
   340	      if (S.mode.time <= 0) { S.mode.on = false; banner('MISSION OVER', 1.8); }
   341	    }
   342	    // nudge spring
   343	    var n = nudgeOff;
   344	    n.vx += -n.x * 260 * dt - n.vx * 9 * dt;
   345	    n.vy += -n.y * 260 * dt - n.vy * 9 * dt;
   346	    n.x += n.vx * dt; n.y += n.vy * dt;
   347	  }
   348	
   349	  function decay(dt) {
   350	    shake = Math.max(0, shake - shake * 6 * dt - 2 * dt);
   351	    if (S.msgT > 0) S.msgT -= dt;
   352	    var f = function (o) { if (o && o.flash > 0) o.flash = Math.max(0, o.flash - dt * 3.2); };
   353	    T.circles.forEach(f); T.slings.forEach(f); T.banks.forEach(f);
   354	    T.banks.forEach(function (b) { b.targets.forEach(f); });
   355	    f(T.spinner); f(T.hole); f(T.kick); f(T.ramp);
   356	    for (var i = pops.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
   357	      pops[i].life -= dt; pops[i].y -= 26 * dt;
   358	      if (pops[i].life <= 0) pops.splice(i, 1);
   359	    }
   360	  }
   361	
   362	  /* ---------------- input ---------------- */
   363	  var pointers = {};
   364	  function toLocal(e) {
   365	    var r = cv.getBoundingClientRect();
   366	    return {
   367	      x: (e.clientX - r.left) / r.width * CWID,
   368	      y: (e.clientY - r.top) / r.height * CH
   369	    };
   370	  }
   371	  function inRect(p, x, y, w, h) { return p.x >= x && p.x <= x + w && p.y >= y && p.y <= y + h; }
   372	
   373	  function press(p, id) {
   374	    SS.audioInit();
   375	    // HUD buttons
   376	    if (inRect(p, 314, 0, 116, 58)) { newGame((Math.random() * 4294967295) >>> 0); return; }
   377	    if (inRect(p, 266, 0, 48, 58)) { SS.setMuted(!SS.isMuted()); return; }
   378	    if (S.over) {
   379	      if (inRect(p, 65, 470, 300, 56)) { newGame(seedNum); return; }
   380	      if (inRect(p, 65, 540, 300, 56)) { newGame((Math.random() * 4294967295) >>> 0); return; }
   381	      return;
   382	    }
   383	    if (p.y < HUD) return;
   384	    var ty = p.y - HUD;
   385	    // plunger drag zone (generous: bottom-right quadrant while ball waits)
   386	    if (ball.state === 'ready' && p.x > 292 && ty > 470) {
   387	      pointers[id] = { role: 'plunge', sx: p.x, sy: p.y, t: performance.now(), moved: 0 };
   388	      S.plunging = true;
   389	      return;
   390	    }
   391	    var role = p.x < CWID / 2 ? 'L' : 'R';
   392	    pointers[id] = { role: role, sx: p.x, sy: p.y, t: performance.now(), moved: 0, swiped: false };
   393	    if (role === 'L') { T.flipL.on = true; } else { T.flipR.on = true; }
   394	    if (!S.tilt) SS.SFX.flip();
   395	  }
   396	  function move(p, id) {
   397	    var q = pointers[id]; if (!q) return;
   398	    var dx = p.x - q.sx, dy = p.y - q.sy;
   399	    q.moved = Math.hypot(dx, dy);
   400	    if (q.role === 'plunge') {
   401	      S.launchPow = SS.clamp(dy / 110, 0, 1);
   402	    } else if (!q.swiped && q.moved > 34 && performance.now() - q.t < 320) {
   403	      q.swiped = true;
   404	      nudge(dx, dy);
   405	    }
   406	  }
   407	  function release(id) {
   408	    var q = pointers[id]; if (!q) return;
   409	    delete pointers[id];
   410	    if (q.role === 'plunge') {
   411	      S.plunging = false;
   412	      launch();
   413	    } else {
   414	      var still = false;
   415	      for (var k in pointers) if (pointers[k].role === q.role) still = true;
   416	      if (!still) { if (q.role === 'L') T.flipL.on = false; else T.flipR.on = false; }
   417	    }
   418	  }
   419	  function launch() {
   420	    if (ball.state !== 'ready') { S.launchPow = 0; return; }
   421	    var pow = S.launchPow;
   422	    ball.state = 'live';
   423	    ball.vy = -(1370 + 380 * pow);
   424	    ball.vx = 0;
   425	    S.launchPow = 0;
   426	    SS.SFX.launch();
   427	    parts.burst(401, 716, 10, COL.am, 200, 0.4);
   428	  }
   429	  function nudge(dx, dy) {
   430	    if (S.tilt || S.over) return;
   431	    var L = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
   432	    var ux = dx / L, uy = dy / L;
   433	    if (ball.state === 'live') { ball.vx += ux * 190; ball.vy += uy * 120 - 60; }
   434	    nudgeOff.vx -= ux * 130; nudgeOff.vy -= uy * 90;
   435	    shake = Math.max(shake, 5);
   436	    var now = performance.now();
   437	    S.nudges.push(now);
   438	    S.nudges = S.nudges.filter(function (t) { return now - t < 2600; });
   439	    if (S.nudges.length > 3) {
   440	      S.tilt = true;
   441	      T.flipL.on = T.flipR.on = false;
   442	      banner('TILT', 3);
   443	      SS.SFX.tilt();
   444	      shake = Math.max(shake, 18);
   445	    }
   446	  }
   447	
   448	  cv.addEventListener('pointerdown', function (e) {
   449	    e.preventDefault(); cv.setPointerCapture && cv.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId);
   450	    press(toLocal(e), e.pointerId);
   451	  }, { passive: false });
   452	  cv.addEventListener('pointermove', function (e) {
   453	    e.preventDefault(); if (pointers[e.pointerId]) move(toLocal(e), e.pointerId);
   454	  }, { passive: false });
   455	  ['pointerup', 'pointercancel'].forEach(function (n) {
   456	    cv.addEventListener(n, function (e) { e.preventDefault(); release(e.pointerId); }, { passive: false });
   457	  });
   458	  document.addEventListener('touchmove', function (e) { e.preventDefault(); }, { passive: false });
   459	  document.addEventListener('gesturestart', function (e) { e.preventDefault(); }, { passive: false });
   460	
   461	  var keys = {};
   462	  window.addEventListener('keydown', function (e) {
   463	    if (keys[e.code]) return;
   464	    keys[e.code] = true;
   465	    SS.audioInit();
   466	    switch (e.code) {
   467	      case 'ShiftLeft': case 'ArrowLeft': case 'KeyA': case 'KeyZ':
   468	        T.flipL.on = true; if (!S.tilt) SS.SFX.flip(); e.preventDefault(); break;
   469	      case 'ShiftRight': case 'ArrowRight': case 'KeyD': case 'KeySlash': case 'Slash':
   470	        T.flipR.on = true; if (!S.tilt) SS.SFX.flip(); e.preventDefault(); break;
   471	      case 'Space': case 'Enter':
   472	        if (S.over) { newGame(seedNum); }
   473	        else if (ball.state === 'ready') { S.plunging = true; }
   474	        e.preventDefault(); break;
   475	      case 'KeyQ': nudge(-1, -0.4); break;
   476	      case 'KeyE': nudge(1, -0.4); break;
   477	      case 'ArrowUp': case 'KeyW': nudge(0, -1); e.preventDefault(); break;
   478	      case 'KeyN': newGame((Math.random() * 4294967295) >>> 0); break;
   479	      case 'KeyM': SS.setMuted(!SS.isMuted()); break;
   480	    }
   481	  });
   482	  window.addEventListener('keyup', function (e) {
   483	    keys[e.code] = false;
   484	    switch (e.code) {
   485	      case 'ShiftLeft': case 'ArrowLeft': case 'KeyA': case 'KeyZ': T.flipL.on = false; break;
   486	      case 'ShiftRight': case 'ArrowRight': case 'KeyD': case 'KeySlash': case 'Slash': T.flipR.on = false; break;
   487	      case 'Space': case 'Enter': if (S.plunging) { S.plunging = false; launch(); } break;
   488	    }
   489	  });
   490	
   491	  /* ---------------- render ---------------- */
   492	  function resize() {
   493	    var vw = window.innerWidth, vh = window.innerHeight;
   494	    var dpr = Math.min(2, window.devicePixelRatio || 1);
   495	    var scale = Math.min(vw / CWID, vh / CH);
   496	    var cssW = Math.floor(CWID * scale), cssH = Math.floor(CH * scale);
   497	    cv.style.width = cssW + 'px'; cv.style.height = cssH + 'px';
   498	    var backing = Math.min(cssH * dpr, 960);
   499	    var f = backing / CH;
   500	    cv.width = Math.round(CWID * f); cv.height = Math.round(CH * f);
   501	    view.f = f;
   502	  }
   503	  window.addEventListener('resize', resize);
   504	  window.addEventListener('orientationchange', function () { setTimeout(resize, 120); });
   505	
   506	  function fmt(n) { return n.toLocaleString('en-US'); }

   508	  function drawHUD() {
   509	    g.save();
   510	    g.fillStyle = '#0a0e18';
   511	    g.fillRect(0, 0, CWID, HUD);
   512	    g.fillStyle = '#1b2740'; g.fillRect(0, HUD - 2, CWID, 2);
   513	    // score
   514	    g.fillStyle = COL.wt; g.font = 'bold 30px ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace';
   515	    g.textAlign = 'left'; g.textBaseline = 'alphabetic';
   516	    g.fillText(fmt(S.score), 12, 36);
   517	    g.font = '11px ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace'; g.fillStyle = '#7d90b3';
   518	    g.fillText('BEST ' + fmt(S.best) + '  x' + S.level, 12, 52);
   519	    // balls
   520	    for (var i = 0; i < S.balls; i++) {
   520	    for (var i = 0; i < S.balls; i++) {
   521	      var used = i < S.ball - 1;
   522	      g.beginPath(); g.arc(206 + i * 18, 26, 6, 0, SS.TAU);
   523	      g.fillStyle = used ? '#26324a' : COL.am; g.fill();
   524	    }
   525	    g.font = '10px ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace'; g.fillStyle = '#7d90b3'; g.textAlign = 'center';
   526	    g.fillText('BALL ' + Math.min(S.ball, S.balls), 224, 46);
   527	    // mute
   528	    g.fillStyle = '#16203a'; roundRect(276, 8, 32, 38, 6); g.fill();
   529	    g.fillStyle = SS.isMuted() ? '#54637f' : COL.cy;
   530	    g.font = 'bold 13px ui-monospace,monospace';
   531	    g.fillText(SS.isMuted() ? 'X' : '((', 292, 32);
   532	    // new table btn
   533	    g.fillStyle = '#182a44'; roundRect(322, 8, 96, 38, 7); g.fill();
   534	    g.strokeStyle = COL.cy; g.lineWidth = 1.4; roundRect(322, 8, 96, 38, 7); g.stroke();
   535	    g.fillStyle = COL.cy; g.font = 'bold 12px ui-monospace,monospace';
   536	    g.fillText('NEW TABLE', 370, 27);
   537	    g.font = '9px ui-monospace,monospace'; g.fillStyle = '#5d6f92';
   538	    g.fillText('#' + seedNum.toString(36).toUpperCase().slice(0, 6), 370, 40);
   539	
   540	    // line 2: mission / message / hint
   541	    var y = 66;
   542	    g.textAlign = 'left';
   543	    if (S.msgT > 0) {
   544	      g.fillStyle = COL.am; g.font = 'bold 15px ui-monospace,monospace';
   545	      g.textAlign = 'center'; g.fillText(S.msg, CWID / 2, y + 4);
   546	    } else if (S.mode.on) {
   547	      var M = MISSIONS[S.mode.idx];
   548	      g.fillStyle = COL.mg; g.font = 'bold 13px ui-monospace,monospace';
   549	      g.fillText(M.label + '  ' + S.mode.prog + '/' + M.need, 12, y);
   550	      g.fillStyle = '#22304d'; g.fillRect(12, y + 6, CWID - 24, 5);
   551	      g.fillStyle = COL.mg; g.fillRect(12, y + 6, (CWID - 24) * SS.clamp(S.mode.time / 60, 0, 1), 5);
   552	      g.textAlign = 'right'; g.fillStyle = '#7d90b3'; g.font = '10px ui-monospace,monospace';
   553	      g.fillText(Math.ceil(S.mode.time) + 's', CWID - 12, y);
   554	    } else {
   555	      g.fillStyle = '#6d80a3'; g.font = '11px ui-monospace,monospace';
   556	      g.textAlign = 'center';
   557	      var hint = ball.state === 'ready'
   558	        ? 'DRAG DOWN THE RIGHT LANE TO LAUNCH'
   559	        : (T.hole && T.hole.lit ? 'MISSION LIT - SHOOT THE MODE HOLE'
   560	          : 'TAP LEFT / RIGHT = FLIPPERS   SWIPE = NUDGE');
   561	      g.fillText(hint, CWID / 2, y + 3);
   562	    }
   563	    g.restore();
   564	  }
   565	
   566	  function roundRect(x, y, w, h, r) {
   567	    g.beginPath();
   568	    g.moveTo(x + r, y); g.lineTo(x + w - r, y); g.quadraticCurveTo(x + w, y, x + w, y + r);
   569	    g.lineTo(x + w, y + h - r); g.quadraticCurveTo(x + w, y + h, x + w - r, y + h);
   570	    g.lineTo(x + r, y + h); g.quadraticCurveTo(x, y + h, x, y + h - r);
   571	    g.lineTo(x, y + r); g.quadraticCurveTo(x, y, x + r, y);
   572	    g.closePath();
   573	  }

   575	  function drawPlayfield() {
   576	    var grd = g.createLinearGradient(0, 0, 0, TH);
   577	    grd.addColorStop(0, COL.bg0); grd.addColorStop(1, COL.bg1);
   578	    g.fillStyle = grd; g.fillRect(0, 0, CWID, TH);
   579	    // subtle arcs
   580	    g.strokeStyle = 'rgba(120,160,220,0.05)'; g.lineWidth = 1;
   581	    for (var i = 1; i <= 5; i++) {
   582	      g.beginPath(); g.arc(195, 300, i * 70, 0, SS.TAU); g.stroke();
   583	    }
   584	    // lane shading
   585	    g.fillStyle = 'rgba(10,16,28,0.7)'; g.fillRect(380, 150, 42, 580);
   586	  }

   588	  function drawSegs() {
   589	    g.lineCap = 'round';
   590	    for (var i = 0; i < T.segs.length; i++) {
   591	      var s = T.segs[i];
   592	      if (s.kind === 'target' || s.kind === 'sling' || s.kind === 'slingedge') continue;
   593	      if (s.kind === 'gate') {
   594	        g.strokeStyle = 'rgba(180,210,255,0.35)'; g.lineWidth = 3;
   595	      } else {
   596	        g.strokeStyle = COL.wall; g.lineWidth = (s.r || 4) * 2;
   597	      }
   598	      g.beginPath(); g.moveTo(s.x1, s.y1); g.lineTo(s.x2, s.y2); g.stroke();
   599	    }
   600	    // rail highlight
   601	    g.strokeStyle = 'rgba(180,208,255,0.16)'; g.lineWidth = 1.4;
   602	    for (i = 0; i < T.segs.length; i++) {
   603	      var q = T.segs[i];
   604	      if (q.kind !== 'wall') continue;
   605	      g.beginPath(); g.moveTo(q.x1, q.y1); g.lineTo(q.x2, q.y2); g.stroke();
   606	    }
   607	  }
   608	
   609	  function drawSlings() {
   610	    T.slings.forEach(function (sl) {
   611	      g.beginPath();
   612	      g.moveTo(sl.p[0][0], sl.p[0][1]);
   613	      g.lineTo(sl.p[1][0], sl.p[1][1]);
   614	      g.lineTo(sl.p[2][0], sl.p[2][1]);
   615	      g.closePath();
   616	      g.fillStyle = '#1d2b45'; g.fill();
   617	      g.lineWidth = 6; g.lineJoin = 'round';
   618	      g.strokeStyle = sl.flash > 0 ? '#fff' : COL.mg;
   619	      g.globalAlpha = sl.flash > 0 ? 1 : 0.85;
   620	      g.stroke(); g.globalAlpha = 1;
   621	    });
   622	  }

   624	  function drawBanks() {
   625	    T.banks.forEach(function (b) {
   626	      var dx = Math.cos(b.ang), dy = Math.sin(b.ang), nx = -dy, ny = dx;
   627	      b.targets.forEach(function (t) {
   628	        var down = t.down;
   629	        g.save();
   630	        g.translate(t.cx, t.cy);
   631	        g.rotate(b.ang);
   632	        var w = 16, hgt = down ? 2.5 : 8;
   633	        g.fillStyle = down ? '#243349' : (t.flash > 0 ? '#ffffff' : COL.gr);
   634	        g.fillRect(-w / 2, -hgt / 2, w, hgt);
   635	        if (!down) {
   636	          g.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.35)';
   637	          g.fillRect(-w / 2, -hgt / 2, w, 2);
   638	        }
   639	        g.restore();
   640	      });
   641	      // bank base line
   642	      g.strokeStyle = 'rgba(110,240,122,0.18)'; g.lineWidth = 1;
   643	      var L = (b.targets.length * 21) / 2;
   644	      g.beginPath();
   645	      g.moveTo(b.x - dx * L + nx * 8, b.y - dy * L + ny * 8);
   646	      g.lineTo(b.x + dx * L + nx * 8, b.y + dy * L + ny * 8);
   647	      g.stroke();
   648	    });
   649	  }

   651	  function drawPops() {
   652	    T.pops.forEach(function (p) {
   653	      var fl = p.flash;
   654	      g.beginPath(); g.arc(p.x, p.y, p.r + 3 + fl * 5, 0, SS.TAU);
   655	      g.fillStyle = 'rgba(79,216,255,' + (0.10 + fl * 0.4) + ')'; g.fill();
   656	      g.beginPath(); g.arc(p.x, p.y, p.r, 0, SS.TAU);
   657	      var gr = g.createRadialGradient(p.x - 4, p.y - 5, 2, p.x, p.y, p.r);
   658	      gr.addColorStop(0, fl > 0 ? '#ffffff' : '#7ee6ff');
   659	      gr.addColorStop(1, fl > 0 ? '#7ee6ff' : '#1c5f80');
   660	      g.fillStyle = gr; g.fill();
   661	      g.strokeStyle = COL.cy; g.lineWidth = 2; g.stroke();
   662	      g.beginPath(); g.arc(p.x, p.y, 4.5, 0, SS.TAU);
   663	      g.fillStyle = '#0b1520'; g.fill();
   664	    });
   665	    T.posts.forEach(function (p) {
   666	      g.beginPath(); g.arc(p.x, p.y, p.r, 0, SS.TAU);
   667	      g.fillStyle = p.flash > 0 ? '#fff' : '#c9d8ee'; g.fill();
   668	      g.strokeStyle = '#6c7f9e'; g.lineWidth = 1.5; g.stroke();
   669	    });
   670	  }

   672	  function drawSpinner() {
   673	    var s = T.spinner; if (!s) return;
   674	    g.save();
   675	    g.translate(s.x, s.y);
   676	    g.rotate(s.ang);
   677	    g.strokeStyle = 'rgba(255,194,79,0.25)'; g.lineWidth = 1.5;
   678	    g.beginPath(); g.moveTo(0, -s.r - 6); g.lineTo(0, s.r + 6); g.stroke();
   679	    var sc = Math.abs(Math.cos(s.rot));
   680	    g.fillStyle = Math.abs(s.vel) > 0.4 ? '#fff' : COL.am;
   681	    g.fillRect(-s.r, -9 * sc - 1, s.r * 2, Math.max(1.5, 18 * sc));
   682	    g.strokeStyle = COL.am; g.lineWidth = 1.6;
   683	    g.strokeRect(-s.r, -9 * sc - 1, s.r * 2, Math.max(1.5, 18 * sc));
   684	    g.restore();
   685	    g.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,194,79,0.55)'; g.font = '8px ui-monospace,monospace'; g.textAlign = 'center';
   686	    g.fillText('SPIN ' + s.spins, s.x, s.y + s.r + 18);
   687	  }

   689	  function drawHole() {
   690	    var h = T.hole; if (!h) return;
   691	    var lit = h.lit || S.mode.on;
   692	    g.beginPath(); g.arc(h.x, h.y, h.r + 7 + h.flash * 6, 0, SS.TAU);
   693	    g.fillStyle = lit ? 'rgba(255,90,168,' + (0.22 + 0.14 * Math.sin(S.t * 7)) + ')' : 'rgba(120,150,200,0.10)';
   694	    g.fill();
   695	    g.beginPath(); g.arc(h.x, h.y, h.r, 0, SS.TAU);
   696	    g.fillStyle = '#05070c'; g.fill();
   697	    g.strokeStyle = lit ? COL.mg : '#5a6d8d'; g.lineWidth = 2.5; g.stroke();
   698	    g.fillStyle = lit ? COL.mg : '#5a6d8d';
   699	    g.font = 'bold 8px ui-monospace,monospace'; g.textAlign = 'center';
   700	    g.fillText(lit ? 'MISSION' : 'HOLE', h.x, h.y + h.r + 13);
   701	  }

   714	  function drawRamp() {
   715	    var R = T.ramp; if (!R) return;
   716	    var p = R.path;
   717	    g.lineJoin = 'round'; g.lineCap = 'round';
   718	    g.beginPath(); g.moveTo(p[0][0], p[0][1]);
   719	    for (var i = 1; i < p.length; i++) g.lineTo(p[i][0], p[i][1]);
   720	    g.strokeStyle = 'rgba(79,216,255,' + (0.13 + R.flash * 0.35) + ')'; g.lineWidth = 30; g.stroke();
   721	    g.strokeStyle = 'rgba(10,16,28,0.82)'; g.lineWidth = 24; g.stroke();
   722	    g.strokeStyle = 'rgba(143,168,204,0.45)'; g.lineWidth = 1.5; g.stroke();
   723	    // mouth arrow
   724	    var m = R.mouth;
   725	    var lit = S.mode.on && MISSIONS[S.mode.idx].k === 'ramp';
   726	    g.strokeStyle = lit ? COL.mg : COL.cy;
   727	    g.globalAlpha = lit ? 0.6 + 0.4 * Math.sin(S.t * 8) : 0.7;
   728	    g.lineWidth = 3;
   729	    g.beginPath();
   730	    g.moveTo(m.x1 + 8, m.y1 + 16); g.lineTo((m.x1 + m.x2) / 2, m.y1 + 2); g.lineTo(m.x2 - 8, m.y1 + 16);
   731	    g.stroke();
   732	    g.globalAlpha = 1;
   733	    g.fillStyle = lit ? COL.mg : 'rgba(79,216,255,0.7)';
   734	    g.font = 'bold 9px ui-monospace,monospace'; g.textAlign = 'center';
   735	    g.fillText('RAMP', (m.x1 + m.x2) / 2, m.y1 + 30);
   736	  }

   738	  function drawFlipper(f) {
   739	    var a = f.angle;
   740	    var ex = f.x + Math.cos(a) * f.len, ey = f.y + Math.sin(a) * f.len;
   741	    g.lineCap = 'round';
   742	    g.strokeStyle = S.tilt ? '#4a5468' : '#e8f1ff';
   743	    g.lineWidth = f.r * 2;
   744	    g.beginPath(); g.moveTo(f.x, f.y); g.lineTo(ex, ey); g.stroke();
   745	    g.strokeStyle = S.tilt ? '#333c4d' : (f.on ? COL.am : '#8ea6c8');
   746	    g.lineWidth = f.r * 2 - 5;
   747	    g.beginPath(); g.moveTo(f.x, f.y); g.lineTo(ex, ey); g.stroke();
   748	    g.beginPath(); g.arc(f.x, f.y, 4, 0, SS.TAU); g.fillStyle = '#0b111c'; g.fill();
   749	  }

   751	  function drawBall() {
   752	    for (var i = 0; i < ball.trail.length; i++) {
   753	      var t = ball.trail[i], a = (i + 1) / ball.trail.length;
   754	      g.beginPath(); g.arc(t[0], t[1], ball.r * (0.35 + a * 0.6), 0, SS.TAU);
   755	      g.fillStyle = 'rgba(200,225,255,' + (a * 0.16) + ')'; g.fill();
   756	    }
   757	    var gr = g.createRadialGradient(ball.x - 3.5, ball.y - 4, 1, ball.x, ball.y, ball.r);
   758	    gr.addColorStop(0, '#ffffff'); gr.addColorStop(0.55, '#cfe0f5'); gr.addColorStop(1, '#5f7391');
   759	    g.beginPath(); g.arc(ball.x, ball.y, ball.r, 0, SS.TAU);
   760	    g.fillStyle = gr; g.fill();
   761	    g.strokeStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.5)'; g.lineWidth = 1; g.stroke();
   762	  }

   764	  function drawPlunger() {
   765	    // lane plunger rod + power meter
   766	    var pull = S.launchPow * 40;
   767	    g.fillStyle = '#2a3a58';
   768	    g.fillRect(394, 720 + pull * 0.2, 14, 10);
   769	    g.fillStyle = '#8ea6c8';
   770	    g.fillRect(399, 726, 4, 6);
   771	    if (ball.state === 'ready') {
   772	      var bx = 386, by = 560, bw = 30, bh = 150;
   773	      g.fillStyle = 'rgba(10,16,28,0.75)'; g.fillRect(bx, by, bw, bh);
   774	      g.strokeStyle = '#2c3d5c'; g.lineWidth = 1; g.strokeRect(bx, by, bw, bh);
   775	      g.fillStyle = S.launchPow > 0.85 ? COL.mg : COL.am;
   776	      g.fillRect(bx + 2, by + bh - 2 - (bh - 4) * S.launchPow, bw - 4, (bh - 4) * S.launchPow);
   777	      g.save();
   778	      g.translate(bx + bw / 2, by - 8);
   779	      g.fillStyle = '#7d90b3'; g.font = 'bold 10px ui-monospace,monospace'; g.textAlign = 'center';
   780	      g.fillText('PULL', 0, 0);
   781	      g.restore();
   782	    }
   783	  }

   813	  function btn(x, y, w, h, label, col) {
   814	    g.fillStyle = 'rgba(20,32,54,0.95)'; roundRect(x, y, w, h, 10); g.fill();
   815	    g.strokeStyle = col; g.lineWidth = 2; roundRect(x, y, w, h, 10); g.stroke();
   816	    g.fillStyle = col; g.font = 'bold 17px ui-monospace,monospace'; g.textAlign = 'center';
   817	    g.fillText(label, x + w / 2, y + h / 2 + 6);
   818	  }
   819	
   820	  function render() {
   821	    g.setTransform(view.f, 0, 0, view.f, 0, 0);
   822	    g.clearRect(0, 0, CWID, CH);

    48	    T.slings.forEach(function (sl) {
    49	      var a = sl.p[0], b = sl.p[1], c = sl.p[2];
    50	      var f = SS.seg(S, a[0], a[1], b[0], b[1], { rest: 0.5, r: 4, kick: 640, kind: 'sling' });
    51	      f.ref = sl;
    52	      SS.seg(S, b[0], b[1], c[0], c[1], { rest: 0.3, r: 3, kind: 'slingedge' });
    53	      SS.seg(S, c[0], c[1], a[0], a[1], { rest: 0.3, r: 3, kind: 'slingedge' });
    54	    });
    55	    // flippers
    56	    T.flipL = { x: 132, y: 652, len: 56, r: 7.5, rest: 30 * D2R, up: -32 * D2R, angle: 30 * D2R, omega: 0, side: -1, on: false };
    57	    T.flipR = { x: 258, y: 652, len: 56, r: 7.5, rest: 150 * D2R, up: 212 * D2R, angle: 150 * D2R, omega: 0, side: 1, on: false };
    58	    // kickback (left outlane)
    59	    T.kick = { x: 54, y: 660, r: 15, charged: true, flash: 0 };
    60	    // ball entry point of the curl
    61	    T.entry = [90, 105];
    62	  }
    63	
    64	  /* ---------- parts ---------- */
    65	  function addPops(T, ax, ay, n, rnd) {
    66	    var base = rnd() * SS.TAU;
    67	    for (var i = 0; i < n; i++) {
    68	      var a = base + i * SS.TAU / n;
    69	      var rr = 30 + rnd() * 12;
    70	      var p = {
    71	        x: ax + Math.cos(a) * rr, y: ay + Math.sin(a) * rr * 0.85,
    72	        r: 17, rest: 0.35, kick: 700, kind: 'pop', flash: 0, hits: 0
    73	      };
    74	      T.circles.push(p); T.pops.push(p);
    75	    }
    76	  }
    77	
    78	  function addBank(T, ax, ay, ang, n, rnd) {
    79	    var dx = Math.cos(ang), dy = Math.sin(ang);
    80	    var bank = { x: ax, y: ay, ang: ang, targets: [], flash: 0, done: false, resetAt: 0 };
    81	    for (var i = 0; i < n; i++) {
    82	      var o = (i - (n - 1) / 2) * 21;
    83	      var cx = ax + dx * o, cy = ay + dy * o;
    84	      var t = { cx: cx, cy: cy, down: false, flash: 0, bank: bank };
    85	      var s = SS.seg(T.segs, cx - dx * 8, cy - dy * 8, cx + dx * 8, cy + dy * 8,
    86	        { rest: 0.45, r: 4.5, kind: 'target' });
    87	      s.target = t; t.seg = s;
    88	      bank.targets.push(t);
    89	    }
    90	    T.banks.push(bank);
    91	    return bank;
    92	  }
    93	
    94	  function addSpinner(T, ax, ay, ang) {
    95	    var sp = { x: ax, y: ay, ang: ang, rot: 0, vel: 0, r: 17, spins: 0, flash: 0 };
    96	    var dx = Math.cos(ang), dy = Math.sin(ang); // lane direction
    97	    var nx = -dy, ny = dx;
    98	    // chute guides either side of the lane
    99	    SS.seg(T.segs, ax + nx * 26 - dx * 40, ay + ny * 26 - dy * 40, ax + nx * 26 + dx * 40, ay + ny * 26 + dy * 40, { rest: 0.3, r: 3.5 });
   100	    SS.seg(T.segs, ax - nx * 26 - dx * 40, ay - ny * 26 - dy * 40, ax - nx * 26 + dx * 40, ay - ny * 26 + dy * 40, { rest: 0.3, r: 3.5 });
   101	    T.spinner = sp;
   102	  }
   103	
   104	  function addHole(T, ax, ay) {
   105	    T.hole = { x: ax, y: ay, r: 14, lit: false, flash: 0, hold: 0, ejectAng: 0 };
   106	    // horseshoe rim so the saucer reads as a target
   107	    var pts = SS.arcPts(ax, ay, 24, 24, 152 * D2R, 388 * D2R, 12);
   108	    SS.chain(T.segs, pts, { rest: 0.4, r: 3 });
   109	  }
   110	
   111	  function addRamp(T, rampX, rnd) {
   112	    var exitRight = rampX <= 195;
   113	    var s = exitRight ? 1 : -1;
   114	    var ey = 340;
   115	    // funnel guides into the ramp mouth
   116	    SS.seg(T.segs, rampX - 48, ey + 82, rampX - 23, ey + 2, { rest: 0.35, r: 3.5 });
   117	    SS.seg(T.segs, rampX + 48, ey + 82, rampX + 23, ey + 2, { rest: 0.35, r: 3.5 });
   118	    var ctrl;
   119	    if (exitRight) {
   120	      ctrl = [[rampX, ey], [rampX + 22, 264], [rampX + 76, 194], [296, 134], [356, 246], [364, 402], [348, 512], [316, 574]];
   121	    } else {
   122	      ctrl = [[rampX, ey], [rampX - 22, 264], [rampX - 76, 194], [94, 134], [34, 246], [26, 402], [42, 512], [74, 574]];
   123	    }
   124	    var path = SS.smooth(ctrl, 7);
   125	    var len = 0, cum = [0];
   126	    for (var i = 1; i < path.length; i++) {
   127	      len += Math.hypot(path[i][0] - path[i - 1][0], path[i][1] - path[i - 1][1]);
   128	      cum.push(len);
   129	    }
   130	    T.ramp = {
   131	      x: rampX, y: ey, path: path, cum: cum, len: len, exitRight: exitRight,
   132	      flash: 0, mouth: { x1: rampX - 23, y1: ey, x2: rampX + 23, y2: ey }
   133	    };
   134	  }
   135	
   136	  /* ---------- generator ---------- */
   137	  SS.genTable = function (seed) {
   138	    var rnd = SS.rng(seed);
   139	    var T = {
   140	      seed: seed, segs: [], circles: [], pops: [], banks: [], posts: [],
   141	      ramp: null, spinner: null, hole: null
   142	    };
   143	    buildShell(T);
   144	
   145	    var rampX = [115, 195, 275][Math.floor(rnd() * 3) % 3];
   146	    addRamp(T, rampX, rnd);
   147	
   148	    var used = [{ x: rampX, y: 388, r: 76 }, { x: 90, y: 112, r: 46 }];
   149	    function free(a, r) {
   150	      if (a[0] < 44 || a[0] > 346 || a[1] < 118 || a[1] > 486) return false;
   151	      for (var i = 0; i < used.length; i++) {
   152	        if (Math.hypot(a[0] - used[i].x, a[1] - used[i].y) < r + used[i].r) return false;
   153	      }
   154	      return true;
   155	    }
   156	    function shuffle(arr) {
   157	      var a = arr.slice();
   158	      for (var i = a.length - 1; i > 0; i--) {
   159	        var j = Math.floor(rnd() * (i + 1)); var t = a[i]; a[i] = a[j]; a[j] = t;
   160	      }
   161	      return a;
   162	    }
   163	    var upper = shuffle([[100, 178], [195, 148], [292, 178]]);
   164	    var mid = shuffle([[78, 316], [312, 316], [195, 288]]);
   165	    var low = shuffle([[108, 440], [282, 440], [195, 462]]);
   166	    var pool = upper.concat(mid).concat(low);
   167	    function take(list, r) {
   168	      for (var i = 0; i < list.length; i++) {
   169	        if (free(list[i], r)) { var a = list.splice(i, 1)[0]; used.push({ x: a[0], y: a[1], r: r }); return a; }
   170	      }
   171	      for (var j = 0; j < pool.length; j++) {
   172	        if (free(pool[j], r)) {
   173	          var b = pool[j];
   174	          var k = list.indexOf(b); if (k >= 0) list.splice(k, 1);
   175	          pool.splice(j, 1);
   176	          used.push({ x: b[0], y: b[1], r: r }); return b;
   177	        }
   178	      }
   179	      return null;
   180	    }
   181	    function sync(a) { var i = pool.indexOf(a); if (i >= 0) pool.splice(i, 1); }
   182	
   183	    // pop bumper cluster
   184	    var pa = take(upper, 58); if (pa) { sync(pa); addPops(T, pa[0], pa[1], 3 + Math.floor(rnd() * 2), rnd); }
   185	    // primary drop bank
   186	    var ba = take(upper.concat(mid).slice(0), 52);
   187	    if (ba) {
   188	      sync(ba); removeFrom(upper, ba); removeFrom(mid, ba);
   189	      addBank(T, ba[0], ba[1], (rnd() < 0.5 ? 1 : -1) * (20 + rnd() * 45) * D2R, 3 + Math.floor(rnd() * 3), rnd);
   190	    }
   191	    // spinner lane
   192	    var sa = take(mid, 46);
   193	    if (sa) { sync(sa); addSpinner(T, sa[0], sa[1], (75 + rnd() * 30) * D2R); }
   194	    // second drop bank
   195	    var b2 = take(low, 50);
   196	    if (b2) { sync(b2); addBank(T, b2[0], b2[1], (rnd() < 0.5 ? 1 : -1) * (15 + rnd() * 40) * D2R, 3 + Math.floor(rnd() * 2), rnd); }
   197	    // mode hole
   198	    var ha = take(mid.concat(low).concat(upper), 40);
   199	    if (!ha) ha = [195, 230];
   200	    else { sync(ha); removeFrom(upper, ha); removeFrom(mid, ha); removeFrom(low, ha); }
   201	    addHole(T, ha[0], ha[1]);
   202	    T.hole.ejectAng = (rnd() < 0.5 ? -125 : -55) * D2R;
   203	
   204	    // scattered rubber posts
   205	    var np = 4 + Math.floor(rnd() * 4);
   206	    for (var i = 0, guard = 0; i < np && guard < 200; guard++) {
   207	      var px = 50 + rnd() * 296, py = 150 + rnd() * 330;
   208	      if (!free([px, py], 22)) continue;
   209	      used.push({ x: px, y: py, r: 20 });
   210	      var post = { x: px, y: py, r: 6.5, rest: 0.62, kind: 'post', flash: 0 };
   211	      T.circles.push(post); T.posts.push(post);
   212	      i++;
   213	    }
   214	
   215	    function removeFrom(list, a) { var i = list.indexOf(a); if (i >= 0) list.splice(i, 1); }
   216	
   217	    T.name = tableName(seed);
   218	    return T;
   219	  };
   220	
   221	  var ADJ = ['CRIMSON', 'HOLLOW', 'IRON', 'VOLT', 'GLASS', 'NEON', 'ASH', 'DEEP', 'STORM', 'COBALT', 'RUST', 'PRISM'];
   222	  var NOUN = ['CARAPACE', 'REEF', 'FORGE', 'SPIRE', 'BASIN', 'CIRCUIT', 'MOLT', 'HARBOR', 'VAULT', 'DRIFT', 'SHELL', 'CRATER'];
   223	  function tableName(seed) {
   224	    var r = SS.rng(seed ^ 0x9e37);
   225	    return ADJ[Math.floor(r() * ADJ.length)] + ' ' + NOUN[Math.floor(r() * NOUN.length)];
   226	  }
   227	})(SS);

   105	  function makeMove(kind, s, rng, tier) {
   106	    var spd = 1 + s * 0.15 + tier * 0.08;
   107	    if (kind === 'ring') {
   108	      return {
   109	        kind: 'ring', dur: rng.range(3.4, 4.4), period: rng.range(0.5, 0.75),
   110	        n: 12 + s * 3 + tier * 3, spin: rng.range(0.12, 0.34) * (rng.chance(0.5) ? 1 : -1),
   111	        speed: 96 * spd, c: COL.ring, r: 4.5, aim: rng.chance(0.4)
   112	      };
   113	    }
   114	    if (kind === 'arms') {
   115	      return {
   116	        kind: 'arms', dur: rng.range(4.0, 5.2), period: 0.075,
   117	        arms: 2 + ((s + tier) % 4), rate: (2.0 + s * 0.4 + tier * 0.3) * (rng.chance(0.5) ? 1 : -1),
   118	        speed: 90 * spd, c: COL.arms, r: 4, sweep: rng.chance(0.5)
   119	      };
   120	    }
   121	    if (kind === 'aimed') {
   122	      return {
   123	        kind: 'aimed', dur: rng.range(3.0, 4.0), period: rng.range(0.75, 1.05),
   124	        n: 3 + s + tier, spread: 0.22 + tier * 0.05, burst: 2 + tier,
   125	        speed: 132 * spd, c: COL.aimed, r: 4
   126	      };
   127	    }
   128	    if (kind === 'wall') {
   129	      return {
   130	        kind: 'wall', dur: rng.range(3.6, 4.6), period: rng.range(1.0, 1.35),
   131	        count: 15 + s * 2 + tier * 2, gap: 66 - s * 5 - tier * 4,
   132	        speed: 104 * spd, c: COL.wall, r: 4.5, seed: rng.int(1, 9999)
   133	      };
   134	    }
   135	    return {
   136	      kind: 'rain', dur: rng.range(3.0, 4.0), period: Math.max(0.06, 0.16 - s * 0.02 - tier * 0.02),
   137	      speed: 118 * spd, c: COL.rain, r: 3.6, seed: rng.int(1, 9999)
   138	    };
   139	  }

   141	  var BOSS_NAMES = ['KESTREL FRAME', 'VAULT CHOIR', 'SKYHAMMER PRIME'];
   142	
   143	  function makeBoss(s, rng) {
   144	    var kinds = ['ring', 'arms', 'aimed', 'wall', 'rain'];
   145	    var phases = [];
   146	    var baseHP = [250, 310, 380];
   147	    for (var p = 0; p < 3; p++) {
   148	      var pool = kinds.slice();
   149	      var moves = [];
   150	      var cnt = 2 + (p > 0 ? 1 : 0);
   151	      // grammar: each phase always contains one "aimed pressure" move plus 1-2 geometric moves
   152	      moves.push(makeMove(p === 0 ? 'aimed' : rng.pick(['aimed', 'rain']), s, rng, p));
   153	      for (var m = 0; m < cnt; m++) {
   154	        var k = rng.pick(pool);
   155	        pool.splice(pool.indexOf(k), 1);
   156	        if (!pool.length) pool = kinds.slice();
   157	        moves.push(makeMove(k, s, rng, p));
   158	      }
   159	      phases.push({
   160	        hp: Math.round(baseHP[p] * (1 + s * 0.3)),
   161	        pods: p < 2 ? 2 : 2,
   162	        podHP: Math.round(85 * (1 + s * 0.25)),
   163	        moves: moves
   164	      });
   165	    }
   166	    return { name: BOSS_NAMES[s], phases: phases };
   167	  }
   168	
   169	  function makeStage(s) {
   170	    var rng = SH.rngObj(90210 + s * 7919);
   171	    var waves = [];
   172	    var t = 1.6;
   173	    var nW = 8 + s;
   174	    var kinds = ['aimed', 'fan', 'spiral', 'wall'];
   175	    for (var i = 0; i < nW; i++) {
   176	      var kind = (i % 4 === 3) ? 'wall' : (i === 0 ? 'aimed' : rng.pick(kinds));
   177	      waves.push(makeWave(kind, s, rng, t));
   178	      t += Math.max(3.0, rng.range(4.3, 6.0) - s * 0.3);
   179	    }
   180	    return {
   181	      index: s, theme: STAGE_THEME[s], waves: waves,
   182	      bossAt: t + 2.0, boss: makeBoss(s, rng)
   183	    };
   184	  }
   185	
   186	  SH.makeStage = makeStage;
   187	  SH.COL = COL;
   188	})(SH);

### deadline-dungeon

LOW/high deadline-dungeon/game.js:55 — `resetRun()` does not clear active input state or particles, so restart during held input can carry movement/actions into the new run.

MEDIUM/medium deadline-dungeon/game.js:140 — The real-time simulation continues while the landscape rotate overlay is visible.

### kart-circuit-zero

HIGH/high kart-circuit-zero/game.js:80 — The portrait rotate overlay does not pause the race; timer and auto-acceleration continue while gameplay is blocked.

MEDIUM/high kart-circuit-zero/game.js:56 — `driftPointer` is not associated with a pointer ID, so releasing the steering finger can cancel drift held by another finger.

MEDIUM/high kart-circuit-zero/game.js:41 — `resetRace()` leaves active pointer/key state intact, allowing controls held across restart to affect the new race.

Verdict: Needs follow-up; race finish/restart, viewport, and keyboard fallback otherwise pass.

### skyhammer

MEDIUM/high skyhammer/game.js:28 — `newGame()` does not clear shared particles, global effects, or active pointers, so restart can inherit stale visual/input state.

MEDIUM/medium skyhammer/game.js:96 — Up to 1,200 enemy bullets are retained and rebuilt into large arc paths every frame, creating a mobile performance risk.

Verdict: Needs follow-up; touch scheme, multi-touch focus/bomb, fail/win, viewport, and keyboard fallback pass.

### backstreet-reckoning

HIGH/high backstreet-reckoning/game.js:791 — The landscape rotate overlay is display-only; the brawler continues simulating and can take damage/advance while the overlay blocks play.

MEDIUM/high backstreet-reckoning/game.js:71 — `newGame()` does not clear `Input` pointer/button/stick state, allowing held controls to leak into a restarted run.

MEDIUM/medium backstreet-reckoning/core.js:117 — Punch/jump buttons are booleans rather than per-pointer state; releasing one of multiple same-role touches cancels the other.

Verdict: Needs follow-up; control coverage/hit sizes, fail/restart, viewport, and keyboard fallback otherwise pass; endless stages are accepted.

### vanguard-four

MEDIUM/high vanguard-four/game.js:199 — `newRun()` resets world state but not input state, so the redeploy gesture or held controls can affect the fresh run.

MEDIUM/medium vanguard-four/core.js:144 — Releasing any one simultaneous strike/super pointer clears the shared button state for all pointers.

LOW/high vanguard-four/game.js:34 — Mute control is only 26×18px, below the 48px hit-area minimum.

LOW/medium vanguard-four/game.js:497 — Delayed Warden attack callbacks can fire after `newRun()` and append old-run strikes to the new run.

Verdict: Needs follow-up; portrait fit, main controls, fail/redeploy, and keyboard fallback otherwise pass; endless rooms are accepted.

### siegebreak

MEDIUM/high siegebreak/core.js:148 — Touch input uses one global pointer and only `changedTouches[0]`; multi-touch overwrites coordinates and can misclassify/release swipes.

MEDIUM/high siegebreak/game.js:44 — `newGame()` does not clear `Input.down` or active touch/key state, so releasing the restart gesture can issue a command in the new run.

Verdict: Needs follow-up; hit areas, win/loss/restart, 390px fit, and keyboard fallback pass.

### shellshock-pinball

Verdict: Needs minor reset hardening; touch multi-flippers/plunger/nudge, hit sizes, game over/replay, viewport, performance, and keyboard fallback pass.
